# STORY BUILDING BLOCKS
# Ideas the program hands the narrator, a few at a time, chosen to fit the fight as it stands. They are
# suggestions to be reworded and woven in, never text to copy and never new events.
#
# HOW IT IS CHOSEN. Before each part of a beat the program looks at what is really happening (which parts were
# hit and how hard, how much each part hurts now, who is fresh and who is spent, who is pinned, what kind of move
# it was) and offers only blocks that fit. Among those it prefers blocks that have been offered least, and it
# will not offer the same block again for several beats. What has been offered is remembered between fights in
# blocks_memory.json (delete that file to start the count again).
#
# FORMAT. A line "== category | condition: values | condition: values" starts a group; every line under it is one
# block. A block can add conditions of its own after a "|" at its end. Lines starting with # are notes.
# Conditions (a block is offered only when every one of its conditions fits):
#   size:  glancing, solid, heavy, tremendous          how hard the blow landed
#   level: sore, hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated     how much that part hurts afterwards
#   zone:  head, neck, chest, belly, back, arm, leg, tail            where on the body
#   has:   tails, tail, fins, horn, ruff, coils, arms, forelegs, legs, ears, antennae, wings, beak, talons, hood
#   state: fresh, winded, tired, spent                 her overall condition
#   kind:  claw, bite, charge, tail, beam, punch, horn, throw, grab, wing, beak, talon      what kind of move it is
#   role:  attacker, receiver, pinner, pinned, winning, losing
#   fade:  fighting, weakening, fading, nearly out      how far gone a pinned fighter is
#   posture: standing, down                          whether she is on her feet
#   held:  early, middle, long                        how long a pin has been held
#   part:  jaw, nose, eye...                          a word of the body part's own name
#   effect: super, weak, none                         how the attack's type meets her body (effect_* blocks only)
#   type:  fire, water, electric, ice, ...            the attack's type        vs: her own type(s)
# In the text of a block, {part} is the body part in question ("left thigh") and {next} a part beside it.
# Add as many lines as you like: more blocks means more variety. /blocks shows the counts.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# REACTIONS: the moment of impact, sized to the blow
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== reaction | size: glancing
a blink and a small shake of the head
one ear flicks, nothing more | has: ears
a short breath out through the nose, annoyed
her eyes narrow and stay on her opponent
a twitch of the skin where it touched, as at a fly
she rolls the shoulder nearest it once and forgets it
a huff, half a laugh
her lip lifts off one tooth and settles again
the tails skip a turn and pick the rhythm up again | has: tails
a glance down at the spot, then back up
she shifts her feet a hand's width and is set again | has: legs
the tip of her tail flicks once | has: tail

== reaction | size: solid
a grunt forced out through shut teeth
a flinch and a bitten-off sound
a quick breath sucked in through the nose
her head jerks away from it before she can stop herself
a half-step back, caught, weight brought forward again | has: legs
her eyes water and she blinks them clear, angry at them
a wince that pulls one whole side of her face tight
a sharp little bark of surprise
her ears go flat and come slowly back up | has: ears
her jaw sets and a muscle jumps in her cheek
the breath she was taking stops halfway and has to start again
a hiss, long and thin, through her front teeth
her fins snap flat to her forearms | has: fins
the ruff along her neck lifts and stays up | has: ruff
her tails stutter out of time with each other | has: tails
her coils draw in a little tighter round themselves | has: coils
she touches the place with a paw, quickly, as if checking it is still there | has: arms
a shudder that starts at the spot and runs out to her tail-tip | has: tail
she swallows the sound and it shows in her throat
her antennae snap rigid for a heartbeat | has: antennae

== reaction | size: heavy
a yelp that cracks in the middle
the breath punched out of her in one bark, eyes watering
teeth clenched on a cry that gets out anyway
a flinch so hard her whole body curls round the spot
she staggers a full step and has to find the ground again | has: legs
her head snaps round with it and comes back slowly
a ragged gasp, mouth open, nothing coming in for a moment
eyes squeezed shut for a heartbeat, opened again wet
a snarl with pain underneath it, rising at the end
her knees dip and catch | has: legs
her whole body jerks away as if from something hot
a cough knocked out of her, then a second, smaller one
her ears pin flat and stay there | has: ears
she bends over the place with a forearm pressed to it | has: arms
her tails lash once, hard, and wrap round her own leg | has: tails
the sickle of her tail goes rigid as a hook | has: tail
her lips peel back from her fangs and the sound that comes out is not a growl
a squeak she is ashamed of the moment it is out
she sways, eyes unfocused, and blinks the world back into place
her fins flare wide and shake | has: fins
her humming note breaks into a hiss | has: coils
a groan dragged out long through her nose
she shakes her head hard, twice, as if to throw the hurt off
her claws scrape the ground for grip she did not need a moment ago
her breath comes back in pieces, three short pulls
tears start and she lets them, too busy to care
a sound between a whine and a snarl
her hind end drops a hand's width and locks | has: forelegs

== reaction | size: tremendous
mouth wide open in a silent scream, eyes squeezed shut
a shriek that breaks off into a wheeze
the head thrown back, a raw cry at the sky
every limb going rigid for a heartbeat, then a ragged gulp of breath
a keening whine through clenched teeth, tears starting
the back arching off the blow, a cry with no air behind it
a scream that cracks, drops, and comes back as a whimper
her eyes fly wide, white all round, and see nothing
the cry bursts out of her and goes on a beat longer than she would ever have allowed
a sound she has never made before, high and thin, and she hears it as if from outside
her jaw works with no sound at all, then the sound arrives all at once
she folds over the place with both arms and rocks | has: arms
her whole body bows round it like a pulled string
a howl with a hitch in the middle where the breath ran out
she bites her own lip to stop the noise and it comes out through her nose instead
her paws open and close on nothing | has: arms
her claws dig furrows in the ground without her knowing | has: forelegs
the tails go stiff and straight out behind her, quivering | has: tails
her tail-fan snaps shut like a hand | has: coils
her coils clench so hard the scales creak | has: coils
her fins clamp flat and tremble | has: fins
the ruff stands out in spikes and every hair shakes | has: ruff
a stunned half-second of silence, eyes huge, and then the scream
tears squeezed out at the corners of shut eyes and running into her fur
she gags on the breath she tries to take
her voice gives out halfway through the cry and the rest is air
her head drops and hangs between her shoulders, mouth open, stringing spit
a long shuddering wail that she cuts off by clamping her jaws
she cannot get the cry out; her chest heaves twice before any sound comes
her legs shake under her and hold | has: legs
the whole of her flinches at once, ears, eyes, tail, as if struck everywhere
a broken, breathy keen on every breath out for three breaths running
she curls her tail tight round her own hind leg and holds on to it | has: tail
her eyes roll to find her opponent and cannot hold on her
her teeth chatter once, hard, as the shock goes through
a bark of pain so sharp it echoes

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# SENSATIONS: what the pain in a part is like, by how bad it now is
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== sensation | level: sore
a dull bruise-ache in her {part} that she can ignore if she does not lean on it
her {part} tender to the touch, warm under the fur
a tightness in her {part}, like a muscle worked too hard yesterday
a low throb in her {part} that keeps time with her pulse
her {part} stings on the surface and is fine underneath
a nagging pull in her {part} when she turns
her {part} feels thick and slow for a breath, then answers again
an itch of heat in her {part} that fades when she moves
a small sharp complaint from her {part} each time she uses it, already quieter
her {part} aches the way a stubbed toe aches: loudly, and not for long
the skin over her {part} feels a size too small
her {part} remembers the blow in a slow, spreading warmth

== sensation | level: hurting
a hot, steady ache in her {part} that does not go away when she stops moving
her {part} pulses, a beat behind her heart
a deep bruise in her {part} that every movement leans on
her {part} sharp when she uses it and sullen when she does not
a band of soreness round her {part} that tightens as it swells
her {part} feels packed with something heavy and warm
a pull in her {part} like a thread stitched too tight
her {part} answers late and complains when it does
a grinding, toothache kind of hurt in her {part}
her {part} burns along the line of the blow and aches for a paw's width either side
she can feel the exact shape of the bruise in her {part} from the inside
her {part} is hot to her own touch
a sick little jolt from her {part} whenever weight goes near it
her {part} throbs harder when she holds still, as if it wanted attention

== sensation | level: very painful
her {part} blazes when she moves it and smoulders when she does not
a pounding in her {part} so strong she can hear it
every heartbeat lands in her {part} like a knuckle
her {part} feels swollen to twice its size and tight as a drum
a bright, sharp line through her {part} with a deep, sick ache behind it
her {part} cramps, lets go, and cramps again, each time a little worse
a hot wire drawn through her {part}, and the wire stays in
the hurt in her {part} comes in waves, and the gaps between them are getting shorter
her {part} shakes when she asks anything of it
a raw, scraped feeling in her {part}, as if the inside had been sanded
she has to think about her {part} before every movement, and thinking about it hurts
her {part} is one hard knot that will not loosen
a stab in her {part} on every breath in, and a long ache on every breath out
her {part} feels wrong-shaped under her, heavy, hot and not quite hers
the pain in her {part} has a taste, like metal at the back of her mouth
she catches herself holding her breath to keep her {part} still
a deep, wrenching ache in her {part} that makes her stomach turn
her {part} flares white whenever anything touches it, even air

== sensation | level: excruciating
her {part} stops being a thing that hurts and becomes the only thing there is
a white, howling pain in her {part} that leaves no room to think round it
her {part} screams when she moves and screams when she is still
the pain in her {part} climbs past anything she has a name for and keeps climbing
a tearing cramp through her {part} that doubles her over it
her {part} feels as if it were being wrung out like a cloth
every nerve in her {part} is lit at once, and the light does not dim
the hurt in her {part} is so large it seems to stand outside her, and she is inside it
she cannot tell where her {part} ends; the pain has no edges
a red-hot weight sits in her {part} and presses outward
her {part} jumps and shudders with a will of its own
she would bargain with her {part} if it would listen
the pain in her {part} comes up her throat as a sound before she knows she is making it
her {part} is fire to the touch and ice a finger's width under the skin
her sight goes grey at the edges each time her {part} is jarred
her whole body arranges itself round her {part}, bent, guarding, breath held
the thought of anything touching her {part} again makes her whimper before it happens
she can use her {part}, barely, and each time pays for it with a cry
a deep, sick, spreading clang in her {part}, like a bell struck inside her
her {part} throbs so hard her teeth ache with it

== sensation | level: devastated
her {part} barely answers her; when it does, the answer is a broken whimper
her {part} hangs and trembles, and every touch on it tears a cry out of her
there is nothing left in her {part} to fight with, only the hurt
her {part} has given everything it had and now it only shakes
the lightest brush on her {part} and her whole body flinches away, a thin sound forced out of her
her {part} feels like one bruise from skin to core, swollen, hot and useless for anything but pain
she keeps her {part} utterly still and it hurts as if she were shaking it
her {part} quivers like something that has run too far
moving her {part} is past deciding; her body simply refuses for her
the pain in her {part} has stopped coming in waves and become weather
a small, cracked sound escapes her every time her {part} so much as shifts
her {part} drags at her like a thing tied to her
she cradles her {part} without meaning to, and cannot bear the cradling either | has: arms
her {part} twitches and each twitch is its own separate agony
a hollow, shaking ache in her {part}, too big for the place it is in
she looks at her {part} as if it belonged to someone she felt sorry for

== sensation | zone: head | level: very painful, excruciating, devastated
her skull rings, and the ringing has a heartbeat in it
the pain sits behind her eyes and pushes
her sight doubles, slides together, and doubles again
the side of her head that took it feels a size bigger than the other
her own pulse is loud in her ears, louder than anything outside her
a hot, tight mask of hurt across her face from brow to jaw
every sound comes to her a moment late and too bright
her teeth ache at the roots all along one side | part: jaw, muzzle, cheek
her eyes will not stop streaming, and blinking only stings them | part: nose, muzzle, cheek, head
the ground seems to tilt toward the side that was hit

== sensation | zone: neck | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
swallowing is a thing she has to plan
each breath scrapes on its way down
her throat closes round nothing and she has to cough it open
the cords of her neck stand out and tremble
air comes through a space that feels no wider than a straw
her voice, when she tries it, is a husk
a hard ring of soreness round her throat that tightens when she lifts her chin
turning her head pulls a hot line from her ear to her shoulder
she breathes in sips because a full breath hurts going past
her throat clicks when she swallows, and the click hurts

== sensation | zone: chest | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
every breath in presses her ribs against the bruise from inside
she cannot fill her lungs; the pain stops her two-thirds of the way
a stitch under her ribs that catches on each breath like a hook
her chest feels banded with iron and the band is tightening
breathing out is easy and breathing in is a climb
her heart seems to beat against the sore place
a cough is waiting in her chest and she is afraid to let it out
her ribs creak when she twists, and she stops twisting
the air she gets is thin and high, never enough to reach the bottom
she breathes with her shoulders because her chest will not do it

== sensation | zone: belly | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
a hot, sick cramp low in her middle that makes her want to fold
her stomach turns over and she swallows hard against it
the muscles of her belly clench round the hurt and cannot let go
standing straight pulls at it; she stays a little bent
a deep, griping ache that spreads out toward her hips
her breath goes shallow so that her middle need not move
her belly feels hollowed out and the hollow aches
every step jolts it, a dull heavy swing of pain
she keeps a forearm across her middle as if to hold it in | has: arms

== sensation | zone: back | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
a deep bruised band across her back that locks when she bends
her spine feels strung too tight, each knob of it sore
twisting sends a hot bar of pain from hip to shoulder
she carries her back stiff as a board and moves round it
the big muscles either side of her spine jump and knot
lying on it would be unbearable; so is standing

== sensation | zone: arm | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her {part} will not close into a grip without shaking
lifting her {part} drags a hot line up into her shoulder
her {part} hangs heavier than the other, slow to rise
the joint above her {part} grinds, hot and loose
she can feel her pulse in her {part} all the way to the tip
weight on her {part} is out of the question; she takes it elsewhere | has: forelegs
her {part} trembles when she holds it out and she pulls it back in | has: arms
her fingers on that side are clumsy, slow to answer | has: arms

== sensation | zone: leg | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her {part} shakes when she puts weight on it
her {part} takes her weight and tells her about it at once
the muscle of her {part} jumps under the skin like something trapped
standing on her {part} is a decision she has to make again at every step
her {part} is a hot, dead ache that will not hold her for long
a thin bright pain runs up from her {part} like a wire pulled through the joint
her {part} gives a little at each step and she catches it each time
she stands with her {part} barely touching the ground
her {part} cramps hard when she bends it and throbs when she straightens it
the whole leg feels too long, the foot arriving late

== sensation | zone: tail | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her tail will not hold its usual line; it sags, and lifting it hurts at the root
a hot ache at the base of her tail that she feels in her spine
her balance is off without her tail doing its work
the tails turn unevenly, one lagging the other, each turn a pull | has: tails
she tucks her tail in close and it throbs against her leg

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TRAVEL: how a hurt spreads from the part that took it to the parts beside it
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== travel
it starts in her {part} and runs up into her {next} like a wire pulled tight
her {part} takes it, and her {next} answers a heartbeat later, duller
the shock goes through her {part} and comes out the other side into her {next}
the hurt in her {part} spreads into her {next} the way spilled water finds the low place
her {next} wrenches as it tries to take the load her {part} has just dropped
a jolt from her {part} lands in her {next} and stays there, smaller and meaner
her {part} goes hot and her {next} goes tight, as if the two were one muscle
the pain does not stay where it was put; it climbs from her {part} into her {next}
her {next} jars with the blow to her {part}, the way a whole wall shakes when one stone is struck
what begins as a point in her {part} opens out into an ache across her {next}
her {next} flinches before the hurt from her {part} has even reached it
the blow to her {part} drags her {next} round with it and both complain
a line of fire from her {part} to her {next}, drawn in one stroke
her {part} screams and her {next} echoes it, quieter, for longer
the ache in her {part} leans on her {next} until that aches too
she feels it in her {part} first, sharp, and then in her {next}, deep
her {next} goes weak in sympathy with her {part}
the force keeps travelling after it lands: {part}, then {next}, then gone

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# BREATH: how she is breathing, by her condition
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== breath | state: fresh
breathing through her nose, even, a little quicker than at rest
one deep breath, let out slowly, and she is ready
her sides move easily; she is not yet short of anything
a breath held for the length of the move and let go after
her breath comes and goes without her noticing it

== breath | state: winded
her mouth has come open; she breathes through it now
her sides heave, steady but fast
each breath out has a little sound in it
she steals two quick breaths between movements
her breath whistles faintly on the way in
she has to finish a breath before she can do anything else
her chest lifts her shoulders with each pull of air
a dry tongue, a dry mouth, breath rasping over both

== breath | state: tired
her breath saws, loud in the quiet
every breath out half-closes one of her eyes
she pants with her tongue showing between her teeth
there is a hitch at the top of each breath, as if it had caught on something
she breathes with her whole body, ribs, belly, shoulders
a long ragged pull in, a short broken push out
her breath comes in threes: two short, one long
spit strings from her open mouth and she does not wipe it
her nostrils flare wide and it is still not enough
she bows her head to breathe, as if air were heavier low down

== breath | state: spent
every breath out drags one eye shut, and it is slower each time to open
her breath shudders in and falls out
she breathes like something at the end of a long run that is not yet allowed to stop
each breath is a small groan she no longer bothers to hide
there are gaps between her breaths now, and she seems to forget and then remember
her sides flutter instead of heaving
air goes in with a wet catch and comes out as a sigh
she holds her mouth wide and still cannot get enough
her head nods with each breath, a little lower every time

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TELLS: what an opponent can SEE of her, hurt or healthy (offered only when it is true)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== tell | state: fresh | posture: standing
she moves easily, weight even, nothing held back
her eyes are clear and quick and miss nothing
her breathing is quiet; she could do this all day
there is spring in her still: she comes up onto her toes between movements | has: legs
her guard is loose and confident, not yet protecting anything
she looks almost bored, which is its own kind of threat

== tell | state: winded
her mouth is open now and her sides are working
she is a shade slower to turn than she was
she has started choosing her moments instead of taking every one
there is a sheen of damp in her fur along the ribs
she takes a breath before she moves, where before she simply moved

== tell | state: tired
her head hangs lower between movements than it did
every breath out makes one of her eyes begin to close
she stands a little too long after each exchange, gathering | posture: standing
her guard has dropped a hand's width and she has not noticed
she blinks slowly, as if each blink might turn into something longer
her feet drag a little on the turn | has: legs | posture: standing
she has stopped wasting movement; there is none to waste
she watches the ground between them as much as she watches her opponent

== tell | state: spent
she sways where she stands and corrects it late | posture: standing
her eyes close on every breath out and come open again by effort
her legs are spread wide to keep her up | has: legs | posture: standing
she is breathing so hard her whole body rocks with it
her head has sunk almost to the ground between her shoulders | has: forelegs
there is a tremor in her that does not stop
she looks at her opponent as if from a long way off
her coils lie slack and she has to gather them to move | has: coils

== tell | zone: arm | level: hurting, very painful
she carries her {part} a little high and a little close
she shakes her {part} out when she thinks nobody is looking
her {part} is slower than the other to come up
she guards with the other side and leaves her {part} out of it

== tell | zone: arm | level: excruciating, devastated
she keeps her {part} tucked tight against her side and does everything with the other
her {part} hangs, and she turns her body so that it is furthest from the fight
she flinches when her own {part} brushes her ribs
her {part} trembles all the time now, a fine fast shake
she holds her {part} with her other paw, wrist in palm, as if it might fall | has: arms
she will not put her {part} to the ground; she stands on three | has: forelegs | posture: standing

== tell | zone: leg | level: hurting, very painful | posture: standing
a slight lean off her {part}, the weight kept on the other side
she tests her {part} before she trusts it, a quick dab at the ground
her {part} is a beat late on every step
she turns the long way round rather than pivot on her {part}

== tell | zone: leg | level: excruciating, devastated | posture: standing
she stands with her {part} barely touching the ground
her whole body tilts away from her {part}; she is half a fighter on that side
her {part} shakes visibly when it takes weight, and she takes it off again at once
she hops rather than steps when she has to move fast
she keeps whatever is solid in this place on the side of her {part}, something to lean on
her {part} drags a mark along the ground

== tell | zone: chest, belly | level: hurting, very painful
a forearm drifts across her {part} between exchanges and stays there | has: arms
she stands a little bent, protecting her {part} | posture: standing
she breathes high and short, sparing her {part}
she flinches from anything aimed near her {part}, even a feint

== tell | zone: chest, belly | level: excruciating, devastated
she is clutching her {part} openly now, hunched round it
she cannot stand straight; her {part} folds her | posture: standing
each breath shows on her face as it passes her {part}
she turns side-on to hide her {part} and it only shows where it is | posture: standing
her elbows are clamped in over her {part} and her guard is gone because of it | has: arms
she moves as if carrying something breakable in her {part}

== tell | zone: head | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
she blinks too often and shakes her head as if to clear water from it
one eye is swollen half shut | part: eye, cheek, head
she is carrying her head tilted, the hurt side turned away
her gaze slides off and has to be dragged back
she winces at loud sounds, even her own
her jaw hangs a little open and trembles at the hinge | part: jaw
her nose runs and she breathes through her mouth | part: nose, muzzle

== tell | zone: neck | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
she swallows often, and each swallow is a visible effort
her chin is tucked low, guarding her {part}
her breath whistles; anyone could hear it
she turns her whole body rather than her head
her voice has gone to a rasp

== tell | zone: back | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
she moves stiffly, all in one piece, as if her back were a plank | posture: standing
she will not bend; she lowers herself with her legs instead | posture: standing
her back is arched a little, held away from its own hurt
she twists to keep her {part} out of reach and the twist costs her

== tell | zone: tail | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her tail hangs out of true, and her balance with it
the tails turn slowly and unevenly, one dragging | has: tails
she keeps her tail tucked in, out of the way, and misses it on the turns

== tell | posture: down, pinned
she lies with the hurt side up, where the ground cannot press on it
she has curled round the worst of it, knees drawn in | has: legs | posture: down
she is propped on one elbow and the elbow is shaking | has: arms
her eyes follow every step toward her, and she does not look away | posture: down
she has got one paw under her and has not yet decided to use it
her sides pump; from above, each breath lifts her whole body off the ground
she watches her opponent's feet, because they are what she can see | posture: down
her tails are still, for once, laid out behind her | has: tails
she is gathering her legs under her a little at a time, hoping not to be noticed | has: legs

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MOVEMENT: ways to stage an attack, by the kind of move
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== movement | kind: claw
a feint high with one paw and the real stroke low with the other
she comes in at an angle so that the stroke crosses the body instead of meeting it
the claws go in shallow and drag, a rake more than a stab
a backhand swipe on the turn, weight following through
she lets the first swipe miss on purpose to open the line for the second
the stroke starts from the hip, the whole body uncoiling behind the paw
claws spread wide to cover more of her, or drawn together to bite deeper in one place
she steps past as she strikes, so that the cut is made in passing
a downward chop with the weight of the shoulder on it
she waits for the breath out, and strikes as the ribs fall

== movement | kind: bite
a snap that is meant to be seen, and the real bite a half-beat after
she turns her head sideways to get her jaws round it
she takes hold, shakes once, and lets go before she can be caught
teeth closing slowly, deliberately, pressure building
a lunge from below, coming up under the guard
she bites and uses the hold to haul her opponent off balance
the bite goes for loose skin first, then shifts deeper
jaws wide, breath hot on the fur, a moment of stillness before they shut
she catches a mouthful in passing and wrenches as she goes by
she worries at it, head jerking side to side, two short pulls

== movement | kind: charge
three strides to build it, and the fourth leaves the ground
she drops her shoulder at the last instant so that the point of it lands first
she comes in low, under the centre of weight, to lift as well as hit
a sidestep one way and the rush from the other
she kicks off whatever is solid behind her for the extra speed
she runs straight through the place where her opponent is standing
the charge curves, cutting off the way out before it lands
head down, eyes up, the ground blurring
she does not slow for the impact; she speeds up into it
her feet tear at the ground and the sound arrives before she does
whatever she is wet with peels off her in a sheet as she comes
she gathers on her haunches, still as a drawn bow, and lets go | has: forelegs

== movement | kind: tail
a full turn of the body, the tail arriving last and hardest
the tail comes round low, at the legs, a sweep rather than a blow
she looks one way and the tail comes from the other
a short hard flick from close in, all in the wrist of the tail
the tails together, one a blink behind the other, a double slap | has: tails
she plants her forefeet and swings her whole hind end round them | has: forelegs
the tail snaps out like a whip and the crack comes a moment after
she coils the tail back along her side and releases it like a spring | has: coils

== movement | kind: beam
she plants herself first; this is not a move to throw on the run
the breath before it, deep, chest swelling, and then the release
it leaves her in a tight line and opens as it travels
she sweeps the beam across her target from one part to the next instead of holding it on one spot
she leans into it, shoulders bunched, as if pushing something heavy
the light changes for as long as it lasts
it starts thin and she feeds it, thicker, harder, with each breath
her feet slide back a finger's width from the force and she digs in
she sweeps it side to side so there is no stepping out of it
the air between them shivers before it arrives
she fires from the hip, quick and ugly, no time to aim properly
she closes one eye to sight along it

== movement | kind: punch
short, from the chest, no wind-up to give it away
the paw turned edge-on, webbing folded tight, a chop rather than a punch | has: fins
two fast ones to make her cover up and a slow hard one after
she steps in so close there is no room to swing, and hits anyway
it comes up from below, under the chin's line of sight
the weight goes from back foot to front as the paw lands | has: legs
she hits through the target, aiming a hand's width behind it
a hook round the outside of the guard, into the side

== movement | kind: horn
she lowers her head and turns it flat, the edge of the horn level with the target
a hooking sweep from outside to in, drawn across rather than driven in
the horn comes up from below, lifting as it cuts
she tosses her head at the last moment, a flick that adds the snap
the dark gathers along the curve of the horn until its edge looks wet
she feints with the horn and follows the eyes that follow it

== movement | kind: throw
she gets under the centre of weight and straightens her legs
a turn from the hips, the other body swinging out wide on the end of her grip
she takes hold of the scruff, sets her feet, and heaves
she uses the other's own rush, steps aside, and helps it on its way
she lifts, holds for a heartbeat at the top, and lets gravity do the rest
a twist at the moment of release, so the body leaves spinning
she drags her opponent off balance first, and only then throws
she throws low and flat, to skip her across the ground like a stone
she picks the spot before she lets go: the hardest thing in reach

== movement | kind: grab
she catches a limb in passing and does not let it go by
the grip closes above the joint, where it cannot be shaken off
she hauls the other in close, closer than striking distance, chest to chest
her tails wind twice round and cinch like a rope on a post | has: tails
her jaws take hold in the loose skin, not to bite, only to keep | has: forelegs
her coils slide round, unhurried, and are suddenly tight | has: coils
she traps the arm against her own body so that pulling only pulls them both
she gets a grip and then improves it, shifting hold by hold

== movement
she circles to put the light in her opponent's eyes
she gives ground on purpose, drawing the other onto worse footing
she changes level suddenly, dropping low or rising up
a pause, a long look, and then everything at once
she comes from the side the other is guarding least
she times it to the other's breath, moving as she breathes in
she lets her foot slip on the bad footing and uses the slip
she makes the same move she made before, to see if it is expected, and changes it late
she crowds, taking away the room to swing
she backs off just far enough to be chased

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THOUGHTS: angles for an inner voice (brief, in her own voice, never a prediction of the ending)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== thought | role: attacker
a quick plan in three short steps
which injury she means to go back to, and why | stage: middle, late, end
what she noticed about the other's footing a moment ago
a dry remark about her own bad habit
a note to herself to stop admiring her work and move
what the other is protecting, and what that leaves open
a wager with herself about how the other will react
counting what she has left: breath, legs, one good trick | stage: middle, late, end
the thing she does not want the other to work out
a flicker of something like regret, dismissed
what she would do in the other's place, and how to punish it
whether anything she has thrown so far today has really landed, and what that tells her | stage: middle, late, end
a decision to stop being careful
admiration for the other's nerve, kept to herself

== thought | role: receiver
an inventory, part by part, of what still works
a furious little correction of her own mistake
a joke, because the alternative is worse
the refusal to make another sound like that one
what she will do to pay this back, in specific terms
surprise at how much it hurt, honestly noted
the plan she had a moment ago and whether any of it survives
a single word, repeated, to get herself through the next breath
what in this place she could put between them, or use
that the other is hurt too, and where | stage: middle, late, end
a promise to herself with a condition on it
something she was taught once and only now understands
the absurdity of where she is and what she is doing
that she is not done, said plainly

== thought | role: pinner
that holding still is harder than hitting
a count of her own hurts and which one will give first | has: hurts
what the body under her is about to try, read through her paws
a reluctance she does not examine
impatience, and the knowledge that impatience is how pins are lost
respect for how long the other is lasting | held: middle, long
a wish that the other would simply stop
what she will do differently if this one gets out

== thought | role: pinned
a list of what she can still move, shorter than she would like
the next try planned in detail, down to which breath | fade: fighting, weakening
a bitter joke about the weight on her
the one place the hold is weakest, and how to reach it | fade: fighting, weakening
that she must not waste the air she has
anger, clean and useful, pushing the fear down | fade: fighting, weakening
a refusal, word by word
that it is getting harder to keep her eyes open, and she must | fade: fading, nearly out
something small and ordinary she suddenly wants very much | fade: fading, nearly out

== thought | role: winning
a warning to herself not to get careless now
that this is taking longer than it should | stage: middle, late, end
a moment of something like pity, set aside
what the other still has that could turn it

== thought | role: losing
that there is one chance left and what it is
stubbornness, pure, with no plan attached
a cold, clear look at how bad it is
that she has been here before and got out | stage: middle, late, end

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EMOTION: what she feels, shown through the body rather than named
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== emotion | role: attacker
satisfaction that shows only as a slow breath out | standing: winning, level
a hard, bright eagerness, weight coming forward onto her toes
contempt, in the way she turns half away before the other has recovered | standing: winning
care, almost fussy, in how she places each paw
anger at being made to work this hard, in the set of her ears | stage: middle, late, end
a grim patience, shoulders low, in no hurry at all
something close to enjoyment, quickly hidden | standing: winning, level
wariness: she strikes and is already out of reach again
a flicker of doubt at how much the other has left, pushed down at once | standing: level, losing
cold focus on one spot, the rest of the fight gone quiet around it
pride she won't let reach her face | standing: winning
relief that the blow worked, close behind it the worry that it won't next time | standing: level, losing
impatience: she wants this over, and it shows in how soon she moves again | stage: late, end
a hunter's stillness, nothing wasted between one move and the next
a sting of guilt at the sound the other makes, gone before it can slow her | standing: winning

== emotion | role: receiver
humiliation, hot behind her eyes, worse than the hurt
fury that makes her shake harder than the pain does
fear, brief and honest, in the white round her eyes
stubbornness in the way she sets her feet again where she stood
disbelief; she looks at the place as if it had betrayed her
a wild, cornered brightness, teeth showing
shame at the sound she made, ears flat with it
a strange calm, everything gone very clear and slow
defiance, chin up, when everything else is down
grief for a moment, small and private, for how well she was doing | stage: middle, late, end

== emotion | role: pinned
panic, pushed down hard, coming back up in her breath
rage with nowhere to go, shaking in her limbs | fade: fighting, weakening
a cold, narrow focus on one thing at a time | fade: fighting, weakening
dread, in how carefully she is rationing her air
pride that keeps her eyes open and on her opponent's face | facing: up
tiredness so deep it feels like being talked to kindly | fade: fading, nearly out

== emotion | role: pinner
strain that she will not let show on her face
unease at how small the body under her feels
a hard, flat determination, jaw set
relief she does not trust yet

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CARRY: how an injury she already has shows while she acts
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== carry | zone: arm
she strikes with the other side because her {part} will not do it | role: attacker
her {part} is slow to lift and the blow starts late | role: attacker
she uses her {part} anyway and hisses as she does
she keeps her {part} tucked and fights one-sided

== carry | zone: leg
she pushes off the good leg; her {part} only follows
her turn is wide because her {part} will not pivot
she lands and her {part} gives a little, and she covers it with the next move
every stride on her {part} is a short stab she breathes through

== carry | zone: chest, belly
she strikes on the breath out, because breathing in hurts too much to time anything to | role: attacker
the twist that powers the blow pulls at her {part} and she grunts | role: attacker
she keeps one side turned away to spare her {part}
she cannot bend to it properly; her {part} will not fold

== carry | zone: head
she squints through the eye that still works and judges the distance twice | part: eye, cheek, head
opening her mouth costs her, and she opens it anyway | part: jaw, muzzle
her head swims as she lowers it and she waits a breath for the world to settle | part: head, ear, horn
she breathes through her mouth because her nose will not do it | part: nose, muzzle
her face is one tight ache and every expression costs her
she shakes her head once to clear it and regrets it

== carry | zone: neck
she breathes in short pulls through her nose, sparing her throat
she cannot roar; what comes out is a rasp
she keeps her chin down and looks up from under her brow

== carry | zone: back
she moves in one stiff piece, turning from the feet
bending to strike low pulls a line of fire up her spine | role: attacker

== carry | zone: tail
her balance is off on the turn without her tail, and she plants a paw to catch it
the tails will not spin up to speed; one drags | has: tails

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TAKEDOWNS: ways one fighter puts another on the ground before a pin (it does no damage of its own)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== takedown
a shoulder into the middle, low and pushing, while a paw hooks the knee out from behind
she catches the scruff, sets her feet, and hauls the other down and round
a sweep of the tail takes both feet at once
she ducks under the arms, lifts, and tips the other over her own back
she catches one leg and simply holds it up until balance gives out
she drives in chest to chest and walks the other backward until something trips her
she takes hold of an arm and turns, and the body has to follow the arm down
she rears, comes down with both forepaws on the shoulders, and rides her to the ground | has: forelegs
she winds her tails round an ankle and pulls, one sharp jerk | has: tails
her coils loop the legs and draw tight, and the standing is over | has: coils
she waits for the bad leg to take weight and takes it away
she bulls in from the side, hip to hip, and tips her over the hurt side
she picks the other up bodily, a grunt and a heave, and puts her down on her back
she catches the tail, pulls her off her line, and is on her before she recovers
a trip, almost gentle, a paw placed behind the heel at the right moment
she feints high so the guard comes up, and takes the legs
she lets the other come at her, steps aside, and helps her on down with a paw between the shoulders
she drags her down by the wrist, turning it until she has to kneel | has: forelegs
she uses the ground itself: one push where the footing is worst
she clamps her jaws on a forelimb and sits back, all her weight on it
she leans, heavier and heavier, until the legs under the other fold
she hooks the horn behind a knee and lifts | has: horn

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PINS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== pin_hold
she locks her elbows so that they will not start to shake
she shifts her weight a hair toward whichever limb is trying something
she feels the other's breathing through her own chest
her own injuries pay for every moment of this | has: hurts
she keeps her claws out of the fur; this is holding, not cutting
she watches the eyes below her for the moment before a try
the effort is all in staying still, and staying still is the hardest thing she has done today
a drop of sweat or spit falls from her onto the face below | facing: up
she can feel a pulse under her paw, fast, faster than her own
she wants to look away and does not
she adjusts one contact at a time, never two at once
her legs have begun to tremble with the strain and she spreads them wider

== pin_under | fade: fighting
she tests each limb in turn, quickly, looking for the loose one
her breath is short but there is plenty of fight in it
she glares up, memorising the face | facing: up
every muscle is coiled; she is choosing the moment
the ground under her is hard and cold and she uses it, digging in a heel

== pin_under | fade: weakening
her tries are slower now and further apart
her breath comes in sips, each one a little effort
she has stopped glaring and started thinking
the weight seems to have grown since it settled
her limbs answer, but they take their time

== pin_under | fade: fading
the edges of her sight are greying in
sounds come from further away than they should
her limbs are heavy, as if the ground were holding them too
she loses a moment, finds it again, and does not know how long it was
she is fighting to stay awake more than to get out
her eyes slide shut and she drags them open

== pin_under | fade: nearly out
her eyes close and it takes all she has to open them again
she can hear her own breath, a thread going in and a thread going out
her paws have opened without her telling them to
the world has shrunk to the weight on her and the cold under her
a thought starts and comes apart before it is finished
she is not sure any more which way is up

# A pin HURTS, both of them, and more as it goes on. pin_pain: what a press feels like to the pinned fighter, by how
# far gone she is. pin_strain: what holding it costs the pinner, by how long she has held (held: early, middle, long).
== pin_pain | fade: fighting
the press on her {part} finds the bruise that was already there and leans on it
her {part} is ground against the hard ground with every breath either of them takes
a hot, tight circle of hurt where the contact sits on her {part}
her {part} aches under the weight in a plain, hard way she can still think round
the edge of the contact digs into her {part} and she cannot shift off it
each small movement above her lands in her {part} as a fresh jolt
her {part} is squeezed between weight and ground like a paw in a door
the pain in her {part} is sharp when she fights the hold and dull when she rests, and she will not rest

== pin_pain | fade: weakening
the ache in her {part} has stopped being pressure and become pain, deep and steady and growing
her {part} throbs against the contact, each throb a little worse than the last
the hurt in her {part} has spread; it is the whole limb now, the whole side
she can feel her own pulse beating against whatever is pressing her {part}
every try she makes is paid for in her {part} before it has moved a hair
her {part} has begun to shake under the load, and the shaking hurts
a grinding, wringing pain in her {part} that climbs each time the weight shifts
she cries out when the press on her {part} is renewed, and cannot stop the cry

== pin_pain | fade: fading
the pain in her {part} has become the loudest thing in the world
her {part} is one long scream that she hears from inside
there is no easing off it; the hurt in her {part} only climbs
her {part} cramps under the press and cannot uncramp, and the cramp is agony
each breath she fights for comes back out as a whimper from her {part}
the hurt in her {part} rolls up through her in waves that leave her gasping
tears run back into her ears from what is being done to her {part}
her whole body is trying to get away from her own {part}

== pin_pain | fade: nearly out
the pain in her {part} is all there is left of her; everything else has gone grey
her {part} hurts so much that the hurt itself seems to be carrying her off
she has no cry left for her {part}, only a thin sound on every breath out
the agony in her {part} swells until there is no room beside it for staying awake
her {part} blazes once more, white, and the white spreads
the last clear thing she knows is the pain in her {part}

== pin_strain | held: early
her own hurts object at once to the position: she has to hold them still too | has: hurts
the first effort of clamping down sends a jolt through whatever of hers is already sore | has: hurts
she finds the one way to brace that spares her worst injury, and it spares nothing else | has: hurts
her breath is loud in her own ears as she settles her weight

== pin_strain | held: middle
her limbs have begun to burn with holding still
the ache of her own injuries has settled in and is getting comfortable | has: hurts
a tremor starts in the leg that is doing most of the work and she wills it quiet
sweat or spit runs into her eye and she cannot spare a paw to wipe it
every try from below jars her own hurts as much as it jars the pin | has: hurts
her jaw aches from being clenched

== pin_strain | held: long
her muscles are shaking openly now; holding on has become its own pain
her own worst injury is screaming at her to move, and she cannot move
she is breathing as hard as the one beneath her
it has gone on so long that her grip feels welded and her joints feel old
she hurts everywhere she is braced, a deep hot ache in every locked limb
she wonders, for one breath, which of them will give out first

== struggle
she bridges on shoulders and heels, trying to make a gap
she flattens, goes loose, and then explodes sideways
she gets a knee in between them and shoves
she twists to bring her teeth to bear on anything in reach
she walks her feet up the other's flank, looking for purchase
she works one arm free a little at a time, pretending to do something else
she rocks, small at first, building a swing
she claws at the ground above her head and pulls

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GETTING UP, DODGING, LANDING
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== getup
she rolls to her front first and gets her knees under her | has: legs
she hauls herself up something solid, paw over paw | has: arms
she rises in stages: elbows, knees, one foot, the other | has: legs
she gets halfway, the world tilts, and she waits it out on one knee | has: legs
she pushes up on her forelegs, hind end still down, and gathers | has: forelegs
she stands too fast, staggers, and plants her feet wide | has: legs
she keeps the bad limb off the ground and makes the others do the work
she is up before she has decided to be, on stubbornness alone
her legs shake under her like a newborn's and she locks them | has: legs
she gets up angry, which helps
she uncoils upward in one long push and sways at the top | has: coils
she spits, shakes her head, and is on her feet

== dodge
she is simply not there when it arrives
a lean, just enough, the blow passing a whisker from her fur
she drops under it and feels the wind of it along her back
she turns side-on and it goes by her chest
a hop back, light, landing ready
she lets it come and steps round it at the last instant
she reads it in the shoulders before it starts
it clips a few hairs and nothing else
she sways out of its line and back in, already answering
she gives ground one fast step and the reach falls short
she ducks her head and it parts the air where her ear was | has: ears
the dodge costs her: a wrench in whatever already hurt

== landing
she hits flat and the ground knocks the breath out of both ends of her
she lands badly, one shoulder first, and rolls with it
the ground meets her all along one side at once
she skids, fur scraping, and fetches up with the taste of the ground in her mouth
she bounces once, and the second landing is worse
she tucks at the last moment and takes it across the back
the ground comes up faster than she can turn
whatever she hit gives her back to herself in pieces
she lands sitting, jarred from tail to skull, and tips over

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AFTERMATH AND OPENING
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== aftermath_winner
the fight leaves her in pieces: breath first, then the shaking, then the hurts one by one
she finds she is still braced for something that is not coming
an injury she had not noticed begins to speak up
she thinks about water, about shade, about lying down, and does none of it yet
she tries a hurt part carefully and learns what it will and will not do now
she looks at the other for a long time
she replays one moment of the fight, the one that nearly went the other way
she starts to tidy her fur and stops; it hurts too much to bend
the quiet is very loud after all that
she lets herself be still at last, and does not take her eyes off the other
she shivers as she cools
pride comes late and smaller than she expected

== aftermath_fallen
her breathing is slow and even, the only thing about her that moves
a paw twitches as if she were still fighting somewhere
the air stirs her fur and she does not feel it
the marks of the fight are all there to read on her
her face has gone smooth, younger, with the fight gone out of it
one ear flicks at a sound and is still again | has: ears
her tails lie side by side, the first time all day they have been still | has: tails
she lies as she fell, and the light moves slowly over her

== opening
the place has its own sounds, and they go on as if nothing were about to happen
each of them looks first at the ground, and only then at the other
one of them tests the footing with a paw before trusting it
a long look that takes in size, reach, and what this place offers
the smell of the place, named exactly, and what it tells each of them
neither wants to be the first to look away
one of them makes herself comfortable, pointedly
small habits show: a tail turning, an ear flicking, claws flexing
the light picks out one detail of each of them and leaves the rest in shadow
the distance between them is exactly as far as neither can cross in one move

# ===============================================================================================================
# MORE OF EVERYTHING (added in the second pass; the groups above set the pattern)
# ===============================================================================================================
== reaction | size: solid
she clicks her teeth shut on whatever she was about to say
a snort, half pain, half insult
her shoulders hunch for a heartbeat and she makes them drop
one eye shuts and the other stays hard on her opponent
she sucks air through her teeth and holds it
a grimace that shows the points of her teeth
her weight rocks back and forward again like a pushed door
her nostrils flare and whiten at the edges
she rubs the place against her own shoulder, quick and rough
a low "hnh" from the chest, more surprise than hurt
the fur along her spine lifts in a line
her cheek marks pull tight as she grits her teeth | has: fins

== reaction | size: heavy
her mouth opens on a cry and she bites it in half
she doubles, catches herself on a forelimb, and straightens by force
the cry comes out through her nose, a high pinched whine
her eyes screw shut and tears jump out at the corners
a full-body shudder, tail to ears, that she has to stand through
she spits, breathes, and the breath shakes
she turns the hurt side away as if she could hide it
a grunt that climbs into a yelp despite her
her claws flex and bite the ground and that is all that keeps her where she is
she reels a step sideways, crosses her own feet, and uncrosses them | has: legs
her head drops and comes up slower than it went down
a wheeze where the cry should have been; the air was already gone
her whole face clenches round shut eyes
the sound she makes is small and furious
her chitter breaks into a bark of pain | has: fins
a sharp hiss, lips pulled back, ears flat | has: ears

== reaction | size: tremendous
her scream starts high and climbs until her voice cannot follow it
mouth stretched wide, eyes clenched, the cords of her throat standing out, and for a long moment no sound
a cry so raw it leaves her throat sore
she writhes, the hurt part held still and everything else twisting round it
her eyes roll up for an instant and come back, glassy and wet
both paws fly to the place and hover, afraid to touch it | has: arms
a cry tears out of her once, loud, and she bites down on the second
her whole frame locks, shaking, like a bowstring at full draw
a shriek that echoes back at her and makes her flinch again
tears pour; she is not crying, her eyes simply will not stop
her jaws snap at the air in sheer reflex
she drags in a breath with a sound like tearing cloth and screams it back out
a strangled, climbing whimper that ends in a cough
her spine arches so hard her head nearly touches her own back
she pants in short high cries, one on every breath
her tails knot round each other and pull | has: tails
her horn scrapes the ground as her head goes down | has: horn
her long body thrashes once from head to tail-fan and lies shuddering | has: coils
a howl that drops into a moan and stays there
her face goes blank with it, wide-eyed and emptied, before the pain floods back in
drool and tears together, and she cannot spare the attention to be ashamed
the scream comes in pieces, each one cut off by the next spasm
she makes a small, lost sound and hates herself for it before it has finished

== sensation | level: sore
her {part} has a warm, bruised heaviness that she notices only when she thinks about it
a faint buzz in her {part}, like a limb that has been slept on
her {part} twinges when touched and is quiet otherwise
the blow has left a tender place on her {part} the size of a paw-print

== sensation | level: hurting
her {part} feels tight and overfull, the skin stretched
a bruise is coming up in her {part}; she can feel it thickening
her {part} smarts in the open air
a slow, insistent ache in her {part} that has settled in to stay
her {part} nags at her like a burr worked into her fur
something in her {part} pulls each time she breathes deep

== sensation | level: very painful
her {part} burns as if it had been held to something hot
a deep, pulsing soreness in her {part} that spikes when she least expects it
her {part} has a sharp centre and a wide sore edge, and both are growing
the ache in her {part} gnaws; it has teeth
she can feel her {part} swelling, pressing outward against its own skin
her {part} jolts her with every step, a hot stab she cannot learn to expect
the pain in her {part} is loud enough that she misses things because of it
her {part} seizes when she tries to use it and lets go only slowly

== sensation | level: excruciating
her {part} is a furnace; the heat of it seems to come off her in waves
the agony in her {part} rises and breaks and rises again, each crest higher
she feels her {part} in her teeth, behind her eyes, in the pit of her stomach
her {part} spasms, and each spasm is a fresh scream held behind her teeth
every nerve from her {part} outward is shrieking the same single note
the pain in her {part} empties her head of everything else, even fear
her {part} feels flayed, raw to the air
she cannot keep her {part} still and she cannot bear to move it
her {part} is too much; her mind keeps sliding off it and being dragged back

== sensation | level: devastated
her {part} is ruined for this fight and tells her so with every heartbeat
the faintest shift of weight and her {part} sends her a pain that whites out her sight
her {part} feels pulped, hot and boneless and unbearably tender
she whimpers on every breath because her {part} hurts on every breath
there is no position for her {part} that hurts less; she has tried them all
her {part} throbs so violently she can see it move
the agony in her {part} has worn a groove in her; she cannot remember being without it
her {part} flinches from the air, from her own fur, from being looked at

== sensation | zone: head | level: sore, hurting
a dull knock behind her eyes that fades as she blinks
her ears ring faintly and then stop
the side of her face feels hot and puffy
a headache beginning, small, at the place it landed

== sensation | zone: neck | level: sore
her throat feels scraped; she clears it and it still does
a stiffness in her neck when she turns to look

== sensation | zone: chest | level: sore
her ribs twinge when she breathes deep, and she breathes a little shallower
a flat ache across her chest like the day after a long swim

== sensation | zone: belly | level: sore
her middle is tender, and she tightens it without thinking
a small queasy flutter where it landed, gone in a breath

== sensation | zone: arm | level: sore
her {part} tingles to the tip and settles
her grip on that side is a little weaker, for now | has: arms

== sensation | zone: leg | level: sore
her {part} feels heavy for a stride or two and then forgets
a stiffness in her {part} when she pushes off

== travel
the blow lands on her {part} and her {next} feels it as a slow, spreading bruise
her {part} takes the edge of it and her {next} takes the weight
pain forks from her {part}: one branch stays, the other runs into her {next}
her {next} locks up to protect her {part} and hurts for its trouble
it rings through her {part} into her {next} like a struck pipe
her {part} flares; a breath later her {next} begins to throb in time with it

== breath | zone: chest
she breathes in halves, stopping each breath where the hurt begins
a catch at the top of every breath, where her ribs object
she breathes out in a long careful stream, as if pouring something
her breathing has gone high and quick, all in the top of her chest

== breath | zone: neck
her breath whistles in her throat
she swallows between breaths, each time with a wince
air rasps going in and wheezes coming out
she coughs, short and dry, and the cough hurts

== breath | zone: belly
she breathes with her chest only, keeping her middle still
each breath out ends in a small grunt she does not seem to hear
she pants shallowly, afraid of the deep breath

== tell | state: winded
her tongue shows between her teeth
she takes one more breath than she needs before each move
a fine tremble in her legs when she stands still | has: legs | posture: standing
her tails turn a little slower than they did | has: tails

== tell | state: tired
her answers come a half-beat late now
she misjudges a distance and has to correct
her head bobs with each breath
her fur is dark with sweat or water along the spine
she lets openings go by that she would have taken earlier

== tell | state: spent
her mouth hangs open and she no longer tries to close it
she stares, and it takes her a moment to see what she is staring at
her whole body shakes between movements
she has stopped guarding anything; she has nothing to guard with
every breath out drags one eyelid down, and each time it rises a little less

== tell | zone: arm | level: hurting, very painful
she flexes her {part} between exchanges, checking it
her {part} drops out of her guard whenever she forgets to hold it there

== tell | zone: leg | level: hurting, very painful
she shifts her weight off her {part} the moment she stops moving
a short stride on the side of her {part}, a long one on the other

== tell | zone: chest, belly | level: hurting, very painful
she winces when she twists
her breathing changes whenever something comes near her {part}

== tell | zone: chest, belly | level: excruciating, devastated
she breathes in tiny careful sips so as not to move her {part}
a sound escapes her every time her {part} is jarred, even by her own step

== tell | zone: head | level: excruciating, devastated
her head hangs to one side as if it were too heavy to carry level
she cannot keep both eyes open at once
she flinches from light, from sound, from her opponent's shadow
spit hangs from her open mouth; she has stopped noticing

== movement | kind: claw
a short chopping stroke from close in, no room for a swing
she draws the claws across slowly, pressing, not slashing
both forepaws, one after the other, left and right like a door swinging | has: forelegs
the stroke goes up, from low to high, opening her from hip to shoulder
she hooks instead of slashing, and pulls

== movement | kind: bite
she bites down and leans her weight on the jaw
a nip to make her flinch one way, and the true bite where she flinches to
her teeth find the old wound and close on it deliberately

== movement | kind: charge
she bounces once off the balls of her feet and goes | has: legs
a low skimming rush, belly almost on the ground
she turns the shoulder in at the last instant and hits with the whole side of her body
she comes in skimming, low and flat, like a thrown stone | has: tails
she winds up through her whole length and strikes like a released spring | has: coils

== movement | kind: beam
she lets it build behind her teeth until it hurts to hold
a short burst first, to find the range, and then the long one
she tracks her target with it, steady as a pointing paw
it takes everything out of her lungs and she lets it

== movement | kind: punch
a jab to the face to bring the paws up, and the real one under the ribs | has: arms
she turns her hip into it and the paw is only the end of the movement | has: arms
quick, light, and three of them before the other can count one | has: arms

== movement | kind: tail
she feints with her forepaws and the tail does the work | has: tail
the tail whips up from the ground, flicking up whatever lies there | has: tail

== thought | role: attacker
how long she can keep this up, estimated honestly | stage: middle, late, end
a rule she is about to break
a small cold satisfaction at being right about something
the next two moves, and the one she will not need if they work
that she is enjoying this more than she should | standing: winning, level
a reminder that the other is not as hurt as she looks | stage: middle, late, end

== thought | role: receiver
one word, sharp, for the pain, and then back to work
what she should have seen coming
a count of how many more of those she can take
an old lesson about getting hit, remembered in someone else's voice
that she can still stand, and what that is worth
a flash of real fear, looked at squarely and put down
spite, bright and cheering

== thought | role: pinner
that she is tired enough to make a mistake, and must not | stage: middle, late, end
a guess at how much air the other has
that the one beneath her is planning something, and what

== thought | role: pinned
that the weight shifts each time the other breathes in | fade: fighting, weakening
how much it hurts, admitted, and then set aside
a single clear instruction to one limb

== emotion | role: attacker
impatience, in a tail that will not keep still | has: tail
a cold kind of interest, head tilted
reluctant respect, in the distance she now keeps | stage: middle, late, end
frustration; she hits harder than she needs to | standing: losing, level

== emotion | role: receiver
indignation, absurd and sustaining
a hot rush of anger that briefly drowns the pain
wounded pride in the stiffness of her neck
something close to tears that turns into a snarl
determination that looks, from outside, exactly like stubbornness

== carry | zone: arm
she winces each time her {part} takes any part of the work
her {part} lags, and the move comes out lopsided

== carry | zone: leg
she plants her {part} carefully before she commits to anything
her {part} buckles a little under the push and she rides it

== carry | zone: chest, belly
she grunts with the effort and the grunt has pain in it
she has to take the breath for it in two parts

== pin_hold
she presses down with her whole body and feels the body under her press back
she keeps her face still; what it costs her shows in her ears
she counts nothing; she only watches for the next try
her grip is slick and she resets it, carefully, one contact at a time
she leans a little harder each time the other moves, and eases back by less
she hears the breath under her change and tightens before the try comes

== pin_under | fade: fighting
she bares her teeth at the face above her | facing: up
she twists her head to find where the weight is lightest
her heart is going hard and fast and she uses it

== pin_under | fade: weakening
she saves her breath now instead of spending it on anger
her eyes go to the edges of what she can see, hunting for anything
she tries less often and means it more

== pin_under | fade: fading
she whimpers and does not hear herself do it
her struggles have shrunk to a shudder
her gaze drifts and she hauls it back to her opponent's face | facing: up

== pin_under | fade: nearly out
her lips move on something she has no breath to say
her body gives one last jerk that is not a decision
the face above her blurs, doubles, and will not come clear | facing: up

== takedown
she crowds in so close the other has to step back, and puts a foot where the step will land
she catches a raised arm, ducks under it, and lets the other's own swing carry her down | has: forelegs
she slams a shoulder into the hip on the hurt side and the leg folds
she seizes the scruff and a hind leg at once and tips her like a table
she rides the other's rush down, turning in the air to land on top
she hooks both tails behind the knees and sits back hard | has: tails
she lets go of the limb she was holding only when the body is already falling
she lifts her clean off her feet, holds her there a breath to make the point, and puts her down flat

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# HOW TO OPEN A PART, AND HOW TO END ONE (so that beats do not all start and stop the same way)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== open_on
a sound, before anything is seen
her breathing, and what it tells her
the middle of a movement already begun
what she is looking at
a thought, short, before the body catches up
the ground under her feet | posture: standing
the other's face
the part of her that hurts most
stillness: the held moment before
a small thing out of place: a hair, a drop, a shadow
the distance between them, measured
something she smells or tastes
her own heartbeat
a decision already made
the light, and what it shows
what her tail, ears or fins are doing without her leave
what the last moment left in her: one after-image, not a retelling
a mistake she is about to correct
the weight on each of her feet, and which one she trusts | posture: standing
the one sound the other is making
a colour: of the light, of the other's fur, of what is on the ground
how far her next breath has to go
the feel of the air on the place that hurts
her own shadow, and the other's, and the gap between them
a muscle she did not tell to tighten
the thing she will not look at
what her ears are turned toward
a count she is keeping without meaning to
the moment after a blink
how quiet she can make herself
what the other's eyes are doing
the temperature: of the air, of her own skin
the last word she thought, still in her head
something very small and very clear, the way things are before a blow
what she can feel through the ground
her tongue, her teeth, the inside of her own mouth
a want, plain and wordless
where her weight is going to go before it goes | posture: standing

== end_on
her eyes, and where they are looking
one breath, described exactly
a sound in the place that has nothing to do with either of them
what her body is doing without being told to
the distance between them now
a small movement from the other that she notices
a thought she does not finish
how she is standing, or lying, held for a moment like a picture
the way she carries the hurt place, guarding it without thinking
silence, and what is in it
the taste in her mouth
what she can hear of the other's breathing
something in the place settling: dust, water, an echo dying
a look that passes between them and what it does not say
the smallest sign that she is not finished
her shadow, her reflection, the mark she has left on the ground
a breath let out slowly, and where it shakes
the other's shape, a little out of focus
what she does with her mouth
a count: of breaths, of heartbeats, of steps between them
the thing she is going to do next, not yet done
one drop: of water, of sweat, of blood, and where it lands
a sound she makes without choosing to
the cold or the heat reaching her again, now that she has time to feel it
which way her ears are turned
the ground, and how it feels where she is touching it
the first thing she sees clearly again
a look away, or the refusal to look away
her pulse, in the place that hurts
the other's stillness, and what it might mean
something she nearly says
how small the place has got, or how big
the part of her that is still ready
a swallow, and what it costs
the space the last sound left behind it
her weight coming back onto her own feet, or not coming back | posture: standing

# A blow thrown at no distance: inside a grab, in a pummel. There is no room to wind up.
== movement | kind: close
short strokes, elbow to wrist, with no room behind them
she works from the shoulder only; there is nowhere to step
the blows come up from underneath, between their two bodies
she uses the grip itself to pull the other onto each one
each one starts where the last one ended, with no drawing back
she turns her wrist over at the end of each, for want of room to swing
the rhythm is quick and ugly, like someone beating a rug
she leans her forehead in close and works by feel

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RAW: a blow on a part that was ALREADY badly hurt (build 113). How raw is set by the program from how hurt the
# part was, how big the blow is, and how much strength she has left: contained (she still has most of her strength
# and keeps it in), cry, scream, raw (nearly spent). Pain only: nothing breaks, no sobbing.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== raw | raw: contained
her jaw locks on the sound so hard it comes out through her nose as a thin whine, and stops there
the {part} jerks back from it on its own and she drags it back into place, breathing through her teeth
for a heartbeat her whole face goes tight and white round the eyes; then she makes it go smooth again
a hiss that she cuts off halfway, as if the rest of it were something she refused to give away

== raw | raw: cry
the cry is out before she can stop it, short and high, and she shuts her mouth on the end of it
she snatches the {part} in against her body and a shudder runs from it right through her
a yelp that cracks, and then she is breathing hard and fast through it, refusing to make another

== raw | raw: scream
a scream, a real one, and her whole body bends round the {part} as if it could shield it from what already happened
she shakes after it, small fast tremors she cannot stop, all down the side of her that holds the {part}
for a moment she cannot see her opponent at all: there is only the {part}, and her own voice ringing off the stone

== raw | raw: raw
the scream cracks in the middle and comes back thinner, and she has no breath left to finish it
every part of her clenches round the {part} at once, and the shaking that follows will not stop
her eyes stream and she cannot blink them clear; there is nothing in her head but the {part} and the next breath
a raw, broken sound goes on longer than she means it to, and stopping it costs her everything she has left

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NEWER MOMENTS (build 113+): clashes, flight, guards, water in the mouth, coils, weather, the moment after an
# escape. Offered only when that thing really happened this beat.
#   moment: takeoff, dive, carried, lift_fail, falling, spiked, grounded, leap, counter (aerial)     block, reflect, catch, turn (guard)
#   weather: rain, sun, hail
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== movement | kind: wing
a buffet of the wing, the leading edge first, like being hit with a door | has: wings
she mantles both wings wide and brings one round hard and flat | has: wings
the wing snaps out from her side with no wind-up at all | has: wings
feathers and grit in the air, and the edge of the wing somewhere inside it | has: wings

== movement | kind: beak
the head draws back on the neck like a bow being bent, and then the beak | has: beak
short hammering strikes of the beak, each one in the same place | has: beak
she turns her head to bring the hooked tip of the beak to bear | has: beak

== movement | kind: talon
the talons come forward and open at the last moment | has: talons
she reaches with her feet as if landing on prey, which is what she is doing | has: talons
the talons close and do not let go | has: talons

== clash
the two attacks meet with a sound like a slammed door the size of the room
for one moment both are leaning into it, and neither can see past the light between them
the air between them buckles; spray, sparks and grit go out sideways in a ring
she feels the other's move through her own, like pushing against a door someone is pushing back on
