the shock comes back up her own body, into her chest and teeth
the floor between them is scoured clean where the two met

== aerial | moment: takeoff
three hard downbeats, grit blasting out from under her, and she is above reach
she climbs in a tight spiral, watching over her shoulder the whole time
the ground lets go of her; the other one is suddenly small and looking up
she hangs in the air on half-folded wings, choosing

== aerial | moment: dive
she folds her wings and drops, and the sound of the air over her feathers rises to a shriek
the shadow arrives a moment before she does
she pulls out of the dive at the last instant and the whole weight of the fall goes into the blow
a dive is a bet: if she misses, she is low and slow and in reach
she comes in out of the light so the other has to squint to find her

== aerial | moment: carried
the ground drops away under her feet and there is nothing to push against
she twists in the grip and the talons only tighten
the arena turns below her, smaller every wingbeat
the moment of letting go is quiet; the fall is not
she has time, falling, to know exactly how hard she will land

== aerial | moment: grounded
the wing gives under her as if a string had been cut, and the air stops holding her
she beats once, twice, lopsided, and comes down sideways
she hits the ground still trying to fly, the good wing hammering at nothing

== guard | moment: block
the barrier takes it with a sound like a struck bell and is gone
the attack spends itself against nothing and she has not even moved
behind the shell her eyes never leave her opponent
the other one's whole effort, wasted against a wall of light

== guard | moment: reflect
she lets it land, on purpose, and gives it back with interest
the force goes in and comes straight back out along the same line
her opponent's own strength, turned round and returned to sender
she was waiting for exactly this, and it shows

== sputter
water up the nose and down the throat at once, and her body forgets everything except getting it out
she coughs so hard her eyes stream, and spits, and coughs again
every breath she tries to take catches on the water still in her
the cough doubles her over and leaves her open, and she knows it
her chest heaves against it; the air comes in ragged and wet

== constriction | fade: fighting
she can feel her own pulse thudding in her paws, slow and heavy, against the squeeze
each breath out is taken from her; each breath in is a little smaller than the last
the coils are warm and patient and do not stop

== constriction | fade: weakening
her paws are going cold and heavy, the blood held back from them
her heartbeat slows in her ears, a heavy, dragging drum, slower each time
the edges of her sight are going grey and soft

== constriction | fade: fading
her heart is still going, but slow, so slow, and everything beyond the coils has gone quiet
her limbs are far away and do not answer when she calls them
she cannot tell any more where her body ends and the coils begin

== constriction | fade: nearly out
a slow, heavy beat, then a long gap, then another: her heart keeping on, too slow to hold her up
the world narrows to one thudding beat and the warm weight around it

== constriction
the scales slide against each other as the coils settle tighter
there is no single place it hurts: it is everywhere at once, and it is steady
she pushes against the coils and they take the push and give nothing back

== weather | weather: rain
rain hammering on stone, on fur, on the water, everywhere at once
the footing goes slick and treacherous in a moment
water runs into her eyes and she blinks it away
everything shines; the light goes grey and soft

== weather | weather: sun
the light turns hard and white, and heat comes up off the ground in waves
the glare off every wet surface makes her squint
her fur is hot to the touch; her breath comes shorter in the heat

== weather | weather: hail
hailstones bounce off the stone like thrown gravel
the stinging comes from everywhere, small and endless
ice gathers in the fur along her back and shoulders
she ducks her head against it and that, too, costs her

== escape_after
she is free, but the body that comes up out of the pin is stiff and slow to answer
her muscles are cramped from being held so long and do not want to do what she asks
she rolls her shoulders and the ache from the hold runs all the way down her back
she keeps her distance and watches, having learned exactly how that pin felt
she knows the shape of that grip now, and will see it coming next time
the blood comes back into her limbs in pins and needles, hot and prickling

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# DWELL: the extra part after a blow on someone already badly hurt (narration.dwell). Nothing new happens: these
# are what the blow has DONE to her, felt from inside. {part} is the hurt part.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== dwell | zone: chest
every breath has to go in past the {part}, and every one of them finds it
she can feel her own heartbeat in the {part}, each beat a fresh push against the hurt
she draws her elbows or forelegs in over the {part} without deciding to, as if holding it shut
the {part} feels too big for her body, hot and swollen and wrong under the fur

== dwell | zone: belly
her belly has gone hard and tight round the {part}, every muscle clenched over it at once
a slow, sick wave rolls out from the {part} and her stomach turns over with it
she folds round the {part} by a few degrees, then a few more, unable to straighten

== dwell | zone: head
a high thin ringing has started in her ears and will not stop
the light seems too bright and moves a little when she moves her eyes
she blinks hard and her vision swims and comes back slowly, the edges last
a dull pressure builds behind her eyes in time with her pulse

== dwell | zone: neck
swallowing has become a decision, and each one hurts all the way down
she holds her head very still, as if it were balanced on something that might give
the throbbing in her {part} reaches up into her jaw and down into her chest at once

== dwell | zone: arm
the {part} hangs at her side; she tells it to move and it answers late and badly
her paw keeps opening and closing on its own, the fingers stiff and slow
there is a hot, deep ache all the way down the arm, and her shoulder has taken it up too

== dwell | zone: leg
the {part} shakes under her weight, a fine fast tremor she cannot stop by tensing
she keeps the weight off it, and the rest of her body starts to ache from the effort of doing so
when she tests the {part} the pain comes up through it like a nail being driven in

== dwell | zone: back
she cannot find a way to hold herself that does not pull on her {part}
her whole spine has gone rigid round the hurt, and turning her head pulls on it
the ache in her back spreads out in slow rings, down into her hips and up between her shoulders

== dwell | zone: tail
her tail lies limp behind her and the hurt in it throbs on its own, out of time with everything else
she tries to lift her tail and it barely moves; the pain answers anyway

== dwell
the hurt doesn't arrive all at once: it comes in, then comes in again, deeper each time
she is shaking now, a fine tremor that starts at the {part} and runs out through the rest of her
cold sweat under her fur, though nothing about her is cold
her body has stopped asking her what to do and is simply curling round the {part}
for a few heartbeats the {part} is the whole world, and the arena is somewhere far off
the muscles round the {part} jump and twitch on their own, little spasms she can feel but not stop
she makes herself unclench one muscle at a time, and the first one she lets go of brings the pain back sharper
the pain has a rhythm now, a slow pulse, and she finds herself bracing for each beat of it
she checks herself the way you check a house after a storm: this still works, this doesn't, this she won't try yet
she can taste copper and grit and does not remember biting anything
the ache settles into the {part} as if it means to live there

== held_in | state: fresh, winded
she bites the sound off before it is more than a breath through her nose
a hiss through her teeth, short, and then her jaw is clamped shut on everything else
the noise starts in her chest and she swallows it back down before it reaches her mouth
a grunt she turns into a cough, as if that fools anyone

== held_in | state: tired
a thin whine leaks out through her shut teeth however hard she clenches them
she presses her muzzle into her own shoulder to keep the sound inside
the sound comes out in pieces between breaths, each one bitten off a little later than the last
she holds her breath to stop the noise and lets it out in a shaking rush that is half a groan

== held_in | state: spent
she has nothing left to hold it in with: the groan comes out low and long and she simply lets it
a broken little sound escapes every time she breathes out, and she has stopped trying to stop it
she tries to keep quiet and cannot: a ragged, wordless noise comes up out of her and goes on
the cry is too tired to be loud: a hoarse, cracked sound that trails off into panting

== held_in | raw: scream, raw
she screams through gritted teeth, which only makes it uglier
after the scream, a long shuddering intake of breath and then a lower sound she can't help

== dwell_breath | state: fresh, winded
her breath comes fast and shallow, high in her chest, and she has to make herself slow it
she counts her breaths to keep them even, and loses count at three

== dwell_breath | state: tired, spent
heavy, dragging breaths, each one shuddering on the way in
she breathes through her open mouth, harsh and loud in her own ears
each breath out ends in a small hitch, like a stair she keeps missing
her ribs heave; she cannot get enough air, and every big breath hurts more than the small ones

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# WATCH: the extra part from the side of the one who did it, studying her (narration.watch).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== watch | posture: down, pinned
she watches the ribs under her heave, counting the hitches in each breath
she feels every tremor through her own body where she holds her, and learns where each one comes from
she watches the eyes: how long they stay shut, how slowly they open, whether they still find her
she notices which limb stopped struggling first, and files it away
she leans her weight a fraction to one side just to see what it does to the face below her
she listens to the sounds being swallowed and hears exactly when one gets out

== watch | posture: standing
she watches the other's weight shift off the hurt side and stay off it
she sees the paw come up to cover the wound and drop again, too late to pretend
she watches the knees, the tremble in them, and waits to see if they will hold
she marks how the other turns her body to keep the injury away, and knows where to aim next

== watch | state: spent, tired
there is a looseness in the way the other holds herself now, and she reads it like a page
the twitches have slowed; the breathing has not
the eyes keep losing focus and finding it again, a little slower every time
she watches a shiver run the whole length of the body in front of her and settle in the {part}

== watch
she does not look away; she wants to see all of it
she studies the flinch the way she studied the opening that caused it
every small movement tells her something: what works, what does not, what is lying
she watches the muscles round the wound jump and twitch, and something in her goes very calm
her own breath is loud in her ears; she slows it so she can hear the other's
she notices the fur standing up along the other's spine and the way it does not settle
she sees the tail stop moving entirely and knows what that means | has: tail
she watches the ears pin flat and stay there | has: ears

# KNOCKOUT: very rarely a beat knocks her out cold with no pin (moves.knockout).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== knockout
the blow lands and something behind her eyes simply switches off
she does not fall so much as stop holding herself up, all of her at once
her eyes stay open a moment after she is gone, and then the lids slide down
every muscle lets go together, and she pours to the ground like water
there is no cry; the sound she was going to make never arrives
she lands and does not try to catch herself, and that is how it is plain she is gone
her body bounces once on the ground, loose as a dropped rope, and settles
a last breath goes out of her in a long sigh, and the next one comes slow and shallow, from very far down
her limbs fold under her at angles she would never have allowed awake
her tail gives one last twitch and lies still | has: tail
her wings fall open on the ground on either side of her and do not close | has: wings

== knockout_watch
she stands over the body, waiting for it to move, and it does not
the silence after is enormous; she can hear her own heart in it
she lowers herself by a degree, ready for a trick, and slowly understands there is no trick
her own legs start shaking now that there is nothing left to hold them up for
she watches the slow rise and fall of the other's side and lets out the breath she had been holding

# COLLAPSE: below zero health, her body gives out on its own (health.collapse).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== collapse
her legs go first, not with pain but with a kind of quiet, as if they had simply stopped being asked
she takes a step that isn't there, and the ground comes up to meet her
there is no blow; she just folds, the way a held breath finally lets go
her knees buckle and for a moment she hangs there, surprised, before the rest of her follows
she feels it coming a heartbeat too late: the edges of the world going soft, the weight of her own body too much
she goes down slowly, almost carefully, like something set down by tired hands
she lies where she fell, blinking, trying to work out how she got there
the worst of it is the silence in her limbs when she tells them to move
she reaches for the ground to push, and her arm only trembles against it | has: arms
her tail drags once across the ground and lies still | has: tail

# SELF_LOOK: the one who is hurt looking at her own body (narration.self_look.chance in ordinary hit passages;
# always offered in dwell and read). {part} is the hurt part.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== self_look
she looks down at her {part} as if it belonged to someone else, and watches it tremble
she makes herself look at the {part}: the fur there flattened and wrong, already swelling under it
she tests the {part} with a small careful movement and watches to see whether it obeys; it does, late
she sees her own paw shaking and cannot make it stop by looking at it | has: paws
she glances down at the {part} and away again quickly, as if not looking might help
she watches the muscle in her {part} jump on its own, a small animal under her skin
she takes stock the way she would of someone else: the {part} first, then everything it is dragging with it
she flexes the {part} once to see what is left, and the answer is less than she hoped
she notices her own breathing before she notices anything else: too fast, too high, and she cannot slow it
she looks at the {part} and understands, with a cold little drop, that it is going to be a problem
she watches a tremor start at the {part} and climb, and braces against it as if it were a blow

== self_look | state: spent, tired, hurt
her own limbs look far away, and she has to think about each one before it moves
she looks down at herself and barely recognizes the shape she is holding
she counts what still works, and it is a short count

== self_look | zone: leg
she puts weight on the leg as a question, and it answers with a jolt that runs to her hip
she looks at the leg and sees it already held off the ground, before she decided to lift it

== self_look | zone: arm
she lifts the arm a little to see if it will come, and watches it shake all the way up
she looks at her paw curled in against her chest and cannot remember curling it

== self_look | zone: chest, belly
she looks down and sees her own sides heaving, faster than she feels them
she sees her middle drawn in tight on its own, guarding, and she lets it

== self_look | zone: head
she blinks and watches the world slide and come back, and checks it again, and again

# READ: the other way round: the one who was hurt studies the one who did it (narration.read).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== read
she watches the other breathe and counts: easy, easy, then one that catches
she sees the eyes go to her hurt side and rest there, and knows exactly where the next one is meant to land
she marks the way the other stands, a little too square, as if one leg had begun to argue
there is satisfaction in that face, and she makes herself look at it and remember it
she reads the stillness up there: patient, unhurried, a hunter who has stopped needing to hurry
she sees a tremor in the other's own limbs, small, quickly hidden, and holds onto it like a stone in her paw
the other is breathing harder than she was a minute ago; not much, but she hears it
she studies the wound she gave earlier, and sees it has not stopped bothering its owner
she notes where the weight goes before every shift, the way you learn a dance by watching the feet
the other's gaze moves over her like hands checking something bought, and she hates it, and watches anyway
she sees the tail twitch once, eager, and understands that the other is enjoying this | has: tail
the wingbeats above her are steady, unhurried; she listens for the first one that isn't | has: wings
she sees the claws flex and settle, flex and settle, and knows what they are thinking about | has: claws

== read | state: spent, tired, hurt
it takes her too long to bring the other into focus, and that frightens her more than the pain
she watches the other's feet because lifting her eyes to the face costs more than she has
she can see the other is fresh still, and the arithmetic of that settles cold in her stomach

== read_thought
*She's favouring it. She thinks I didn't see.*
*Look at her. She thinks it's over.*
*She's breathing harder too. Good.*
*She's going to go for the same place again. Let her try.*
*Not like this. Not to you.*
*Remember that face.*
*She's tired too. She's hiding it better, that's all.*
*Every time she does that, she drops her left side.*

== watch_thought
*That one went in. Look at her.*
*Not getting up from that quickly.*
*She's guarding it. Good. Now I know.*
*Breathing like that won't last.*
*Every twitch is telling me something.*
*Hold still. Let me see.*
*She's trying not to make a sound. She won't manage it for long.*
*Remember this. Remember exactly where.*

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MORE OF THE THIN ONES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== breath | state: fresh
she breathes out once, long, and resets
easy breathing still, but she is paying attention to it now
she takes a deep breath through her nose and lets it out slow, steadying

== breath | zone: belly
she breathes high in her chest to keep from moving her belly at all
every breath presses her stomach out against the hurt, so she keeps them small

== breath | zone: chest
breathing in all the way is out of the question; she takes half-breaths and makes do
her ribs catch on each breath like a door sticking on its hinge

== breath | zone: neck
air scrapes on the way in, a rasp she can hear and so can everyone else
she breathes carefully round the throat, as if the air itself had edges

== sensation | level: sore | zone: back
a stiff bruised ache across her back when she twists
her back feels tight and hot, like a pulled muscle

== sensation | level: sore | zone: head
a dull throb at her temple, already fading
her head rings faintly, more annoyance than pain

== sensation | zone: tail | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
the hurt in her {part} pulls at the base of her spine with every swing
a deep, aching throb runs from her {part} up into her hips
her {part} has gone heavy and slow, and moving it sends a bolt up her back

== sensation | zone: back | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
pain in her {part} spreads sideways along her ribs whenever she breathes deep
the {part} feels bruised to the bone, a heavy, deep ache that settles with her weight
twisting at all lights her {part} up like a struck match

== travel
the hurt in her {part} runs into her {next} like water finding a channel
her {next} tightens in sympathy, bracing a pain that isn't its own
the ache climbs from her {part} into her {next} and sits there, dull and wide
her {next} starts to throb too, an echo half a beat behind

== thought | role: receiver | stage: late, end
*Not yet. Not like this.*
*Keep breathing. Just that.*
*I can still move. I can still move.*
*How much more is there in me?*

== thought | role: losing
*Think. There has to be something left.*
*She's not unbeatable. She's breathing hard too.*
*One opening. That's all I need.*

== thought | role: winning
*She's slowing down. Keep it on her.*
*Don't get careless now.*
*Finish it clean.*

== struggle
she plants what she can and heaves, every muscle at once
she twists her hips against the weight, looking for any give at all
she bucks once, hard, and the effort leaves her gasping
she works one limb free an inch at a time, and has to start again when it's pinned back
she goes still on purpose, waiting for the grip to shift, and then tries again

== landing
the ground comes up all at once and knocks the breath out of her
she hits and skids, grit tearing at her fur
she lands wrong, on the side that already hurt
she rolls with it, but not enough
for a moment after she lands she cannot tell which way is up

== dodge
she twists aside with a hair's width to spare and feels the air of it pass
she drops low and it goes over her
she turns her shoulder and it slides off nothing
she reads it a heartbeat early and is simply not there when it arrives

== getup
she gets one limb under her, then another, and stops to breathe before the last
her first try folds under her; the second holds, just
she uses everything she can reach to haul herself up, and stands swaying
she rises slowly, testing each part before trusting it with her weight

== sputter
water in her nose burns all the way back into her skull
she coughs up a mouthful of water and gasps after it
the coughing shakes the hurt in her ribs every time

== clash
they meet in a blaze of light and force that throws grit across the floor
both of them are rocked back by the meeting of the two attacks
the sound of it goes on echoing long after the light is gone

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GUARDS AND MISSED CHARGES (rare: moves.guard, moves.missed_charge)
#   guard moment: catch (a blow caught on a forearm, foreleg, wing, horn, coils), turn (turned aside)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== guard | moment: catch
the guard comes up a heartbeat before the blow and takes it, and the shock runs all the way to her shoulder
she catches it on the hard outside of the limb, where there is more bone than anything else
it lands on her guard instead of where it was aimed, and still hurts, but nothing like it would have
she feels the force go into the guard and stop there, and lets out the breath she was holding
the block numbs the limb for a moment; she shakes it out and doesn't take her eyes off her opponent
her horn rings with the impact, and she sets her head against the force of it | has: horn
she takes it on a loop of her body, scales flexing, the force spread along the coil | has: coils
a wing comes round like a shield and the blow drives into the stiff feathers at its leading edge | has: wings

== guard | moment: turn
she meets it at an angle and it simply slides off her, wasted
she gives with it instead of against it, and it goes on past her into nothing
a touch at the right moment, no more, and the whole blow turns aside
she redirects it with almost no effort, and that is the insulting part
for an instant they are very close, and then the attack has gone wide and her opponent is stumbling past

== crash
there is nothing where she aimed, and all that speed has to go somewhere
she sees the obstacle coming and cannot stop, and turns her shoulder at the last moment to take it
the impact shocks back through her own body, teeth to tail
she hits it hard, harder than she meant anything to hit, and bounces off
for a moment after the crash she does not know which way she is facing
the rage of the miss is worse than the bruise, almost
dust and grit come down round her where she struck

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FIGHTER-SPECIFIC: "fighter: name" blocks are offered only for that fighter, and preferred for her when they fit
# (narration.blocks.fighter_boost). Add your own for anyone in fighters.json.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== watch | fighter: nocturne
she watches with her head low and her red eyes narrowed to slits, the way she would watch prey decide whether to run
her sickle tail goes still behind her while she watches; only the tip twitches
she notes every flinch with a cold, measuring contempt, and files each one away
her ears turn to catch the sounds the other is trying to swallow
she lets her lip peel back from one fang as she watches, not quite a snarl

== watch | fighter: seraphina
she watches with her head raised and swaying slightly, unhurried, red eyes half-closed
her antennae drift toward the other, as if they could taste the pain in the air
she watches the way still water watches the sky: patient, unreadable, missing nothing
her low hum goes on under her breath as she watches, calm as a lullaby

== watch | fighter: ripples
she watches with her chin tucked and her fins flared, quick black eyes darting from wound to wound
she watches warily, half crouched, her twin tails turning slow behind her, ready to go if it was a trick
her cheek marks twitch as she watches; she is counting, working out what she has bought

== watch | fighter: talon
she watches with her head cocked, one eye and then the other, the way a hawk studies something in the grass
her crest lifts slowly as she watches, and her talons flex on the ground

== watch | fighter: vesper
she watches without blinking, tongue flickering, tasting the fear in the air
her hood spreads a little wider with every twitch she sees

== watch | fighter: blaze
she watches with her ears pricked and her head tilted, honest concern flickering under the battle heat
heat shimmers off her fur as she watches, her flanks heaving

== watch | fighter: aura
the appendages on the back of her head lift as she reads the other's aura: she can see the pain as a flicker of light
she watches in a still stance, feet set, reading where the strength is draining out

== watch | fighter: undertow
she watches with her flotation sac puffed and a grin she can't quite keep down
she bounces on her toes as she watches, cocky, tails whirring

== watch_thought | fighter: nocturne

*Not so quick now.*
*I warned you about the dry ground.*

== watch_thought | fighter: seraphina

*Poor thing. You fought well.*
*Breathe. It will be easier if you stop resisting.*

== watch_thought | fighter: ripples

*Don't get cocky. Don't get cocky.*
*She felt that one. Good.*

== watch_thought | fighter: talon
*Again. Until she stops looking up.*
*Grounded prey. Easy prey.*

== watch_thought | fighter: vesper
*Struggle. I like it when they struggle.*
*Slower now. Slower.*

== held_in | fighter: nocturne
a growl rises in her chest and she turns it into nothing but breath through her teeth
she hisses, sharp and short, and refuses to give it any more than that
a low, ragged snarl, the only sound she will allow herself

== held_in | fighter: ripples
a squeak escapes her before she can clamp her jaw on it, and her cheeks burn with it
she chitters through gritted teeth, fast and furious, instead of crying out
a high bark of pain cracks out of her and echoes, and she hates that it echoes

== held_in | fighter: seraphina
her low humming note cracks in the middle and she holds the silence after it
a long hiss escapes her coils as they tighten on their own

== held_in | fighter: talon
a harsh shriek tears out of her open beak before she can close it
she hisses through her beak, feathers fluffed out, and keeps the rest in

== dwell | fighter: nocturne
her ruff stands up all the way round her neck and will not lie down
her ears flatten so hard they ache, and she shakes her head to clear it, and it doesn't clear
she tells herself it is nothing, and her legs disagree with her

== dwell | fighter: ripples
her fins clamp flat to her forearms and her twin tails stall and hang
her throat works as she swallows, over and over, her chin tucked tight to guard it
she curls her webbed paws into fists until her claws bite her own palms

== dwell | fighter: seraphina
her coils tighten round nothing on their own, and the hurt rolls down the length of her body
her scales dull and flare along her side in slow waves, out of her control

== thought | role: receiver | fighter: nocturne
*Pain is information. Nothing more.*
*She will pay for that.*
*Don't show it. Don't show anything.*

== thought | role: receiver | fighter: ripples
*Stupid. Should've seen it.*

*So. She has teeth after all.*

== thought | role: attacker | fighter: nocturne
*Faster than her. Always faster.*
*End this.*

== thought | role: attacker | fighter: ripples
*In and out. Don't let her grab you.*
*Now. While she's turned.*

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FEINTS, JUGGLES, LAST STANDS
#   feint moment: bit (the defender fell for it), read (she saw through it)
#   juggle moment: up (knocked into the air), caught (hit there and smashed down)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== feint | moment: bit
the shoulder dips one way and every instinct she has goes with it, and the real blow is already coming from the other side
her eyes follow the fake, and by the time they come back it is far too late
she commits to blocking a blow that never comes, and is turned wide open for the one that does
a twitch of the head, a half-step, and she flinches from nothing, and pays for it
the feint drags her guard high, and the strike goes in underneath

== feint | moment: read
the fake is good, but she has seen it once already and her weight doesn't move
she watches the eyes, not the paw, and the eyes give it away
she doesn't bite; she simply waits for the real thing

== juggle | moment: up
the ground leaves her all at once and there is nothing under her paws at all
for a moment she hangs in the air, weightless, and has time to know she cannot do anything about what comes next
she is lifted clean off her feet, limbs flung out, the world tilting

== juggle | moment: caught
nothing to push against, nothing to brace with: the second blow takes her in the air and she simply goes where it sends her
she was still rising when it hit, and the force reverses her in mid-air and drives her down
the ground comes back up at her twice as fast as she left it

== last_stand
everything she has left goes into this, and she knows there will be nothing after it
a sound tears out of her as she throws it, half roar and half gasp of effort
she has been saving nothing, and now she spends it all at once
for one moment she is as strong as she was at the start, and then she is emptied out

== frenzy
she has stopped counting; her body just keeps doing it
the same place, again, again, until the spot is all there is
somewhere in it the blows stop being a choice and become a rhythm
she hears herself panting in time with it and doesn't stop
the first blows were aimed; the last ones are just hers, falling where the others fell

# OFF TARGET (moves.aim): a blow aimed at a small or hard to reach part lands on the part beside it instead.
== off_target
she picks her spot and the spot moves; the blow lands a hand's width off it
the place she wanted is tucked away behind a chin or a shoulder, and she hits what is in front of it
she sees the opening, and by the time she gets there it has closed; she takes what is there instead
it goes where she sent it, near enough; just not quite where she meant
a small target, moving; she gets the part beside it
she adjusts in the last instant and it is not enough; the blow skids off onto the next thing over
== off_target_take
she tucks it away just in time, and the blow finds the part beside it instead
a twist, a dip of the head, and the blow that was meant for one place lands on another
she does not save herself the hit; she only moves where it lands
the blow comes for something small and finds something bigger
she gives up the bigger part to keep the smaller one safe, without thinking

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# DEVASTATING BLOWS (very rare: moves.devastating). Why this one is so much worse, and how both of them feel it.
#   reason: position (caught open), weak_point (a weak point in a weak point), angle (perfect angle and timing),
#           ground (the landing found every sore spot)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== devastating | reason: position
she had no ground under her, nothing braced, nothing to give with: the whole force went in and stayed in
it came while she was turned the wrong way, and her body had already spent its guard on something else
caught mid-movement, every muscle committed elsewhere, there was nothing between the blow and the inside of her
she couldn't roll with it, couldn't twist, couldn't even tense: it simply arrived, all of it

== devastating | reason: weak_point
it found the one place in the {part} that was already hurt the deepest, and went straight into it
the {part} had been holding together on nothing but stubbornness, and this was the blow that found the bottom of it
of all the places on the {part}, it hit the exact spot that had already taken the most, and the old hurt and the new one became one thing
the earlier blows had softened the {part}; this one arrived at the soft centre of it

== devastating | reason: angle
the angle was perfect: not a sliver of force glanced away, all of it went in
it caught her on the breath out, when everything inside her was loose and open
the timing was so exact it was almost quiet: one instant she was set, the next the force was already inside her
it landed square, flush, with the whole weight behind it, the kind of blow that only comes once in a fight

== devastating | reason: ground
she came down so that every hurt part hit at once, as if the ground had been waiting for each one
the landing found the bruises, the swollen places, the wounds, all together, and hammered each of them
there was no good way to land, and she found the worst one: every sore spot first

== devastated_reaction | state: fresh, winded
the shock of it goes through her like cold water, and for a moment she can only stand there and take it
a sharp cry is out before she can stop it, and then her jaw is clamped and she is breathing hard through her nose
she jolts from end to end, and her eyes have gone wide: nothing in this fight has hit her like that
everything in her goes very still for a heartbeat, the way it does after a near fall, and then the ache arrives
she shakes her head hard, as if she could shake it off, and knows she can't

== devastated_reaction | state: tired, spent
a sound comes out of her she has never made before, and doesn't stop when she wants it to
for a long moment she cannot do anything at all: not breathe, not move, not see
her whole body locks round the hurt and starts shaking, a hard, deep shudder from the core outward
her legs simply stop holding her up, and she has to fight to stay where she is
the colour seems to go out of everything; there is the pain, and a high ringing, and nothing else
she curls in on it so hard her own claws dig into her

== devastated_thought | state: fresh, winded
*Where did that come from?*
*Okay. Okay. Don't let that happen again.*
*That was— no. Focus.*

== devastated_thought | state: tired, spent
*That one. That one did something.*
*Can't. Can't breathe. Can't—*
*How is it so much worse?*
*Get up. Get up get up get up.*
*Something's wrong. Something's really wrong.*

== devastating_thought
*She felt that. All of it.*
*That went in. That went all the way in.*
*There. Right there. That's the one.*
*Didn't even feel it land. Just felt it go through.*

== devastating_thought | fighter: nocturne
*Finally. The opening I wanted.*
*Now she understands.*

== devastated_thought | fighter: ripples
*No. No no no. Not like that.*
*Okay, that— that was bad. That was really bad.*

# DEVASTATING_SELL: how to make a devastating hit believable at every point in the fight.
#   health: fresh (60%+ of her strength left), worn (30-60%), spent (under 30%)
#   partlevel: light (the part isn't badly hurt even now), hurt (very painful to excruciating), ruined (devastated)
== devastating_sell | health: fresh | partlevel: light
for a heartbeat she just stands there, blinking, as if her body is waiting for permission to feel it
it rattles her down to her paws: not the {part} so much as all of her, shaken like a struck bell
her eyes go wide and stay wide; nobody has hit her like that in a long time, maybe ever
she laughs, once, short and shocked, because the alternative is something else
the cry gets out, sharp and surprised, and she is angry at it before it has finished echoing

== devastating_sell | health: fresh | partlevel: hurt, ruined
the {part} stops answering her for a moment, and she looks down at it as if it belonged to someone else
she's still strong, still fresh, and that makes it worse: she can feel exactly how much it took out of her
the hurt in the {part} goes from a fact to the only fact, and it is far too early in the fight for that

== devastating_sell | health: worn | partlevel: light
it doesn't break anything open, but it knocks loose whatever she was holding together with
she sways, steadies, sways again: the blow is spent but her balance hasn't heard yet
somewhere in her the fight changes its mind about how long she can last

== devastating_sell | health: worn | partlevel: hurt, ruined
the {part} was what she was hiding, and now there is no hiding it
she has been rationing her strength all fight; this spends a week of it in a heartbeat
her body folds round the {part}, unfolds because she makes it, and folds again a little further

== devastating_sell | health: spent | partlevel: light
there's nothing left in her to absorb it, so even this goes straight through
she doesn't fall, quite, but she stops being sure which way is down
it lands somewhere that wasn't even hurt, and somehow that is the most frightening thing yet

== devastating_sell | health: spent | partlevel: hurt, ruined
there is no buffer left: the {part} takes it raw, and everything in her goes with it
the scream, when it comes, sounds far away to her, like someone else's
or there is no scream at all, just a long, terrible quiet, and her eyes not quite finding anything
she shakes, a deep, steady shaking she can't stop, from the {part} outward to every limb

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ANTICIPATION: a blow coming at a part that is already soft or badly hurt. She sees it coming and her body tries
# to protect it before it lands; the attacker sees that.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== anticipate
her whole body flinches before it lands, the hurt part snatched in against her
she turns the hurt side away without meaning to, a fraction too late
everything in her braces round that one place, and she knows it shows
she sees where it's going and a small sound gets out of her before it even arrives
the old hurt flares just from seeing it come, as if it already knew
her guard drops to cover it, and leaves the rest of her open
her fins clamp flat against her arms before the blow even reaches her | has: fins
her ears pin back and her ruff goes up as she twists the hurt part away | has: ruff

== anticipate_seen
she sees the flinch before she lands it, and aims for exactly that place
the way she guards it tells her everything she needs to know
she watches the hurt part get pulled in, and smiles inside
she feels her opponent brace, and puts the blow where the bracing is
*There. That's what she's afraid of.*
*Protecting it. Good. Now I know.*

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RAW, MORE: a ruined part touched, or worse, struck, by a fighter with almost nothing left to hold it in with
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== raw | raw: raw
her body jerks away from the {part} on its own, every limb pulling in, and a raw sound tears out with every breath
she can't stop her legs from scrabbling at the ground, trying to get away from the {part} that is part of her
a sound comes up out of her with the pain, and then another, cracked and helpless
she curls so hard round the {part} that her own claws dig into her, and still she can't stop shaking

== raw | raw: scream, raw
even the touch was too much: the {part} has nothing left to take anything with
the scream comes before she even knows she's been touched there

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# STATUS TELLS: what a status looks like on her (status_tell) and what her opponent reads in it (status_seen).
#   status: flinched, dazed, off_balance, doubled_over, breathless, sputtering, stiff, cramped, bound, constricted,
#           paralyzed, confused; status_seen also: opening (a blow going into one)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== status_tell | status: flinched
her head snaps away from it and stays turned, eyes screwed shut, for one heartbeat too long
every muscle locks at once and for a moment she is a statue of a fighter
she tells her legs to move and they don't, not yet
her ears flatten and her whole face pinches shut round the blow | has: ears
her fins clamp flat to her forearms and won't open | has: fins

== status_tell | status: dazed
the world comes in two copies that slide slowly back together
she blinks hard and shakes her head, and the ringing doesn't go
sounds arrive late, as if from the other side of water
her eyes drift off her opponent and have to be dragged back

== status_tell | status: off_balance
her weight goes too far forward and she has to throw a paw down to catch herself
she stumbles a step past where she meant to stop
for a moment her feet are in the wrong places and she knows it

== status_tell | status: doubled_over
she folds round it, head down, unable to straighten
her guard drops to clutch the hurt, and stays there
she stays bent, breathing at the ground, for one heartbeat, two

== status_tell | status: breathless
her mouth opens and nothing goes in
her ribs heave against nothing; the breath is just gone
she can't get enough air to do anything but stand there and want it

== status_tell | status: stiff
her limbs answer slowly, numb from the hold, like borrowed ones
she shakes out the arm that was trapped and it tingles all the way down

== status_tell | status: bound
the held limb goes cold and heavy, prickling with pins and needles
she pulls and the grip only tightens; the limb hardly feels like hers

== status_tell | status: sputtering
water streams out of her nose and she coughs until her eyes run
she can't stop coughing long enough to set her feet

== status_seen | status: flinched, dazed, off_balance, doubled_over, breathless, sputtering
she sees it and her whole body leans toward the opening
that's the moment, and she knows it
*Now. While she can't.*
she doesn't wait to see if it lasts
she marks the opening the way she marks a weak spot: once, coldly

== status_seen | status: stiff, cramped, bound
she sees how slowly the limb answers, and files it away
she feels the strength going out of the held limb

== status_seen | status: opening
she goes straight into the gap the flinch left
she doesn't aim; she just goes where the guard isn't
the blow is already moving before the opening has finished appearing

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RESOLVE SHOWN: what a fighter DOES when the story wants to say "she would not be defeated". The program offers
# two of these when a draft tells resolve instead of showing it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== resolve_shown
she sets her feet again, wider than before
her eyes come back to her opponent and stay there
she lowers her head and squares up
she takes one long breath through her nose and lets it out slowly
she shakes herself once, hard, and steps forward instead of back
she takes back the ground she just lost, one deliberate step
her weight settles low and even
the trembling stops because she makes it stop
she rolls the stiff shoulder once and stops paying it any attention
she wipes her muzzle with the back of a paw and doesn't look at what comes away
her chin comes up a fraction
she picks her line across the ground and starts along it
she flexes her claws into the ground and lets them go
she doesn't glance at the hurt place, not once
she breathes out slowly so the breath won't show
the hitch in her stance is gone the next time she moves
she circles toward the side her opponent is favouring
she spits to one side and resets
she waits, perfectly still, letting the other one decide when
she lets her opponent see her stand up straight
she tests the hurt limb with her full weight, deliberately, where her opponent can see
she steps over the scuffed ground where she went down without looking at it
her breathing evens out, one count at a time
she moves first, before she can think better of it
she plants herself between her opponent and the ground she wants
her lips peel back from her teeth and stay there | has: forelegs
the hackles along her ruff rise and do not go down | has: ruff
her ears come forward, both of them, and stay up | has: ears
her tail goes rigid, then lifts | has: tail
the twin tails pick up their rhythm again, steady and even | has: tails
the fins on her forearms open and stay open | has: fins
she curls her webbed paws into fists and opens them again | has: arms
she plants both forepaws and leans into them | has: forelegs
she turns her head so the horn faces her opponent squarely | has: horn
she lowers the horn, slowly, into line | has: horn
she tucks her chin to cover her throat and comes on anyway

# very painful may draw a short cry, excruciating may draw screams, devastated means the part barely works.

the tail trembles and twitches
her balance wavers with the tail gone useless
she wraps the tail around herself, shaking

== pain_show | level: excruciating

== pain_show | level: excruciating | state: tired

== pain_show | level: excruciating | state: spent

== pain_show | level: excruciating | state: winded
