# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# BUILD 133: FLIGHT, ELEMENTS AND THE NEW BODIES
# moment: takeoff, dive, carried, grounded (her own hurt wing gives out), knocked (a blow knocks a sound flyer out
#         of the sky), struck_from_above (the one a dive lands on), strain (staying up on a badly hurt wing),
#         lift_fail (a flyer seizes her but can't haul her up), falling (struck on the way down after a carry),
#         spiked (driven straight down into the ground from above, faster than she'd fall)
# kind: fire, ice, wind, rock (the element of a move, alongside its shape: a Fire Fang is bite and fire)
# has: wings, feathers (a bird: beak, no fur), crest, flame, mane, tails (several), talons, beak, arms
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== aerial | moment: takeoff
she runs three steps, opens her wings and the ground falls away beneath the fourth | has: legs
a downdraft flattens the grass in a ring around the place she was standing
the first wingbeat is all effort; by the third she is riding the air instead of fighting it
she goes up steep and fast, out of reach before the other has finished turning
she climbs with her head turned down, never once taking her eyes off her opponent
dust and loose feathers swirl under her as she lifts, and then there is only the shadow | has: feathers
her wings open to their full span and the whole arena seems to shrink beneath them
she hangs at the top of the climb, wings spread wide, and lets the other one look up at her

== aerial | moment: dive
she tips over at the top of her climb and comes down like a dropped stone
the wings close tight against her body and she becomes a single falling line
the air screams past her and she aims the whole fall at one place
a flare of the wings at the last moment, and all the speed goes into the blow
she drops out of the sun and the other has to throw up a guard half blind
the dive comes in at a slant, low over the ground at the end, too fast to sidestep
she corrects once in the fall, a twitch of one wingtip, and the line is true again
the sound reaches the other a heartbeat before she does: a hiss of air over feathers | has: feathers

== aerial | moment: struck_from_above
the hit comes from above, where there is no way to brace for it
the weight of the whole fall lands on her at once and drives her down against the ground
she looks up too late: there is only a shape, and then the blow
it is like being hit by something thrown from a cliff
the air goes out of her in one push, as if the sky itself had stepped on her
she staggers under it and has to put a hand to the ground to stay up | has: arms
she flattens under the impact, legs splaying for a moment before they find the ground again | has: forelegs
she has no idea where it came from until the shadow slides off her

== aerial | moment: carried
the talons sink in and the ground drops away, and every wingbeat takes it further | has: legs
she kicks at nothing; her feet find only air | has: legs
the grip around her is the only solid thing in the world, and it is going to let go
she can feel each wingbeat above her as a lurch upward
the arena below wheels slowly, the trees small, the water a bright coin
for one long moment at the top of the climb neither of them moves at all

== aerial | moment: carried
the talons close on her and lock, and then her own weight is hanging from them | has: talons
hands clamp under her arms and the ground simply leaves | has: arms
the wingbeats come slow and enormous above her, each one a lurch that leaves her stomach behind
she twists to look down and wishes at once that she hadn't
the wind of their climb roars in her ears, and below them the arena shrinks to a map of itself
she claws at the grip on her and it only tightens
the higher they go the quieter it gets, until there is only the wingbeats and her own heart
then the grip opens, almost gently, and there is nothing at all holding her up

== aerial | moment: lift_fail
the wings thrash and the dust boils up, but her feet never leave the ground
she gets her grip and heaves, and the weight below simply does not come with her
for a moment the two of them strain against each other, one pulling up, one dragging down, and the ground wins
she braces and drops her weight, and the wings above her beat themselves out against it
the grip tears loose and the flyer lurches upward alone, suddenly much lighter

== aerial | moment: falling
she is still falling when it reaches her: there is no ground to brace on, no footing to roll from
it takes her in mid-air and spins her, and the sky and the ground swap places twice
she has her limbs out for the landing, and the blow comes from the side instead
the wind of her fall is still roaring in her ears when the strike arrives on top of it
there is nothing to do in the air but take it, and she takes it
she tumbles end over end from the blow, the ground somewhere below, closer every turn

== aerial | moment: spiked
the blow comes from above and adds itself to the fall, and the ground arrives far sooner than it should
she is driven down like a stake into the ground, and the ground gives first
the impact goes through her from the top down: the blow, then the earth, with nothing between
dust jumps up in a ring around her where she hits, and she does not bounce
for a moment the sound of it is louder than anything else in the arena, a single deep thud that the ground answers
she hits so hard her body leaves its shape in the ground beneath her
the flyer comes down with her the whole way and only pulls up at the very last instant, the impact hers alone
the world goes white at the edges, and when it comes back she is lying in a hollow she made

== aerial | moment: grounded
the hurt wing folds under her in mid-beat and the sky lets go of her
she tries to beat the bad wing and it only flutters, and she slides sideways out of the air | has: wings
the wing that has been carrying her on pain alone finally will not answer
she comes down in a long tilting spiral, the good wing hammering, the bad one trailing
the ground rises at her faster than she can sort out which way is up

== aerial | moment: knocked
the blow knocks her clean out of her wingbeat and she tumbles
she is spun round in the air, wings flailing for purchase that is not there
the hit stops her dead in the air, and for a moment she simply falls
she loses the air completely; the ground comes up in a rush of green and brown
feathers burst loose from where it struck her and drift down after her | has: feathers
she tries to catch herself with a wild beat of both wings and only half manages it | has: wings
the blow takes her out of the sky like a stone takes a bird off a branch
she is knocked sideways off her line, rolls in the air once, and comes down hard

== aerial | moment: strain
every downstroke drags at the hurt wing like a hook pulled through it
she flies lopsided, the bad wing beating short and late
she stays up on will alone, and every beat of it costs her
the wing trembles at the top of each stroke, as if it might simply stop
she holds the bad wing a little higher than the good one, favoring it even in the air
a feather tears loose from the hurt wing and spins away below her | has: feathers
her breath comes harder up here than it ever did on the ground
she knows she should come down; she does not

== movement | kind: fire
a breath drawn deep, and then the fire comes out of her like a door flung open
the flames roll across the gap and lick round her opponent from every side
the heat arrives before the fire does, a dry wall of it
a gout of flame, white at the heart and orange at the edges
the air shimmers and buckles between them as the fire crosses it
the fire spreads where it lands and clings, as if it were hungry
sparks scatter across the ground at the edges of the blast
the fire wraps round her opponent and for a moment there is only the glow inside it
her flame flares bright with the effort, sputtering high and white | has: flame
the heat on her own fur makes her eyes water, and she keeps pouring it out | has: forelegs

== movement | kind: ice
the air in front of her mouth turns white and the cold rolls out with it
frost creeps over the ground ahead of the beam, crackling as it spreads
the cold arrives with a sound like a pond freezing all at once
glittering crystals hang in the air behind the attack and drift slowly down
the beam is pale blue and so cold that it smokes
everything it touches turns white at the edges

== movement | kind: wind
her wings sweep forward together and the air comes with them like a wave | has: wings
the gust picks up leaves, grit and loose grass and throws all of it at once
the wind tears at her opponent's footing and pushes her back a step at a time
a spinning column of air whips up between them, full of debris
the blast of wind flattens the grass in a long fan in front of her
the air itself is the weapon: invisible until it lands, and then everywhere

== movement | kind: rock
stones lift from the ground around her and hang there for a heartbeat
the rocks come in a ragged volley, some high, some skipping off the ground
a jagged spike of stone erupts from the earth exactly where she means it to
she throws her head and the stones fly from her mane like shrapnel | has: mane
the rocks crack against each other in the air and come down in a hail of grit

== movement | kind: wing
she pivots on one foot and brings the wing across like a flail | has: wings
the wing comes down from above, the leading edge first, heavy as a branch | has: wings
both wings beat at once and she rises into the strike | has: wings
the wing is hard as steel along its edge, and she swings it like a blade | has: wings

== movement | kind: beak
she drives the beak in with the whole weight of her neck behind it | has: beak
the beak snaps shut an inch from where it was aimed, and the next one does not miss | has: beak
a quick double strike of the beak, faster than the eye can follow | has: beak

== movement | kind: talon
she comes in feet first, talons spread wide, wings braking hard above her | has: talons
the talons rake and catch and rake again | has: talons
she seizes with one foot and strikes with the other | has: talons
the grip of the talons closes like a trap and she leans back on it with her whole weight | has: talons

== movement | kind: punch
the fist comes round low and hooks up under her guard | has: arms
a short, chopping blow with the heel of the hand, the arm barely bending | has: arms
her clawed hand closes into a fist as it travels and lands like a hammer | has: arms

== reaction | size: solid, heavy
her feathers fluff out all over at once, then slowly settle | has: feathers
her crest snaps up rigid and stays there | has: crest
her wings jerk half open, as if to fly from it | has: wings
her beak gapes open on a sound that does not quite come out | has: beak
her tail flame flares high and flickers | has: flame
her tails lash out behind her, every one of them at once | has: tails
the rock spikes in her mane grate against each other as she flinches | has: mane

== reaction | size: tremendous
her wings fly open and she staggers, every feather on end | has: feathers
the flame on her tail gutters low and spits | has: flame
a scream tears out of her open beak, harsh and ragged | has: beak
her tails go rigid behind her, fanned out wide | has: tails

== pain_show | level: sore, hurting
she settles her ruffled feathers with an irritated shake | has: feathers
a sharp clack of her beak, more temper than pain | has: beak
her crest flattens briefly, then rises again | has: crest
she mantles her wings and refolds them, slower than before | has: wings
her tail flame flickers once, then burns steady | has: flame

== pain_show | level: very painful, excruciating
she screams, beak wide, the sound harsh and ripped | has: beak
the feathers over {part} stand out stiff and ragged, and she cannot make them lie down | has: feathers
her crest lies flat against her skull and stays there | has: crest
her wings sag at her sides, the tips trailing on the ground | has: wings
her tail flame shrinks to a sputtering, smoking flicker | has: flame
the tails bunch tight against her body as if to shield it | has: tails

== pain_show | level: devastated
her beak hangs open and she pants, too hurt even to scream | has: beak
her feathers hang lank and wrong, and she does not try to settle them | has: feathers
her tail flame is a weak, wavering glow; she curls her tail in to protect it | has: flame

== pain_show | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated | zone: arm
the wing hangs a little lower on that side, and she holds it half open, unable to fold it cleanly | has: wings
the wing beats out of rhythm with the other one, short and late | has: wings
she flinches every time the wing brushes against her side | has: wings

== tell | state: winded, tired
her beak hangs open as she pants | has: beak
her wings droop at the shoulders, the tips touching the ground | has: wings
her feathers stay ruffled; she has stopped bothering to settle them | has: feathers
her flame burns lower than it did, steady but smaller | has: flame
her crest has stopped rising: it lies half flat now | has: crest

== tell | state: spent
she can barely hold her wings off the ground | has: wings
her tail flame is a sickly, guttering flicker | has: flame
her beak is open and her head bobs with every breath | has: beak

== held_in | state: fresh, winded
a harsh shriek, cut off short | has: beak
a hiss through her open beak, feathers flattening | has: beak
a low, rumbling growl deep in her chest, smoke curling from her nostrils | has: flame

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE NEW FIGHTERS: their own way of watching, thinking and holding a sound in
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== watch | fighter: cygnet
she watches down her long neck with cool disdain, as if the other were something on her plumage
she arches her neck and watches, wings half raised, every feather on end

== watch | fighter: duchess
she watches with her head cocked, red-ringed eyes hard, her long tail flicking once
she bobs her head as she watches, sharp and judging

== watch | fighter: valor
she watches with her chest thrust out and her crest flared high, already looking for the next charge
she screams at the other one, a challenge, and waits to see if it is answered

== watch | fighter: swift
she hops from foot to foot as she watches, forked tail flicking, already itching to go
she cocks her head and studies the damage, quick and pleased

== watch | fighter: zephyr
she watches calmly, circling slowly, her long crest streaming in the breeze
she rides the air above, watching with patient eyes for the next opening

== watch | fighter: pyra
she watches through the shimmer of her own heat, flames rising higher as she sees the damage
her fiery crest flares as she watches, imperious, waiting

== watch | fighter: glacia
she watches in perfect stillness, breath steaming, the air around her growing colder
her long tail ripples behind her as she watches, serene and unreadable

== watch | fighter: ember
she watches from her perch, eyes locked on, embers sparking from her wingtips
she fidgets, bobbing, unable to keep still, waiting for the moment to dive again

== watch | fighter: cinder
she watches with her tail flame flaring and a grin full of small fangs, smoke curling from her nostrils
she flexes her claws as she watches, wings half spread, enjoying it

== watch | fighter: tidecrest
she watches anxiously, antennae drooping, half sorry for what she has done
she watches with her small wings fluttering, worried and determined at once

== watch | fighter: gloam
she watches wide-eyed, swaying, slime dripping from her antennae
her antennae reach toward the other as she watches, curious, sorry, and still ready

== watch | fighter: kitsune
she watches with a thin, satisfied smile, her nine tails fanning slowly behind her
her red eyes glitter as she watches; this is exactly how she planned it

== watch | fighter: kindle
she watches with her six tails puffed out and her ears forward, trembling but proud
she huffs a little puff of embers as she watches, trying to look fierce

== watch | fighter: sable
she watches with her huge ears swiveled forward, nose wrinkled, thinking
the tufts in her ears puff warm air as she watches, studying the wound

== watch | fighter: dusk
she watches with her head low and her mane bristling, lips curled back from her fangs
she stalks a slow half circle as she watches, rock spikes rattling softly

== watch | fighter: vixen
she watches with a sly grin, her mustache markings twitching, already planning the next cheap shot
she creeps a little closer as she watches, tail curling, eyes bright with mischief

== watch_thought | fighter: cygnet
*Ruffled. She has RUFFLED me.*
*Stay down where you belong.*

== watch_thought | fighter: duchess
*Know your place.*
*Again, then. Properly this time.*

== watch_thought | fighter: valor
*Is that all? Come on!*
*Never back off. Never.*

== watch_thought | fighter: swift
*Too slow! Way too slow!*
*In and out. Again. Again.*

== watch_thought | fighter: zephyr
*Patience. The wind will turn.*
*Wait for the gap. Then fall.*

== watch_thought | fighter: pyra
*Burn.*
*Nothing stands in the heat for long.*

== watch_thought | fighter: glacia
*Cold settles everything, in the end.*
*Be still. It is easier.*

== watch_thought | fighter: ember
*Faster. Hotter. Again.*
*She can't track me. She can't.*

== watch_thought | fighter: cinder
*Now THAT was a hit.*
*Come on, give me more than that!*

== watch_thought | fighter: tidecrest
*I'm sorry. But I can't stop now.*
*Please just stay down.*

== watch_thought | fighter: gloam
*Ow. That looked like it hurt.*
*Don't be mad at me, okay?*

== watch_thought | fighter: kitsune
*Exactly as I planned.*
*You'll remember this.*

== watch_thought | fighter: kindle
*I did it! I really did it!*
*Don't be scared. Don't be scared.*

== watch_thought | fighter: sable
*Interesting. That's her weak side.*
*Keep your distance. Think.*

== watch_thought | fighter: dusk
*Hold. Wait for the opening.*
*She's slowing. Go.*

== watch_thought | fighter: vixen
*Too easy.*
*Mine. All of it's mine.*

== held_in | fighter: cygnet
a furious hiss through her open bill, neck drawn back in an S
a harsh, trumpeting honk that cracks in the middle

== held_in | fighter: duchess
a strangled squawk, cut short
she clacks her beak hard and keeps the rest behind it

== held_in | fighter: valor
a roar of pain that turns into a war-scream
she screams in defiance instead, louder than the pain

== held_in | fighter: swift
a sharp, broken chirp she cannot keep in
a thin shriek, quickly bitten off

== held_in | fighter: zephyr
a ragged, gasping screech
she holds the cry in, beak clamped, and only a rasp escapes

== held_in | fighter: pyra
a ringing scream that makes her flames gutter
a hiss of steam and smoke instead of a sound

== held_in | fighter: glacia
a thin, cracking wail, like ice giving way
a sharp intake of breath that frosts in the air

== held_in | fighter: ember
a crackling shriek, sparks spitting from her feathers
she screams and the embers flare with it

== held_in | fighter: cinder
a roaring bellow that breaks into a hoarse growl
she clenches her fangs and growls, smoke pouring from her nostrils

== held_in | fighter: tidecrest
a low, mournful bellow
she whimpers, antennae drooping flat

== held_in | fighter: gloam
a long, bubbling wail
a wobbling gurgle of pain she can't stop

== held_in | fighter: kitsune
a high, broken yelp she resents at once
she snarls, thin and vicious, refusing to cry out

== held_in | fighter: kindle
a thin, whining yelp
a whimper she tries to turn into a growl

== held_in | fighter: sable
a squealing, tearful yelp
she sniffs hard and holds back the cry, ears flat

== held_in | fighter: dusk
a strangled snarl that breaks into a whine
she growls through bared fangs, holding her ground

== held_in | fighter: vixen
a high, indignant yelp
she yips, then catches herself and grins through it

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CRUSHED: a charge that drives her into something and keeps pushing (the receiver's side, sized to her condition)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== crushed
her head snaps back and cracks against it a moment after her back does
the breath is squeezed out of her in one long push she has no say in, spit flying with it
there is no room to fall and no room to breathe: she is the thing caught between two hard things
her ribs bow inward and she can feel each one take the weight
the bark, the stone, whatever it is, prints itself into her back while the weight grinds into her front
her arms come up to push and find nothing to push against but the weight itself | has: arms
her hind feet scrabble at the ground for purchase and only slide | has: legs
the heat of the charge is still on her front, pressed into her with the weight | kind: fire
the water of the charge is still pouring over her face while she is pinned there, in her nose and mouth | kind: water

== crushed | state: fresh, winded
her mouth opens on a squeal that has no air behind it, only the shape of one
she clamps her jaw and takes it, eyes squeezed shut, every muscle locked against the pressure
a choked, voiceless gasp, and she will not give it more than that
she twists against it once, hard, testing, and it gives her nothing

== crushed | state: tired, spent
the sound that comes out of her is high and broken, all the air she had left
she sags into it, too spent to brace, and the weight does what it likes with her
her eyes roll and water; for a moment she can only hang there between the two
a thin whine leaks out of her with every bit of breath the weight lets her have

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# BUILD 133: MORE HIT AND DAMAGE REACTIONS
# Covered pain (a strong fighter badly hurt in one place) is shown in state: fresh/winded at high levels; the raw
# ones are for the worn down (state: tired/spent).
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== reaction | size: glancing
she barely checks her stride; her eyes flick to the spot and away
a small grunt, more surprise than hurt
she shrugs the shoulder nearest it, as if settling a strap
the skin there twitches once and is still
she blinks it off and is already moving again

== reaction | size: solid
the breath goes out of her in a short, flat huff
she rocks back a half step and plants herself again | has: legs
her whole body stiffens round the spot for a heartbeat
a sharp intake of breath through her nose, held, let out slowly
her head turns away from it on its own before she drags it back
she grunts and shifts her weight off that side
her eyes squeeze shut for an instant and snap open again

== reaction | size: heavy
the blow knocks a sound out of her she didn't choose to make
she folds a little round it, and has to make herself straighten
her legs go loose for a moment and she has to lock them | has: legs
she staggers, catches it, and the catching hurts too | has: legs
the air goes out of her all at once and comes back in a ragged pull
her mouth falls open on a gasp with no sound in it
for a moment she simply stands there and lets it land on her

== reaction | size: tremendous
the force of it lifts her and drops her an arm's length away
her whole body jerks, head to tail, as if she had been struck by lightning
she sags round it, and the next breath does not come when she calls it
the world goes quiet for a moment, as if someone had shut a door on it
her eyes go wide and white, and for a moment she does not seem to be in there at all

== pain_show | level: hurting
she keeps the hurt side turned a little away from her opponent
every few breaths her hand drifts toward the spot and she stops it | has: arms
she tests it with a small movement and does not like the answer
she breathes through her nose, slow and deliberate, the way you breathe through a cramp
she moves a fraction more carefully now, as if carrying something that might spill

== pain_show | level: very painful
a low sound slips out of her and she bites the rest of it off
her eyes water and she will not blink them clear in front of her opponent
she holds the hurt part close and stiff, guarding it with her whole body
the pain makes her clumsy for a moment; her next step is shorter than she meant
her breath comes in short pulls, each one stopping where the hurt begins

== pain_show | level: excruciating, devastated | state: fresh, winded
she is clearly hurting badly, and just as clearly refusing to show how badly
her face locks into a hard, flat look while her eyes water anyway
she cries out once, sharp, and swallows the rest of it with her jaw clamped
she keeps the part tucked in close and turns her other side to her opponent, guarding it with everything she has
her breathing goes careful and shallow; she does not trust a deep breath near that place
a shudder runs through her and she stamps it out by setting her stance
she bares her teeth at the pain as if it were another opponent | has: forelegs
she bares her teeth at the pain as if it were another opponent | has: arms

== pain_show | level: excruciating, devastated | state: tired, spent
the sound that comes out of her is raw and she cannot stop it
she curls round the hurt part and for a moment does not move at all
her whole body shakes, a fine, constant tremor she has no control over
she makes a broken, keening sound on every breath out
tears run and she does not even try to hide them now

== pain_show | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated | zone: leg
she takes the weight off that leg and leaves it just touching the ground | has: legs
her stride hitches every time that leg takes her weight | has: legs
she limps now, and is angry about it, which makes it worse | has: legs

== pain_show | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated | zone: arm
she holds that arm tucked against her body | has: arms
she keeps the foreleg lifted a little, the paw curled, unwilling to put it down | has: forelegs
she uses the other side for everything now, and anyone watching can see it

== pain_show | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated | zone: chest, belly
she breathes high and shallow, not letting her ribs move more than they must
she hunches slightly round her middle, as if against cold
every breath stops short of the full one she needs

== pain_show | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated | zone: head, neck
she shakes her head as if to clear water from her ears, and winces at the shake
she holds her head very still, as if it might spill
she swallows carefully, and the swallow costs her

== held_in | state: fresh, winded
a grunt through closed teeth, and nothing more
her breath hisses in, sharp, and is let out slow and controlled
a short, hard exhale through her nose, and her jaw sets
a small sound, bitten off before it is fully out

== held_in | state: tired
a cry she only half catches, ragged at the end
a groan that comes up from somewhere deep and does not stop at once
she whimpers before she can stop herself, and hates it

== held_in | state: spent
a sob of pain, raw, unguarded
a long, wavering cry that breaks in the middle
she does not even try to keep it in any more

# ===============================================================================================================
# TYPE MATCHUPS (build 148): how an attack's type meets her body. effect: super / weak / none; type: the attack's
# type; vs: her own type(s). effect_know / effect_body: the one hit; effect_see: the one who threw it.
# ===============================================================================================================
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EFFECT_KNOW: the one hit, her own awareness of the matchup. Inner knowledge, instinct, memory of training and
# old fights, dread when it works on her, contempt when it cannot. Ideas for a thought or a realisation in her body.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== effect_know | effect: super
she has always known this is the kind of blow her body cannot answer
the old dread wakes, the one she carries out of every fight against this
her body flinches before her mind does, as if it remembered first
there is nothing in her built to turn this aside, and she knows it
all that training was about never letting exactly this land, and it just did
a cold certainty that this one will cost far more than it should
she knows the shape of this hurt from long ago, and it has not changed
her instinct told her to get clear and she was half a step slow
it slips past everything she relies on, like water under a door
she feels it go where her strength does not reach
an old fight comes back to her, the one she lost to exactly this
the knowledge sits low in her belly: a few more of these is too many
whatever toughness she has is for other blows, not this one
the fear she keeps folded small unfolds all at once
she can feel it working on her from within, unhurried and thorough
the worst thing she could have been hit with, and she knows precisely why
her whole nature is wrong for this, and the other has found it
she had hoped her opponent would not think of this; that hope is gone
a small voice that has always been there says this is how she loses
she knows from experience that this hurt will not stay where it landed
the urge to hide the weakness, too late now that it has been found
a sick recognition, like hearing a lock turn to the right key
she remembers being warned about this, and how lightly she took the warning
her body knows its one soft door, and something has just walked through it
a flash of anger at her own make: why this, of all things

== effect_know | effect: weak
she knows before it lands that it will not amount to much
her body was made for this, and it shrugs almost bored
contempt, cool and quiet: is that really all the other has
the old confidence of a body that has taken this a hundred times
she almost wants to tell her opponent to try something else
the kind of blow she was born half-armoured against
a flicker of amusement at being hit with the one thing she does not fear
she remembers whole days of training with this thrown at her, and how little it did
her instinct barely bothers to brace for it
she knows it will sting and fade, and it does
there is a comfort in it: the world still works the way she knows it does
she feels it spend itself against her and finds she is almost smiling
her body reads it, files it as harmless, and moves on
she knows her opponent has chosen badly, and she means to make that cost
a quiet pride in what her body is built to bear
the fear she braced for never comes, only a dull knock
she lets it land on purpose, to show how little it means
her nature turns it aside before she has to think about it
a surge of nerve: if this is the other's best, the fight is hers
she knows the feel of this blow and knows it has no teeth for her
an old lesson pays off: let that kind of hit come, it cannot reach far
she was ready to be hurt and is faintly insulted not to be
like being struck with the very stuff she grew up in
if the other had anything better, she thinks, it would have come by now
a slow, satisfied breath: the matchup is hers and her body says so

== effect_know | effect: none
it simply cannot touch her, and she knows it before it arrives
she does not even brace; nothing in it reaches her
a long-held certainty that this kind of thing passes through her like air
she watches it come and go with a stillness that is almost rude
her body has no place in it for this to land
a strange calm in being untouchable to something meant to hurt
she feels nothing at all, and lets her opponent see that she feels nothing
she has known since she was small that this cannot lay a finger on her

# effect_know by the attack's type
== effect_know | effect: super | type: electric
she has always hated storms, and now one is running through her
the memory of a thunderhead overhead, long ago, and how small she felt under it
she knows the jolt will find every wet place in her, and she is never dry

== effect_know | effect: super | type: fire
she knows heat is the one thing her body cannot hold off
an old fear of fire, older than any training she ever had
she has always kept her distance from flame, and now it has found her

== effect_know | effect: super | type: water
she has always known the flood would find her out one day
a dread of deep water that she has carried since she was small
her body cannot stand a drenching like this, and it never could

== effect_know | effect: super | type: grass
she knows the green clinging stuff takes far more than it gives
her body has always lost to roots and vines, and it remembers losing
the drinking pull of it is something she has felt before and hated

== effect_know | effect: super | type: ice
she has always hated the cold, and now it is inside her wing | has: wings
her body was never made to keep warm against this, and she knows it
the cold she has always run from has finally caught up with her
she remembers a winter that nearly stopped her, and this is that winter again

== effect_know | effect: super | type: fighting
against a trained fighter's blow her body has always folded, and it folds now
there is no trick in her that turns a strike this honest
she remembers sparring with fighters and always coming away sore for days

== effect_know | effect: super | type: flying
she has always feared what comes down out of the sky
there is no reaching up to something that strikes from the air, and she knows it
her strength is all rooted to the ground, and the ground is no help against this

== effect_know | effect: super | type: psychic
she knows strength is no use against a thing that reaches her mind
the old fear of being moved by something she cannot see or grip
she has never learned to guard her own thoughts, only her body

== effect_know | effect: super | type: rock
she knows plain heavy stone is what brings her kind down
she remembers a rockfall from long ago, and the ache it left for weeks
the hard simple weight of rock has always been her undoing

== effect_know | effect: super | type: ground
she knows the earth is the one thing she can never get away from
she has always trusted the ground, and now it has turned on her
there is nowhere to stand against a thing that is the floor itself

== effect_know | effect: super | type: ghost
she knows the cold from the other side is the one thing she cannot stare down
the fear of the dark she was born with comes back up all at once
she has never known how to fight what she cannot properly touch

== effect_know | effect: super | type: dark
she knows a mind like hers is easiest hurt by low, sly tricks
the old lesson comes back: whatever lives in the shadows will catch her out
she never could read a fighter who fights dirty, and she still cannot

== effect_know | effect: super | type: dragon
she knows the old power when it hits her, and it knows her too
a deep fear of a force older and wilder than anything she was taught to meet
her body recognises this weight and shrinks from it on instinct

== effect_know | effect: super | type: fairy
she knows that soft, bright stuff is the one thing her strength cannot beat
it makes no sense that something so gentle can hurt so much, and yet she always knew
an old wariness of the sparkling ones, proved right in a single touch

== effect_know | effect: super | type: steel
she knows hard metal will always be harder than she is
she remembers how metal once rang through her, and her body remembers too
the cold edge of steel is the one thing her body cannot refuse

== effect_know | effect: super | type: poison
a sickness like this is the one fight her body always loses
the sting she always feared most, more than any claw or fang
she feels the slow work begin and knows exactly where it ends

== effect_know | effect: super | type: bug
she knows the small, many stings have always found her out
it is beneath her, and yet she knows it bites her worse than most
an old itch of hurt from swarms long ago, back again under her skin

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: fire
she knows fire; she has lived wrapped in heat all her life
heat is home to her, and this is only a little more of it
she almost welcomes the warmth, and knows the other can see that

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: water
water is where she is strongest, and this is only more of it
she knows the feel of a soaking and has never once feared it
she has been wetter than this every day of her life

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: electric
she knows the jolt will not find a way through a body like hers
the charge feels small against her, and she knows it will stay small
she has stood out in storms before and stood up after

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: grass
she knows green things wither against her, and they always have
the clinging stems hold no fear for her at all
she has never been held for long by leaves, and she will not be now

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: ice
she knows cold, and cold knows better than to stay on her
the chill is old and almost friendly to her
frost cannot settle on a body like hers, and she does not even shiver

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: fighting
she knows a trained blow cannot find its true mark in her
she has always slipped the punches of fighters, and this one is no different
a strike like this was never going to be enough for a body like hers

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: normal
plain force has never troubled her, and this is very plain force
she knows a simple blow cannot get past what she is made of
she lets the ordinary thing come, knowing it is ordinary

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: flying
she knows the swooping strikes break on her like wind on a wall
the rush of air has never moved her, and she plants herself against it
she has stood against wind all her life and it has never won

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: psychic
her mind is a shut door to this kind of pressure, and she knows it
she feels the push coming and knows exactly where to put it
there is nothing in her thoughts for it to pull on

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: rock
she knows stone, and she knows how little it does to her
the stones have always fallen short against her, and she expects no different
she was made to take the earth's hard knocks without a murmur

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: steel
hard meets harder, and she knows which of them she is
the metal blow is nothing her body has not met and outlasted before
she knows the edge will skid off her before it ever lands

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: dark
she knows the sly ones' tricks and is never surprised by them
the low blows have no secrets from her; she has thrown them herself
she shrugs at the shadow, knowing it far too well to fear it

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: dragon
even old, wild power has to bend against what she is made of
she has never feared that great force, and she knows why
she knows the weight will break on her, and she waits for it to break

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: fairy
the bright stuff tickles more than it hurts, and she knows it will
she has always found the sparkle faintly ridiculous
she knows the glow will fade off her quicker than it came

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: ghost
she knows the dark and has never been afraid of what lives in it
the chill of the other side is old company to her
she has never been haunted by anything, and she will not start now

== effect_know | effect: weak | type: poison
her body knows poison and turns it away without asking her
she has always been too tough for a sting to sicken
she knows the toxin will lose itself somewhere in her and go quiet

== effect_know | effect: none | type: ghost
she knows nothing from the other side can lay a hand on a body as plain as hers
the cold from beyond goes through her, as it always has, and leaves nothing

== effect_know | effect: none | type: poison
she knows the poison has nowhere to soak into her hard body
her body has always been beyond sickness, and she does not even wait to see

== effect_know | effect: none | type: psychic
her mind is wrapped in a dark that pressure cannot see into
she knows her thoughts are hidden from this, and always have been

# EFFECT_BODY: the one hit, what this element does to this kind of body. How it gets in, how it does its work,
# how the body answers: super bites deeper than its size, spreads and lingers; weak spends itself and is shed;
# none passes off or through. {part} is the part that was hit.

== effect_body | effect: super
it bites deeper into her {part} than its size has any right to
the hurt spreads out from her {part} instead of staying where it landed
her {part} has nothing to shed it with; it soaks in and stays
it lingers in her {part} long after the blow itself has passed
the sting does not fade but settles in, and then it grows
it finds the soft seam in her and works its way along it
her {part} goes heavy and wrong, as if it belongs to someone else
her body tries to throw it off and cannot find a grip on it
a small blow by any measure, and her {part} answers as if it were enormous
it gets under her guard and under her skin in the same instant
her {part} trembles with it, a fine shiver that will not settle
the shock travels the whole length of her before it is done
her {part} throbs in a rhythm that is not her heartbeat
it sinks in where other blows bounce, and her body has no second wall behind the first
the strength drains out of her {part} as if poured from a jug
the hurt in her {part} doubles back on itself, sharper the second time round
her muscles lock around it instead of letting it go
it does not land so much as arrive and move in
a thin, wrong feeling spreads from her {part} into her chest
her {part} burns and aches at once, two hurts from one touch
her breath catches and stays caught while the blow keeps working
she cannot shake it out; every shake seems to drive it further in
it wakes a weakness in her body she had nearly forgotten was there
the ache keeps climbing after the blow is over, as if it had only just started
it works on her like rust works on iron, quietly and everywhere at once

== effect_body | effect: weak
it spends itself on her {part} and leaves little more than a warm patch
her {part} takes it the way a stone takes rain
it glances off her and loses itself in the air
her body drinks it in and is hardly changed for it
it beads off her skin and is gone
the force spreads thin across her {part} and fades to a dull pat
her {part} stings for a breath, then forgets
it finds nothing on her to hold on to and slides away
her body sheds it the way a shaken coat sheds rain
a knock, a tingle in her {part}, and it is over
the blow sounds a great deal worse than it feels
it scatters against her like a handful of thrown sand
her {part} absorbs it and gives back nothing but a shrug
only the surface of her {part} notices it; underneath, nothing at all
half its force is gone before it ever reaches her
her {part} warms, tingles, and settles back to itself
her body was built around this kind of hurt and simply carries it
a sting like a nettle's, sharp and shallow and quickly gone
it loses its bite somewhere between the other and her skin
the shock thins out and runs off her without catching
her {part} rocks with it and comes back as it was
a faint ache, the kind she would never bother to mention
the blow goes soft at the last moment against her
she feels the push of it far more than the hurt
her {part} hardly marks; a flush of warmth, and gone

== effect_body | effect: none
it passes through her as if she were not there
not a mark on her {part}; it meets nothing to take hold of
it washes off her without the faintest whisper of hurt
her body is simply not the kind of thing it can touch
it slides around her like water round a post
a stir of air against her {part}, and nothing more
it reaches her and finds nowhere at all to go

== effect_body | effect: super | type: ghost

== effect_body | effect: super | type: dark

it hurts the way being caught out hurts, sharp and shaming
her focus scatters and her {part} goes with it

== effect_body | effect: super | type: dragon
it lands with an old, deep weight her body has no smaller answer for

the heat and the weight of it come together and she cannot pull them apart

# effect_body for the sharpest pairs: super
== effect_body | effect: super | type: water | vs: fire
the water hits her and her inner flame gutters, a hiss of steam all round her | has: flame
steam rises off her in a cloud as her heat fights to stay lit
the warmth she lives on is dragged out of her in one heavy wash
she can feel the fire in her shrink back from it, small and frightened
her coat steams and the heat in her chest goes thin and weak
the flame on her body sputters and sinks low, and it is slow to rise again | has: flame

== effect_body | effect: super | type: electric | vs: water
the current races through her wet coat and lights up all of her at once
her damp skin carries the jolt everywhere it is wet, which is everywhere
the water on her becomes the road the charge takes, end to end
her fins go rigid as the charge runs out along them | has: fins
she lives wet, and the wet carries every spark of it into her
the shock spreads through the slick of her like ripples through a pond

== effect_body | effect: super | type: electric | vs: flying
the bolt finds her up high with nothing below to carry it away
the charge runs out along her wings and the muscles lock mid-beat | has: wings
her feathers stand up and crackle and the wingbeat falters | has: feathers
there is no ground under her to take the shock off her
her wings stutter and she drops a body's length before they answer | has: wings
the current pours down the length of her and has nowhere to leave

== effect_body | effect: super | type: ice | vs: flying
the cold gets into the wing joints the way it never gets into fur | has: wings
frost rimes her feathers and they stiffen and will not lie flat | has: feathers
the flight muscles across her chest go slow and heavy with chill
her wingbeat thickens, every stroke harder than the last | has: wings
ice clings along the edge of her wing and the air will not hold her right | has: wings

== effect_body | effect: super | type: ice | vs: dragon
the cold soaks into her and her body has no way to make heat to fight it
everything in her runs slow and sluggish, her fire damped low
her {part} stiffens fast, the cold reaching further than it should
she goes still and stiff, the warmth she runs on leaking away
the chill settles in her chest and her breath comes short and shallow

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fire | vs: ice
the heat bites into her and her cold body softens at the edges
frost on her steams away and the skin beneath it smarts
she feels herself go slack and wet where the heat landed
her chill is no shield; it only makes the heat feel worse

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fire | vs: steel
the heat soaks into the metal of her and does not leave
her steel warms, then hurts, and keeps holding the heat
the hard parts of her that turn blows aside only drink the fire in
the heat spreads out from her {part} and stays trapped there

== effect_body | effect: super | type: rock | vs: flying
the stone clips her and the air will not hold her any more
the weight of it drags her out of the sky and down hard
her wing folds under the blow and her lift goes with it | has: wings
the rock knocks her out of the air she trusted

== effect_body | effect: super | type: rock | vs: fire
the stone smothers her heat where it lands
the rock does not burn and does not care, and her flame has no answer for it | has: flame
the weight of earth presses the fire in her low and close
grit and stone choke her heat and her breath together

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fighting | vs: normal
the blow is trained and her body is not, and it lands square
nothing about her is built to turn a strike placed this well
it finds her plain, honest body and drives straight through
she takes it full, with no trick or toughness to slip it

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fighting | vs: steel
the strike lands on her steel and the shock rings all the way through
her hard body has no give, and so it takes the whole of the force
the metal of her shudders, humming long after the blow
the hardness she trusts becomes a bell for the blow to ring

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fighting | vs: rock
the blow finds the seam in her stony body and the shock spreads from it
her hardness has no give, and the jarring goes deep
the strike smacks into her stone and she feels it right through
she is solid, and solid things take a well-placed blow worst

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fighting | vs: dark
the honest strike finds her where her tricks cannot hide her
she was ready for a feint, and a plain hard blow takes her square
her slyness counts for nothing against a strike this direct
the clean blow lands and there is no shadow for her to slip into

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fairy | vs: dragon
the bright touch unpicks the old strength in her like a knot pulled loose
her great power drains under the glow as if it had never been hers
something light and gentle hurts her more than any fang ever has
the shimmer sinks into her and her might goes thin

== effect_body | effect: super | type: fairy | vs: dark
the light finds her in her own shadow and stings there
her dark nature curdles under the brightness
it shines straight into her, and she has nowhere dim left to hide
the glow stings across the whole of her at once

== effect_body | effect: super | type: dragon | vs: dragon
a force like her own crashes into her and finds every weak place she knows she has
her body knows its own kind and has no defence against it
old power meets old power, and hers gives first
the rumble of it is in her own chest, and so is the hurt

== effect_body | effect: super | type: steel | vs: ice

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EFFECT_SEE: the attacker's side. She sees it working on her opponent and knows she has the right tool (super),
# sees it fall short and knows the matchup is against her (weak), or sees it pass off without a mark (none).
# The opponent is "her opponent" or "the other"; has: tags here would test the ATTACKER, so the opponent's body
# is kept in neutral words.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== effect_see | effect: super
she watches it land and sees her opponent take it badly, worse than it should
she knows she has the right tool now, and she means to keep using it
the other flinches harder than the blow deserves, and she notes it
a quiet satisfaction: this is the key that fits that lock
she sees the hurt spread through her opponent and does not let up
the reaction tells her everything: the other has no answer for this
she files it away: whatever else happens, this works
she feels her opponent give under it in a way she has not before
the matchup leans her way and she can feel it in every hit
she sees the other's guard falter, not from the force but from what it is
a hunter's calm settles on her; she has found the soft place
she watches the other try to shake it off, and fail
she can tell from the other's face that this one went deep
her opponent seems to shrink from it, and she presses forward
she can feel it already: once this starts working, it keeps working
she lets herself feel it for a moment: the fight just tilted her way
there is no need to be clever now, only to keep doing this
the other's breathing changes after it lands, shorter and warier
she sees her opponent glance down at where it hit, worried
she knows the other knows it too, and that is half the fight won
a small, fierce joy at a blow that does exactly what it should
her opponent's footing goes uncertain, and she sees why
she reads the shiver that runs through the other and allows herself a smile
the right blow at last, after all that hard work to get here
the other's nerve slips a little, and she can see it slip

== effect_see | effect: weak
she sees it land and sees it do almost nothing
the other barely blinks, and she knows the matchup is against her
a sinking feeling: this is the wrong tool for this opponent
she watches it spend itself on the other and fade
she knows she will have to find another way in
the other's body takes it as if it were made to
frustration tightens her jaw; that should have hurt far more
she notes the lack of any reaction and starts to think
the hit is clean and it does not matter, and she hates that
she sees the other almost smile, and she knows why
the other has a body built for exactly this, and it shows
her opponent shrugs it off in plain sight, to make the point
she threw her best and it bounced
she feels the fight lean away from her, just a little
she needs something else, and she does not have much else
like pushing on a door that opens the other way
she sees the force go out of the blow before it lands
a cold realisation that this kind of hit will never be enough here
she watches the other drink it in and wonders what she was thinking
the other does not even turn toward it
she sets her teeth: she will have to win this the long way round
she is already adjusting; if this does not work, something closer might
the matchup is wrong, she knows it, and she knows the other knows
the impact looked good and meant very little
her opponent seems to soak it up and wait for more

== effect_see | effect: none
she watches it go clean through the other and leave no mark behind
nothing: not a flinch, not a sound, and she understands why
this was never going to reach a body like her opponent's, and she sees that now
it goes off the other like light off glass
she has wasted the breath and knows it at once
a jolt of disbelief, then cold understanding
the other stands there untouched, and she must think again, fast
she sees it meet nothing at all and fall away

# effect_see by the attack's type
== effect_see | effect: super | type: electric
she sees the other's muscles lock as the charge runs through, and she knows she has her
the other jerks and twitches, and she feels the rightness of the shock
she sees the current find a road through her opponent and race along it

== effect_see | effect: super | type: fire
she sees the heat sink into the other instead of skimming off
the other shies from the flame, and she knows exactly why
she watches the burn take and spread, and feeds it more

== effect_see | effect: super | type: water

the other cannot reach her up there, and every dive lands

she watches her opponent's strength fail to matter at all

== effect_see | effect: super | type: water | vs: fire

== watch | posture: down, pinned
she watches the head come up off the ground and sink back, and counts how long it stayed up
she sees the effort it takes just to turn over, and how much of it goes nowhere
she watches the hurt {part} pressed into the ground as if hiding it there
she notices the breath fogging the dust in front of the other's mouth, short and uneven
she sees the body below her try the same movement twice and fail both times the same way
she watches for the gathering of strength before a try, and sees how long it now takes

she notes how the other's eyes keep going to the {part}, as if checking it is still there
she watches the eyelids flutter and keeps very still, so as not to wake anything
she sees the shiver start under her and feels it arrive in her own body a heartbeat later | posture: pinned
she watches the struggles come further apart and each one smaller than the one before | posture: pinned
she sees the other turn her face away, and reads that more clearly than any cry
she watches the dust settle on the other's side and nobody brush it off
she sees the ears flick back toward her and flatten again | has: ears
she watches a twin tail lift a hand's width and drop | has: tails
she sees a wing half open against the ground, try to push, and fold again | has: wings
she watches the coils tighten and loosen on nothing, restless and weak | has: coils
she notices the breath catch at the top every time, right where the {part} begins
she sees the grit stuck along the other's cheek and that it has not been wiped away

== watch | posture: standing
she watches the other's stance widen, the way it does when the ground starts to feel unsteady
she sees the hurt {part} held a fraction away from the body, guarded without thinking
she watches the head shake once, hard, and sees that it did not help
she marks the half step back, the one the other probably did not mean to take
she sees the other glance at her own {part} and drag her eyes back up too late
she watches the shoulders rise and fall, faster now, the breath coming from the top of the chest
she notices the other is breathing through her mouth, and what that means
she sees the weight rock forward and back, looking for somewhere comfortable to settle and not finding it
she watches the guard come up a little lower than before, a little slower
she watches the other try to hide a limp by standing still, and sees through it
she sees the knees lock, the way legs lock when they cannot be trusted bent | has: legs

she watches the eyes blink hard, twice, clearing something that will not clear
she sees the muscles along the {part} twitching in small hard knots

she sees the tail held low and still, a weight now rather than a balance | has: tail
she watches the crest lie flat and stay flat | has: crest
she sees the other's flame gutter and climb back, thinner | has: flame
she watches the wings held half open for balance, trembling at the edges | has: wings
she sees the hood fold a little narrower than it was | has: hood

== watch | state: spent, tired
she watches the other sway and correct, sway and correct, a little later each time
the other's eyes are taking longer to follow her; she moves once just to be sure
she notices that the snarl is gone, and only the breathing is left
she watches the sweat darken along the other's sides and the shaking in the {part}
she sees the other's head hang, then lift, then hang a little lower

she sees that the other no longer reacts when she shifts her weight, and wonders if it is a trick
the other has stopped hiding anything; she can read every hurt at once
she watches the {part} give a little under the other's own weight, then hold

== watch | state: fresh, winded
she watches the other shake the blow off almost at once, and adjusts her plans
she sees the anger come into the other's eyes and knows the next exchange will be faster
she notes how quickly the other's guard came back, and does not like it
she watches the {part} flex once, testing, and sees that it still answers well enough
she sees the other's breath steady inside two heartbeats and respects it, a little

== watch
she watches without moving, so as not to miss the small things
she studies the {part} the way she might study a lock: where it is weak, how it will give
she watches the other's mouth, the tight line it has become
she notices the smell of the other has changed, sharper, with fear or effort in it
every flinch she sees she adds to a slow, careful tally
she sees the other glance at the ground, measuring a fall that has not come yet
she keeps her eyes on the {part}, where the muscles are still settling around the hurt
she watches the other's breathing and matches her own to it without meaning to
the other's eyes find hers and drop, and she takes note of who looked away
she watches the dust drift off the other's body, slow, in the still air
she sees the other's whole body jerk at a sound from somewhere else, and files it away
she watches the other's colour, the flush coming and going round the hurt
she studies the shake, how it starts small at the {part} and travels
she sees the other lick dry lips and swallow, and hears it
she watches the other's head turn to keep her in view, slower than before

she watches for the moment the other forgets the hurt and moves normally; it does not come

she watches the mane hang damp and flattened along the other's neck | has: mane

she does not trust it, not yet; she has seen her come back before in this fight

== dwell | zone: chest

a deep, heavy throb in her {part}, slow as a drum, and her stomach rolls with each beat

== dwell | zone: head

== dwell | zone: neck

== dwell | zone: arm
the {part} has gone strangely light and far away, then all at once heavy and full of ache

the hurt in her {part} has found the joint and settled in, so that any bend at all bites

== dwell | zone: leg

== dwell | zone: back
the hurt sits right where she cannot reach or see it, and that makes it worse

any twist at all wakes the {part}, and so she has stopped twisting
a slow ache runs out from her {part} along her ribs, wrapping round to the front

== dwell | zone: tail
the hurt in her {part} drags at her balance; she keeps tipping toward it
the {part} throbs, a slow heavy beat that she can feel all the way up her spine
she holds her {part} carefully off the ground, and holding it up is its own ache

== dwell
she cannot tell anymore where the {part} ends and the hurt begins
the ache fills the {part} slowly from the middle out, like water filling a cup
a wave of heat goes over her and leaves her shivering
her eyes water on their own, not from crying, from the plain size of it
everything around the {part} has gone tense and watchful, braced for more
the hurt pulses, steadies, then pulses again harder, as if it were catching its breath too

a faint sick lightness creeps up from the {part}, making the edges of things waver
she notices she is holding perfectly still, and that she does not dare stop
she feels small and slow and very aware of her own body, all at once
the {part} keeps sending up little jolts long after the blow, each one a surprise
her muscles have gone tight in places nowhere near the {part}, all bracing for it
a sting runs over her skin round the {part}, like a burn under cold water
she can feel every heartbeat arrive in the {part} and spread outward from it
she shifts once to ease it, and the shift makes it worse, and she goes still again

she has started trembling in a slow, deep way that comes from the middle of her | state: tired, spent
the ache is heavy now, a weight laid over the whole of her, not just the {part} | state: tired, spent

== dwell_breath | state: fresh, winded
she breathes out hard through her nose, twice, as if she could push the hurt out with it
her breath stutters once on the way in and then she has it again, deliberately slow
she holds the next breath a moment at the top, waiting to see whether it will hurt, and it does
she makes herself breathe low and steady, though her body wants quick little gulps
a sharp hissed breath in, and the long one after it shakes a little
her breathing goes quick and loud for a few beats before she wrestles it level
she lets one breath go in a hiss and the next comes easier
she breathes through it in short counted pulls, the way she would through a stitch
she swallows air, steadies, and breathes out slow enough to hide the shake

== dwell_breath | state: tired, spent
each breath is a small climb, and she rests at the top of every one
she breathes with her whole body now, sides pumping, and still cannot get enough
her breath whistles faintly on the way out and she cannot make it stop
she gasps, holds it, lets it go in a long ragged sigh that leaves her emptier than before
the breaths come uneven, two quick, one long, then a pause that frightens her
there is a wet rasp at the bottom of each breath, and she coughs to clear it, and it costs her
she pants with her mouth open, too tired to care how it sounds
she cannot slow her breathing; it has its own pace now, fast and shallow and hers no longer
every breath out comes with a faint sound attached, small and helpless
she breathes in sips, not daring a full one, and the sips are not enough
her breathing hitches and catches like a sob, though she is not crying
she drags the air in as if through cloth, slow and heavy and never quite full

== read
she watches the other's eyes go to the place they just hit, a quick proud glance, and hates it
she notes that the other is breathing through her nose again, recovered, unhurried
she sees the other roll a shoulder as if loosening it, and wonders if it is hurt or showing off
the other has stopped circling and simply waits, and she reads the confidence in that
she sees the other's weight settle back on the heels, easy, a sign of someone in no hurry
she watches the other's mouth, the small set smile at one corner, and remembers it
she notices the other blinking a little more than before; not much, but something
she sees a tremor in the other's stance after the effort, there for a moment and hidden
she watches the way the other turns to keep her in view, and sees it is slower on one side
the other's guard has relaxed by a fraction, and she wonders if that is a mistake or bait
she sees the other glance away for half a heartbeat and marks it like a gap in a fence
she notes the dust on the other's body, and where it is, and what that says about the last exchange
she watches the other shake out a limb that must have jarred on the blow
she listens to the other's breathing and finds a small catch in it, like a stitch
she sees the other's eyes are fixed, intent, already thinking two moves ahead
she reads the stillness as a choice, and the choice as patience, and patience as danger
the other is careful where she steps now, and she wonders what she is careful of
she sees the other's chest rise high and fall slow, someone settling herself on purpose
she watches the other's crest lift, proud and bristling | has: crest
she sees the flame on the other burning bright and steady, unbothered | has: flame
she watches the horn lower a degree, already lining up | has: horn
she notes the other's ears pricked straight at her, listening for every breath | has: ears
she watches the hood flare and settle, flare and settle, measuring her | has: hood
she watches the other's mane lift in the air of her own breathing, damp and dark | has: mane

== read | state: spent, tired, hurt
she sees the other is tired too, the sides heaving, and that is the only good news she has
she notices a limp in the other she had not seen before, and clings to it
she watches the other wipe her mouth, slow, and understands the other is hurting somewhere too
the other's stance has widened, the way a tired fighter's does, and she takes heart from it
she sees sweat darkening along the other's sides and is fiercely glad of it
she can only hold the other in focus for a moment at a time, and spends every moment on the face

== read_thought
*She wants me to look at her eyes. Watch the shoulders.*
*She's pleased with herself. That's when they slip.*
*She hits harder from that side. Stay off it.*
*She waited for me to breathe. Clever.*
*So that's how she does it.*
*She's not even looking at my good side.*
*She thinks she knows where I'm weak. She's half right.*
*She always resets her stance first.*
*She hesitated there. Why?*
*Too pleased. Much too pleased.*
*She's patient. I hate patient.*
*I know that look. She's going to go again.*
*Wait for her to commit.*
*She's watching my hurt side. Use it.*

== read_thought | state: fresh, winded
*Fine. She's good. I'm better.*
*That was a lucky angle. Don't give her another.*
*Same trick twice? Go on, then.*
*She thinks she's ahead. One hit. One.*
*Stop admiring her and move.*
*She's quick, but she's loud about it.*
*Watch her, learn her, then hurt her.*

== read_thought | state: tired, spent
*There. A catch in her breath. I heard it.*
*She's not as fresh as she's pretending.*
*Watch her. Just watch. Don't move yet.*
*Still standing. She noticed.*
*She's careful now. She's worried about something.*
*She won't get that close again for free.*
*If I can just see her clearly.*
*She's looking at me like it's finished.*
*Make her wait. Make her doubt.*

# SELF_LOOK: more of the hurt one looking at her own body ({part} is the hurt part)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== self_look
she glances at the {part} and sees it shaking, though she cannot feel the shake from inside
she looks down and sees the {part} already swelling, the shape of it changed
she checks the {part} the way she would check a trap she has set: carefully, from the side
she moves the {part} a little and watches it answer, stiff and grudging
she sees a thin line of blood at the {part} and stares at it as if it were someone else's
she looks at the {part} only long enough to know, and then not again
she makes herself look properly, and finds it neither as bad nor as good as she feared
she notices the {part} is held at a strange angle and straightens it, slowly, wincing
she watches the skin round the {part} flush and darken where the blow fell
she sees the {part} twitch and realises she did not tell it to
she finds herself staring at the {part} and drags her eyes off it, back to the fight
she looks at the {part} and feels, oddly, embarrassed for it
she looks down and sees grit stuck to the {part}, pressed in by the blow
she sees the {part} has gone still and quiet, and does not like the quiet

== self_look | state: spent, tired, hurt
she looks herself over and stops counting the hurts halfway, because the count does not help
she sees her own body leaning to one side and cannot remember starting to lean
her sides are heaving and she watches them as though watching someone else breathe
she looks down and the ground seems further away than it should be
she watches a tremble run across her whole body and wait, and run again

== self_look | zone: leg
she lifts the leg and sees it hang slack a moment before it tightens
she watches the leg take her weight and sees it shiver with the strain
she looks at the leg and sees it planted crooked, and does not trust it to straighten

== self_look | zone: arm
she looks at the hurt limb and sees it held tight against her body, guarding all on its own
she watches the hurt limb rise only halfway when she asks for all of it

== self_look | zone: chest, belly
she looks down at the hurt and sees each breath push against it, in and out
she sees her middle flinch with every heartbeat, a small jump under the skin

== self_look | zone: head
she squeezes her eyes shut and opens them to check the world has stayed where she left it
she glances down and the movement makes the whole place swing

== self_look | zone: neck
she tries to look down at herself and finds her neck will not let her lower her head
she turns her head a little to see the hurt, and stops when the pull reaches her jaw

== self_look | zone: back
she twists to look at the hurt on her back and stops short, the twist itself too much
she cannot see the hurt, only feel how her whole body has hunched round it

== self_look | zone: tail
she looks back at her tail and sees it lying limp, not curled the way it should be | has: tail
she watches her tail try to lift and fall back | has: tail

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# RESOLVE SHOWN: more of what she DOES instead of the story saying she would not give in
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== resolve_shown
she straightens one degree at a time until she is square to her opponent
she closes her mouth and breathes through her nose, slow and even
she takes a half step in, small, but forward
she turns her good side away on purpose and offers the hurt one
she shakes the dust off and doesn't watch it fall
she lets her opponent move first and does not flinch when she does
she lifts her head and looks past the pain to the other's eyes
she settles her balance low, and the sway goes out of her
she swallows hard and the next breath is steady
she presses the hurt place once against the ground, testing, and pushes off it anyway
she stops favouring the sore side and simply moves
her eyes narrow and stay narrowed
she rocks her weight from one side to the other, finding her centre again
she draws herself up to her full height and holds there
she exhales through her teeth and resets her stance in one motion
she brushes the grit from her face with one quick sweep and squares up again
she picks a spot on her opponent and never lets her gaze slip off it
she breathes in as deep as the hurt will let her, and then a little deeper
she flexes every limb once, quickly, as if checking a set of tools
she keeps her guard up even while the shaking runs through her
she circles, slow and level, refusing to give up the middle of the ground
she shifts back to the front of her balance, ready
she nods once, to no one, and comes on
she stops backing away and lets her opponent come to her
her stance tightens, compact and ready, nothing wasted
she closes the distance by a step her opponent did not expect
she leaves the sting on her face alone and keeps her stance tight
she sets her feet under her shoulders and roots there | has: legs
she plants her hind feet, digs in, and leans forward over them | has: legs
she draws her coils in under her, tight and level, and lifts her head high | has: coils
she raises her head and her hood opens wide | has: hood
she shakes her wings out once and folds them tight again, ready | has: wings
she rousts her feathers up and smooths them flat in one long shiver | has: feathers
she clenches her talons into the ground until they hold | has: talons
her flame flares up and holds, bright and steady | has: flame
her crest lifts slowly and stays up | has: crest
she lifts her fists back up into guard, shaking, but up | has: arms
she shakes her mane out of her eyes and fixes them forward | has: mane
her ears swivel off every other sound and lock on her opponent | has: ears
she sets her horn level and holds it there | has: horn
she lowers herself on her forelegs, weight forward, ready to spring | has: forelegs
she lets the ache stay where it is and moves anyway | state: tired, spent
she takes a breath, then another, and on the third she moves | state: tired, spent
