# MORE WAYS TO OPEN A PART, AND TO END ONE
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== open_on
the sound of her own breath catching, and the silence just after
the place where the last blow still stings
the other's weight shifting, before anything else moves
a smell carried across the space between them
a small adjustment of her stance that only she would notice | posture: standing
the feel of the air moving against her face
the other's breathing, heard before she looks
something that has changed since the last moment: a stance, a gaze, a distance
a sound from the far edge of the place, and neither of them turning to it
the stillness of her own body as she waits for it to obey
a question she asks herself and does not answer
the dust still hanging in the air from the last exchange
how dry her mouth has become
the place her eyes go first, and why
an ache she had forgotten, coming back
the moment she stops waiting
the other's shadow touching hers
the grit under her, pressing into her side | posture: down, pinned
the sky, or the roof, or whatever is above her, seen from flat on her back | posture: down, pinned
how tall the other looks from down here | posture: down
the weight on top of her, before anything else | posture: pinned
the taste of the ground, close and gritty | posture: down, pinned
her own pulse, loud and fast in her head
the smallest movement she can make, and whether she makes it
a sound the other makes without meaning to
the way the light has shifted since the fight began
an itch, a sting, a trickle, something small and insistent
her breath leaving her, and what she does before the next one
the gap she has been watching, about to close
what her body wants to do, and what she makes it do instead

== end_on
her breath going out, slow, and not coming back in quite yet
the dust settling round her | posture: standing
her eyes closing for a moment, then opening again
one sound, and then nothing
the other's face, and the expression on it
a tremor running out through her and away
the stillness of the place around them
something she sees and will remember
the ache settling deeper, quiet now, waiting
a breath held at the top, waiting for whatever comes
her weight shifting forward, ready to go | posture: standing
her gaze locked and steady
a small sound in her throat, swallowed
the ground against her face, and how far away everything else is | posture: down, pinned
the weight above her, steady as stone | posture: pinned
the sky above her and the grit under her | posture: down
her heartbeat slowing, or refusing to
the gap between them, measured once more
a stray breeze moving through the place, and neither of them feeling it
her body settling into a new shape round the hurt
the last of the echo fading off the walls or the water or the trees
a small muscle in her face, working
the sting of sweat in her eye, and she does not blink it away
the other's breathing, just audible
what she can still feel, and what she has stopped feeling
the warmth or chill of the ground, felt through her side | posture: down, pinned
one more look, and then nothing but readiness
her own stillness, chosen
the tension left hanging between them, unspent
a thought she leaves behind, half formed

== opening
the first sound either of them makes, and who flinches at it
the way each of them holds her weight, and what that says about how she fights
one of them glances at the sky, the other at the ground
an old scar on one of them, noticed by the other and filed away
the wind deciding which of them it favours
a slow circle that neither of them seems to have started
the stillness of one against the restlessness of the other
the ground marked by something that happened here before them
the temperature of the place on their skin, and who likes it better
a breath taken by both at once, by accident
the first small feint, so small it might be nothing
one of them settles; the other notices and does not
the light catching the one thing on each of them that shines
what each of them does with her mouth while she waits
a sound in the distance that neither of them looks toward
the way each one's gaze travels over the other, and where it stops
how close they stand, closer than strangers would choose
the patience of one, tested by the silence
something about the other that surprises, quietly
the ground between them, empty, waiting to be crossed

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PINS: more of being held down, by how far gone she is
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== pin_under | fade: fighting
she drives with everything at once and feels the weight above her rock and resettle
she breathes in big, deliberate pulls, storing up air for the next try
she keeps her eyes moving, hunting for the slack in the hold
she tenses one side, then the other, feeling which one the weight answers
she hisses up at the other, every limb straining in a different direction
she shoves at the ground with whatever she can reach and gains a hair's width
her muscles jump under the hold, all of them ready, none of them yet spent
she tucks her chin and turns her face, making herself smaller and harder to hold
she pretends to rest, breathing slow, and lets the hold relax into her
her heart is hammering, and she spends the hammering on another heave
she feels the pinner's grip slip a fraction and goes for it at once
she scrapes at the ground, the grit dragging under her
she gets her coils moving beneath the weight, a slow wave that tries to lift it | has: coils
she beats the free wing against the ground in hard, angry strokes | has: wings

== pin_under | fade: weakening
each heave is smaller than the last, and she feels the difference in every limb
she lies still between tries, gathering, and the gathering takes longer each time
her breath has a catch in it now, a little hitch at the top that steals half the air
she has stopped wasting effort on the face above her and saves it all for her body | facing: up
the strength she had at the start is draining out of her limbs like water from a leaking bowl
she pushes once and her arms shake so hard the push is gone before it starts | has: arms
she can still move, but she has to think about it first
her limbs feel thick and slow, as if moving through mud
the ground has stopped feeling hard and started feeling like it is pulling her in
she tries a twist and only half of her answers
the pinner's breath is louder now than her own, and that frightens her
she manages a buck and it lifts nothing at all
she breathes in and the weight presses the air straight back out of her

== pin_under | fade: fading
she cannot tell anymore if her eyes are open or shut
the edges of her vision have gone soft and grey
sounds come to her as if through water
her limbs feel very far away, and very heavy, and not quite hers
she is sinking under the weight, though she has not moved at all
she finds herself staring at a single grain of grit and cannot look away from it
her breathing has gone slow and shallow without her deciding it should
a twitch runs through her limbs, the ghost of a try
she squints up at the face above her and cannot make it hold still | facing: up
she makes a small sound, not a cry, just air with nowhere else to go
she forgets for a moment what she is fighting, and the forgetting scares her awake
her body is trying to rest, and she is losing the argument with it
the light seems to be getting dimmer, though nothing has moved

== pin_under | fade: nearly out
she can hardly feel the ground under her anymore, only the weight
her eyelids are too heavy to hold up and they slip, and she lets them
a long, slow breath goes out of her and the next one takes its time arriving
the sounds of the place have drawn away to almost nothing
she is barely there; most of her has gone somewhere quieter
the last of her strength flickers in one limb and goes out
her body has stopped answering, and she has stopped asking
the weight on her feels almost like a blanket now
she drifts, comes back for a breath, drifts again
something in her keeps trying, very small and very far away
her face has gone slack, the fight smoothed out of it
she is only breathing now, and the breathing is slow

== pin_pain | fade: fighting
the weight sits squarely on her {part} and bruises it a little deeper with every heartbeat
the grit under her {part} is pressed in until it stings
her {part} aches in a hard, flat way, like something left too long under a stone
a sharp, pinching pressure on her {part} that flares when she pushes and eases when she stops
her {part} is pressed so hard to the ground she can feel every pebble under it
she feels her {part} being slowly squashed out of its own shape
the press on her {part} sends a jolt through the rest of her each time she heaves
every breath the pinner takes leans a little more on her {part}
her {part} throbs under the hold, but the anger is louder than the hurt, still
a grinding ache in her {part}, worst at the exact point where the weight sits
the hard ground and the hard weight meet in her {part}, and neither of them gives
her {part} is pinned at an angle it does not like, and the angle aches more than the weight

== pin_pain | fade: weakening
the throb in her {part} has stopped coming and going; it stays, and climbs
her {part} burns under the weight, a deep heat spreading out from the contact
the press on her {part} drags a groan out of her whenever she draws breath
her {part} has gone numb at the centre and screaming at the edges
she can no longer tell where the ache in her {part} stops and the rest of her begins
each shift of the weight above sends a fresh wave of hurt through her {part}
her {part} is trembling under the press, the tremble feeding the hurt and the hurt feeding the tremble
the ache in her {part} has seeped outward until she cannot find its edges
she whimpers as the weight on her {part} settles a little deeper
a hot, swelling ache has filled her {part}, and the weight keeps pressing it fuller

== pin_pain | fade: fading
her {part} hurts so much she cannot think around it any more
the weight on her {part} crushes the breath out of her in short, broken sobs
her {part} is a hot, throbbing mass, and every heartbeat pushes more hurt into it
the pinner only has to breathe for the hurt in her {part} to surge
she has stopped trying to move her {part}; even thinking about it hurts
the hurt in her {part} has pushed everything else to the edges, the fight included
the press on her {part} wrings a thin, high sound out of her that she cannot stop
her {part} cramps and twists under the weight, and the cramp brings tears
the ache in her {part} reaches all the way through her and comes out as shaking
she cannot get away from her {part}; the hurt follows her down into every breath

== pin_pain | fade: nearly out
the pain in her {part} has gone dull and enormous, a weight inside the weight
her {part} hurts so steadily now it has become the only clock she has
there is nothing left in her to push the hurt in her {part} away; she simply lies in it
the hurt in her {part} rises once more and carries her a little further off
she can hardly feel anything now except the deep, slow throb in her {part}
the ache in her {part} blurs into the grey that is coming in from every side
her {part} pulses with pain, and each pulse seems to come from further away
the last of her strength goes into a tiny flinch away from the press on her {part}

== pin_hold
she presses down evenly, giving the other nothing to push against
she keeps her breathing slow so the other cannot use the rhythm of it
she shifts her weight onto the side that is trying hardest to rise
she feels the next try coming in the tightening under her and is already braced for it
she spreads herself wide and low, making herself heavy as a stone
she holds the other's eyes and does not blink | facing: up
she can feel the other's muscles bunching through her own body, and she leans into each one
she keeps her grip firm but not crushing, saving her strength for the long haul
she settles a little lower each time the other relaxes, taking the ground back
she lets her own hurts ache and keeps her weight exactly where it is | has: hurts
she turns her face aside from a bite, never loosening her hold
she listens to the breathing under her and changes nothing
she drives her weight down through her hips and keeps it there | has: legs
she braces her arms either side and locks them straight | has: arms
she loops her coils one turn tighter and holds them there | has: coils
she pins a wing under her body and keeps her own wings spread for balance | has: wings
she grips with her talons and leans her whole body forward over them | has: talons
she rides each heave the way she would ride a gust, loose and then heavy again
she holds still so long the stillness itself becomes a kind of pressure
she keeps one eye on the other's face and one on the free limb
she tightens the hold the instant the other draws breath to try again
she lets the other wear herself out against her and gives back nothing at all
she adjusts her balance by a hair every few heartbeats, never letting the other find a pattern
the other's body heat comes up through her own, and she uses it to feel each move
she lowers her head so the other cannot reach her face | facing: up
she feels a shiver run through the body under her and holds steady through it
she keeps her weight a little off-centre, ready to follow a roll
she wedges a limb behind the other's to stop any turn before it starts
she leans her full weight down in a slow, measured push, then holds
she waits through each struggle like waiting out a rainstorm

== pin_strain | held: early
the strain of holding settles first in her shoulders, a warm tightness
she grits her teeth as her own hurt flares with the effort of bracing | has: hurts
her breath is coming fast, matching the struggle under her
she feels her balance tested with every move below her, and has to work to keep it
her muscles are already humming with the effort, though it has only just begun
her worst hurt throbs the instant she clamps down, as if it had been waiting for this | has: hurts
her grip is strong now, but she can already feel where it will tire first

== pin_strain | held: middle
her muscles have begun to ache in a deep, steady way that will not let her forget them
her breathing is getting ragged and she forces it back into a rhythm
she shifts one limb to rest it and the hold slips a little; she shifts it back
the strain is in her neck now, a hot band from holding her head braced
her own wound sets up a constant hurt that matches every try from below | has: hurts
a cramp threatens in the limb she has braced longest and she breathes through it
sweat runs down her sides and the grip grows slick where it touches
her heart is pounding as hard as if she were the one trapped

== pin_strain | held: long
every muscle she owns is shaking now, and she can feel the shake travel into the hold
her limbs have gone stiff and hot, and she is not sure she could let go quickly if she wanted to
the hurt she brought into this has grown teeth, and it bites with every breath | has: hurts
her breath sobs in and out of her, as spent as the one beneath her
she is holding on by will now; the strength left her arms a while ago | has: arms
her whole body is one long ache from the effort of staying still
her eyes keep wanting to close, and she will not let them
she can feel her grip loosening on its own and has to tighten it on purpose, again and again
her own legs are trembling so hard she wonders if she will be able to stand after | has: legs

== struggle
she gets a limb wedged against the other's middle and pushes until it shakes
she hunches and heaves, trying to get her back under her
she throws her weight to one side, then the other, rocking the hold loose
she bites at whatever part of the other comes close
she slides an inch at a time toward the edge of the weight, patient and sly
she arches hard enough to lift both of them, for one heartbeat
she twists her head free and uses it as a lever for the rest of her
she kicks at the ground for anything to push off | has: legs
she pries at the hold with her free arm, one grip at a time | has: arms
she whips her coils against the ground and tries to roll the weight off | has: coils
she flaps the free wing wildly, trying to lever herself out | has: wings
she goes limp and slippery, then drives up hard all at once
she braces one shoulder on the ground and pivots round it
she plants her head and pushes, trying to bridge from it
she wriggles, small and constant, never letting the hold settle
she bites back the effort sound and heaves again, silently
she tucks in tight, then explodes outward, every limb at once
she scrapes and claws at the ground, dragging herself a hand's width
she squirms toward the side where the weight is lightest
she shoves upward with her hips and turns at the same moment, looking for the gap

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TAKEDOWNS: more ways to put the other on the ground before a pin
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== takedown
she ducks low and drives her shoulder in under the other's balance
she grabs a limb, pulls it in close, and sweeps the support out from under
she slams her full weight into the other's side and keeps pushing until something gives
she drops suddenly, letting the other overbalance onto her, then rolls her down
she catches the other mid-step and shoves just as the weight comes onto the front foot
she hooks a limb behind the other's and twists, taking her down sideways
she rears up and drops onto the other's back, pressing her flat | has: forelegs
she wraps both arms round the other's middle and lifts and turns | has: arms
she winds her coils round the other's body and squeezes until balance goes | has: coils
she knocks the other off balance with a buffet of her wings and rides her down | has: wings
she seizes a shoulder in her talons and drags her down from above | has: talons
she lets the other lunge past and helps her along with a hard shove to the ribs
she pushes the other's head down with one hand and heaves from underneath with the other | has: arms
she drives forward hard, chest to chest, and the other topples backward under the push
she catches the other on the turn and leans, just a little, at the right moment
she spins out of a grab and uses the other's own pull to throw her down
she dives low across the ground and scoops the other's footing away
she yanks the other off her line by whatever she can grab and drops her face first
she shoulders into the hurt side and lets the pain do the rest
she wedges her horn under the other's chest and heaves her over | has: horn
she climbs onto the other's back from behind and rides her to the ground
she clamps her beak on a limb and twists, and the other spins off balance | has: beak
she rams in low and fast and lifts as she hits, tipping the other over her shoulder
she trips the other with a quick hooking sweep and drops on top of her | has: legs
she bears down with her wings spread, a sudden weight from above, and pins the other flat | has: wings

== escape_after
she staggers clear, shaking out every limb as if to make sure each one is still hers
she breathes deep, gulping the free air, and turns to keep the other in sight
she rolls away and comes up crouched, ready, but slower than she wants to be
the places where she was held throb now that the pressure is off them
she backs off and keeps her eyes on the other, wary of being caught again
she shakes her head hard, clearing the grey that crept in under the hold
her limbs feel light and strange after so long under weight
she spits out grit and wipes her face and does not take her eyes off the one who held her
the relief is so sharp it nearly knocks her down
she stays low, catching her breath, every muscle still quivering
she puts space between them fast, then stops and turns to face the other
she rubs at the places that were pinned, and the rubbing makes them sting
she circles away from the spot where she was held as if it were still dangerous
she can still feel the weight on her, a ghost of it, and has to shake it off
her heart is still hammering from the struggle, and it does not slow
she stands up straighter than she needs to, letting the other see she is free
she takes a long, shaky breath and lets it out, steadying
she moves stiffly at first, the pinned limbs slow to remember how to move
she tests her balance with a step, then another, and finds it holds
she turns to face the other with something new in her stance: caution, and anger

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CARRY: more of an old injury showing while she acts ({part} is the hurt part)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== carry | zone: arm
she leads with the other side and lets the {part} trail, guarding it | role: attacker
the {part} comes through the strike late and weak, and she covers it with her weight | role: attacker
she braces with the {part} and it trembles under the load | role: pinner
she shifts the hold off her {part}, and the hold is weaker for it | role: pinner
she guards the {part} so carefully that half her chances go unanswered

== carry | zone: leg
she drives off the {part} and it buckles slightly, throwing her line wide | role: attacker
she steps short with the {part} and the whole move comes in a beat late | role: attacker
she braces on the good side and lets the {part} only rest against the ground | role: pinner
the {part} shakes under her as she bears down, and she leans away from it | role: pinner
she hops more than steps, sparing the {part}, and the rhythm of it is off

== carry | zone: chest, belly
she cannot lean all her weight into the hold; her {part} will not take the press | role: pinner
she holds her breath through the effort, sparing the {part}, and lets it out in a gasp | role: attacker
she strikes with her body angled away, shielding her {part} even as she commits
every heave from below drives into her {part}, and she grunts each time | role: pinner

== carry | zone: head
she blinks hard before every move, waiting for her sight to settle | role: attacker
she keeps her head steady and low, as if it might spill if she moved it
she misjudges the distance by a hair and has to adjust mid-move | role: attacker
she presses her face away from the other, sparing the {part} even as she holds | role: pinner

== carry | zone: neck
she holds her head stiffly, turning her whole body instead of her {part}
every lunge pulls at her {part} and she shortens them to spare it | role: attacker
she braces her {part} against the effort of holding and breathes through clenched teeth | role: pinner

== carry | zone: back
she moves carefully, keeping her spine straight, and it makes her stiff and readable | role: attacker
the strain of holding settles straight into her {part} and stays there | role: pinner
she throws the blow with her {part} held rigid, and it lands flat and stiff | role: attacker

== carry | zone: tail
her {part} hangs heavy behind her, and every turn she makes is a little off balance
she tries to whip her {part} round for momentum and it drags instead | role: attacker | has: tail
she cannot use her {part} to brace the hold, and has to make do without it | role: pinner

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AERIAL: more of flight, falls and the ground coming up
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== aerial | moment: takeoff
she crouches, springs, and catches the air on the first stroke
the wind of her rising throws grit in the other's face
she lifts off sideways, low and fast, and only climbs once she is clear
each downbeat booms like a sheet shaken out, and the ground drops further
she rises in a slow, heavy spiral, as if the air itself were thick
she claws her way up the first few lengths, then the air takes her and she climbs easy

== aerial | moment: dive
she drops in a long curve that ends exactly where the other is standing
her shadow grows on the ground, faster than the other can step out of it
the wind roars over her and she narrows her eyes against it, holding the line
she folds tight and falls, and the speed builds until the air hums
she feints one way in the fall and comes down on the other
she comes in so steep there is no time to see her until she is there

== aerial | moment: struck_from_above
there is a rush of air and a weight from nowhere, and she is flat before she understands
the blow comes down through the top of her and the ground pushes back up
she staggers, half crouched, the strike still ringing through her whole body
she hears it coming, the hiss of the fall, and has just time to flinch
she buckles under the drop and only her pride keeps her from going all the way down
the force drives the breath out of her in a single cough

== aerial | moment: carried
the ground pulls away and her stomach stays behind with it
she dangles in the grip, every swing of her body tugging at the hold
the air is cold up here, colder with each wingbeat, and she has no way to get down
the grip on her shifts once and her heart stops, then it closes again
she can see the whole place below now, the scuffs and marks of the fight laid out small
she hangs in the talons, kicking, and the talons only bite in harder | has: talons
she feels the arms locked round her tighten with each beat upward | has: arms

== aerial | moment: grounded
the hurt wing locks open, useless, and she drops like a leaf in still air | has: wings
she beats hard with the good wing and only spins herself round | has: wings
she sees the ground swinging up and cannot level out in time
the wing folds under her mid-stroke and she simply stops flying | has: wings
she comes down in a skidding, flailing landing, the bad wing dragging | has: wings
she tries to glide on the hurt wing, but it will not hold its shape and the glide becomes a fall | has: wings

== aerial | moment: knocked
the strike stops her wings mid-beat and she falls out of the sky | has: wings
she is flung back through the air, wings beating wildly to catch herself | has: wings
the hit spins her round and she loses the air, plummeting
her balance in the air goes all at once, and she is falling before she knows it
she drops, one wing still half spread, the other folded against the hurt | has: wings
the blow knocks the wind out of her wings as much as her body, and she sinks | has: wings

== aerial | moment: strain
each stroke of the bad wing comes slow and short, and she drifts sideways | has: wings
she labours to hold her height, losing a little with every beat
the hurt wing flutters weakly, and she can feel it getting worse | has: wings
she grits through each downstroke, fighting the pull of the ground
every wingbeat is a small agony she has to choose to take
she holds the hurt wing stiff, gliding on it instead of beating, and loses height steadily | has: wings

== aerial | moment: lift_fail
the flyer hauls upward, and the weight below digs in and does not budge
the grip strains and slips, and the flyer flaps clear, the lift abandoned
she braces, the wings beat hard above her, and she stays exactly where she is | has: wings
for a moment both of them hang in the struggle, and then the grip tears loose
the attempt ends in a tangle of grit and air, and the ground keeps her

== aerial | moment: falling
she is still turning in the air when the hit comes, and it spins her faster
there is no ground to brace against; the blow simply sends her somewhere else
she takes the strike in mid-fall and lands a heartbeat later, twice hurt
the blow knocks her sideways in the air and the landing comes at a worse angle
the air is no help at all; it only lets her be hit wherever the other likes

== aerial | moment: spiked
she is driven down so fast the ground is there before she has finished falling
the impact punches her into the dirt and drives a long groan out of her
she hits and the ground answers with a dull, heavy thump she feels all the way through her
she is slammed flat, and the flyer is already climbing away before she can move
the force of it pushes her down into the earth and holds her there a moment

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# KNOCKOUT, COLLAPSE, AFTERMATH: more of the end of it
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== knockout
her eyes go blank in the middle of the blow, the lights simply out
she sags, folding at every joint at once, and is down before the sound of the hit has faded
the tension drains from her all in one go, and she drops like cloth
she tips slowly to one side, her face empty, and does not try to stop the fall
her head lolls as she goes down, loose and heavy
the breath leaves her in a small sound, and then there is just the slow rise and fall of her side
she lands without a flinch, without a cry, as if the ground did not hurt at all
her limbs twitch once, then lie loose where they fell
her eyes roll up and close, and the fight is gone out of her face
for a heartbeat she is still upright, swaying, and then all of her lets go
she crumples, not like someone falling but like someone set down
her mouth falls a little open and stays that way, breathing slow
she goes down in a heap and stays there, still breathing, far away
her coils slacken all along their length and spill flat on the ground | has: coils
her ears drop sideways and stay there | has: ears
her feathers settle round her loose and rumpled, no longer held in place | has: feathers
her flame sinks low and steadies, a quiet ember | has: flame
her arms drop to her sides and stay there, palms open | has: arms
her legs give way and fold under her, and she lies where they put her | has: legs
her crest lies down flat and does not lift again | has: crest

== knockout_watch
she waits for the eyes to open, and they do not
she listens to the slow breathing and feels her own heart start to come down
she does not go closer; she does not trust it yet
the stillness in front of her is so complete it rings
her body is still coiled for the next exchange, and there is no next exchange
she lets her guard down a little at a time, watching for the trick that does not come
she looks at what she has done, and the looking is strange
she feels the fight go out of her own limbs as surely as it went out of the other's
the sound of her own panting fills the place
she stands there a long moment, and nothing happens, and she understands
a slow wave of something like relief goes through her, and after it, the tiredness
she is glad, oddly, of every breath she sees the other take
she takes one step back, then another, and only then lets herself breathe
the place seems very quiet now, very wide, and very still
she notices she is shaking, and cannot tell if it is from effort or something else
she half expects the other to rise, and when she does not, she has to look away
she stands guard over the stillness out of habit, and only slowly stops
her own hurts begin to speak up, one by one, now that there is room for them
she lowers her head and breathes, and breathes, and the world comes back
she lets her wings sag open, too tired to fold them | has: wings
her tail drops to the ground behind her, its work done | has: tail

== collapse
her body gives up the effort a little at a time, and then all at once
she goes down slowly, one part after another, until there is nothing left standing
she sways, tries to catch herself, and only finds the ground
her limbs tremble and then simply stop holding
she sinks down as if through deep water, unhurried and unable to stop
she tilts, drops to one side, and lies there blinking up at nothing
there is no pain in it, only a sudden absence of strength
she tells herself to stand and nothing answers
her breath goes out of her in a long sigh as she goes down
she slumps forward and her face meets the ground before she can turn it
the effort of staying up becomes too much, and she lets go of it
her eyes stay open as she falls, wide and surprised
she settles on the ground in a heap, all the tension gone out of her
she makes one more try to rise, and her body only shudders
her head drops, then the rest of her follows it down
her vision tunnels and the ground arrives softly, almost gently
she folds at the middle, then all the way down
her legs splay out from under her and she sits, then sags flat | has: legs
her coils slump and spread, the strength gone out of every length of her | has: coils
her wings droop open and drag in the dirt as she goes down | has: wings
she gets an arm out to catch herself and it folds under her | has: arms
she is too tired even to fall properly; she just sinks | state: spent

== aftermath_winner
she lets her breath go in a long, unsteady rush, and only then realises she was holding it
the tremor starts in her limbs as soon as the need for them ends
she lowers herself carefully, every hurt arguing with the motion
the place is quiet now, and she can hear her own heart slowing
she touches a sore spot, finds it worse than she thought, and lets it be
she cannot stop watching the other, as if the fight might still come back
the heat of the fight drains out of her and leaves her cold and stiff
she finds herself swaying where she stands and widens her stance to stop it
one of her hurts throbs louder now that nothing else is shouting over it
she licks a split lip and tastes the fight on it
she closes her eyes for a moment and the whole place seems to tilt
she feels the ache settle into every part that took a hit, one after another
a strange emptiness follows the fight, where the purpose of it was
she is suddenly very thirsty, and very tired, and very aware of everything that hurts
she straightens slowly, taking stock, and the stock is not good, but she is standing
she lets her guard down a piece at a time, as if unbuckling armour
her ears turn toward the other, still listening for a sign of movement | has: ears
she folds her wings with care, wincing where a stroke went wrong | has: wings
she shakes her feathers loose and they settle crooked, ruffled from the fight | has: feathers
she sits down heavily, not meaning to, her legs simply done | has: legs
she sinks onto her coils and rests her head low, breathing hard | has: coils
she can barely stand; winning has taken nearly everything she had | state: tired, spent
she is still breathing easily, and that surprises her more than winning does | state: fresh, winded

== aftermath_fallen
she lies very still, her breath barely stirring the dust by her mouth
a faint frown stays on her face, as if she were still thinking about the fight
her body has settled into the ground the way water settles into a hollow
the hurt places are plain to see now that she is not hiding them
her side rises and falls, slow and steady and far away
one limb lies at an odd angle, the way it landed, and she has not moved it
the tension has gone out of her face, and she looks smaller for it
dust drifts down onto her and stays where it lands
a small twitch runs along her body and fades
she looks almost peaceful, if not for the marks the fight has left
her mouth is slightly open, breathing slow and shallow
the light falls across her and picks out each bruise
her eyes are closed, and her brow smooths slowly as the moments pass
her breath catches once, then evens out again
a fine shiver passes through her and is gone
she lies on her side with one limb tucked under her, the way she fell
the stillness of her is the loudest thing in the place
her wings lie spread where they fell, one half folded under her | has: wings
her coils are loose and spread out across the ground | has: coils
her feathers are rumpled and still, lifting a little in the air | has: feathers
her tail lies straight out behind her, quiet for the first time | has: tail
her flame burns low and steady, the only thing still awake | has: flame

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# DEVASTATING BLOWS: more thoughts on both sides
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== devastated_thought | state: fresh, winded
*That— how did she find that?*
*Breathe. Just breathe first.*
*Don't let her see. Don't let her see.*
*All right. That one counts.*
*Where's the ground? There. Good.*
*Move. Hurting can wait.*
*I didn't even see it.*
*She'll go there again. Cover it.*
*No. I'm fine. I'm fine.*
*That's going to cost me later.*

== devastated_thought | state: tired, spent
*Too much. That's too much.*
*Everything's ringing.*
*Where am I? Where is she?*
*Hold on. Just hold on.*
*Not that. Anything but that again.*
*It won't stop. Why won't it stop?*
*Stay awake. Stay here.*
*I can't feel the edge of it.*
*Please. Not now.*
*Up. Somehow. Up.*
*I can't do that again.*

== devastating_thought
*That one went all the way through.*
*Exactly there. Exactly.*
*She won't shake that off.*
*Felt it land right in the middle.*
*Couldn't have placed it better.*
*That's the one she'll remember.*
*She didn't even have time to brace.*
*There it is. Everything she had, gone out of her.*
*I felt it go in deep.*
*That's what all the others were for.*
*She went soft under it. She felt it.*
*Never landed one like that before.*
*The whole fight was building to that.*
*Don't stop now. Not after that.*
*Look at her face. She knows.*
*Right on the hurt. Right on it.*
*That was perfect. Don't think. Follow it.*
*She's not going to forget where that came from.*
*All that work, and it paid in one.*
*She bent round it. I saw her bend.*

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# STATUS SEEN: more of the one who notices an opening and goes for it
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== status_seen | status: flinched, dazed, off_balance, doubled_over, breathless, sputtering
she sees the guard waver and moves before it can come back
she catches the stumble from the corner of her eye and is already closing
the other's attention slips for half a heartbeat, and she is in that half heartbeat
she reads the wobble in the stance and aims right at it
she doesn't think; the gap is there and her body goes into it
*There. Right there.*
*She's open. Go.*
she sees the other gasping and takes the moment while it lasts
she watches the other blink hard, unfocused, and strikes before the focus returns
the lean is too far, the balance gone, and she shoves into it

== status_seen | status: stiff, cramped, bound
she sees the limb answer late and times the next move to the lateness
she notes the stiffness and circles to the slow side
she sees the hold has taken the spring out of the other and presses that advantage
*She can't turn that side. Use it.*
she watches the other try to move freely and fail, and goes where the movement can't follow

== status_seen | status: opening
she drives straight into the opening before it can close
she strikes where the guard used to be
the space is there, and she fills it with everything she has
she moves into the gap like water finding the low place
*Now, before she covers it.*
she doesn't hesitate; the opening is the invitation and she takes it

# IMPACT EXPANSION: more reactions, sensations, pain shows and the physical moments of a fight
# (same categories and condition combinations as story_blocks.txt; a few new sensible combinations)

# REACTIONS: the moment of impact, sized to the blow
== reaction | size: glancing
a quick frown, as if someone had stepped on her shadow
she breathes out sharply and the moment is gone
her skin shivers over the spot the way a pond shivers under a dropped leaf
she does not look at it at all, which says enough
a small click of her teeth, irritation more than anything
her chin comes up a fraction, insulted
the touch skids off her and she lets it
she resettles her weight as if nothing had asked her to move it
a dry sniff, and her eyes stay exactly where they were
it costs her a blink, no more
the faint sting makes her lip twitch, and she smooths it at once
a narrow look that promises to remember it
her crest flicks up and lies straight down again | has: crest
a few feathers lift where it brushed her and settle on their own | has: feathers
her wings give one small rustle against her sides | has: wings
her flame leans away from the blow and stands straight again | has: flame
she tips her beak toward the place, considers it, and lets it go | has: beak
her coils shift one loop over and lie still | has: coils
the rocky points of her mane grind once and are quiet | has: mane
she dusts the spot with the back of one paw, almost a sneer | has: arms
her ruff lifts at the edges and settles | has: ruff
her talons shift their grip on the ground and hold | has: talons

== reaction | size: solid
a grunt that comes from low in her belly
the hit pushes a short "huh" out of her, half a question
she bares her teeth at nothing and shakes her head once
her weight goes onto the far side and stays there a beat
a sharp sniff, and her eyes go hard
she squints as if into wind, mouth tight
the breath she had saved for the next move is gone and she has to fetch another
she twists a little, as though she could turn the sting out of herself
a quick, clipped yelp, more anger than pain
her eyes flick to the place and back, measuring it
her mouth pulls sideways and stays crooked for a moment
she sways back from it and comes forward again on the same breath
a hiss, then a long breath out to finish it
the shock of it hums under her skin for a few heartbeats
she takes it with her head down and her back hunched, like rain
her feathers lift along her back in a ridge and only slowly lie down | has: feathers
her crest jerks flat against her head | has: crest
her beak snaps shut hard, a sound like a lid dropped on a box | has: beak
her flame jumps tall and ragged for a breath | has: flame
she half spreads one wing as if to fend it off, too late | has: wings
a ripple runs down the whole length of her coils and out to the tip | has: coils
her talons clench and scrape short grooves in the ground | has: talons
her mane bristles and the hard points of it clatter together | has: mane
her ears flatten sideways, then turn back toward her opponent | has: ears
her tail jerks straight out behind her and slowly curls back | has: tail

== reaction | size: heavy
the blow wrenches a cough out of her and she nearly bites her tongue on it
she hunches round it, mouth open, eyes squeezed to slits
a loud grunt, and her front end dips toward the ground
she goes stiff from head to foot and only her breath moves
her cry comes out flat and short, as if stamped out of her
she spins half round with the force and has to find her opponent again
a whine slips out on the breath after, quieter and worse
her face crumples for a heartbeat and she makes it hard again
she sucks a breath through her teeth that whistles
the strength goes out of her stance and she puts it back piece by piece
she lurches sideways and her balance chases after her
a bellow of hurt, too loud, that she regrets at once
her eyes go wet and blank, and then focused and furious
the hit drives a long groan out of her that she cannot stop halfway
her head goes down as if ducking a second blow that is not coming
she shakes all over, once, the way she would shake off water
her feathers flare out in a ruffled burst and stay raised | has: feathers
her wings bang open with the shock and she drags them shut | has: wings
a hoarse squawk breaks out of her beak before she can stop it | has: beak
her flame gutters low and climbs back up, unsteady | has: flame
her crest slams flat and quivers | has: crest
her coils jerk tight, then loose, as if they had been stung | has: coils
her talons rake the ground backward as she is shoved | has: talons
her tails fan wide behind her and every tip trembles | has: tails
she clutches at the place with her free hand and her face twists | has: arms

== reaction | size: tremendous
the scream catches somewhere in her chest and comes out as a hoarse bark
her body throws itself away from the blow before her mind has caught up
for a moment her limbs do not know which of them should hold her
a strangled howl, and then the ragged sucking for air after it
she reels, eyes rolling, and the cry follows a heartbeat late
she clenches so hard round the hurt that she shakes like a drawn bow
her mouth gapes and shuts and gapes, like a fish on a bank
a wail that rises, hitches, and rises again
the sound she makes bounces back at her off everything around them
she drops her head nearly to the ground and keens into it
she staggers in a half circle, unable to stop her own momentum
the cry is so big it seems to empty her; she sways in the quiet after it
every muscle she owns jumps at once and then hangs slack for a heartbeat
she shrieks, and the shriek turns into coughing
her face shows it all in a moment: lost, then found, then furious
a howl breaks loose, then splinters into gasping
she makes a sound like a kicked door and then no sound at all for a long moment
the shock throws her head back so hard the cry goes straight up
her legs fold and unfold under her as if they were arguing | has: legs
she hugs herself round the hurt, rocking on the spot | has: arms
her forelegs splay and she stops herself just short of the ground | has: forelegs
she rocks back on her heels from it, mouth wide | has: legs
her beak gapes wide and the shriek that comes out of it is harsh enough to hurt her own ears | has: beak
both wings snap fully open and beat once, wildly, at nothing | has: wings
her flame flares huge and then shrinks to a shaking ember | has: flame
her crest stands bolt upright and trembles | has: crest
her coils whip and lash across the ground in one long convulsion | has: coils
the hard points of her mane rattle as she shudders | has: mane
her tails thrash in every direction and then cling to her | has: tails
her talons clench on nothing and stay clenched, trembling | has: talons

# SENSATIONS: what the pain in a part is like, by how bad it now is
== sensation | level: sore
her {part} stings like a slap from a wet branch, sharp and shallow
a warm tingle spreads over her {part} and cools again
her {part} feels faintly bruised, the way it does after a long day's running
there is a small hot spot on her {part} that she keeps almost noticing
her {part} prickles under the skin as though there were grit in it
her {part} protests on the first movement and is quiet on the second
a dull, sulky ache in her {part} that is easy to step over
her {part} feels stiff, as if it had cooled down too fast
the surface of her {part} burns, and the burn is already thinning
a tender patch on her {part}, nothing worse, as long as nothing touches it twice
her {part} hums faintly, like a plucked string going quiet
her {part} is a little slow, a little warm, a little sorry for itself
the sting in her {part} sharpens when she breathes in and fades as she breathes out
her {part} aches in a narrow line where the blow skidded across it
she can feel her heartbeat in her {part} for a few beats, and then she cannot

== sensation | level: hurting
her {part} aches deep and wide, as if the blow had sunk in and spread
a stubborn heat sits in her {part} and does not cool
her {part} tightens up between movements and has to be coaxed loose again
a knot of hurt in her {part} that tugs on everything around it
her {part} throbs slow and heavy, like a drum heard through a wall
every use of her {part} wakes a sharp complaint and leaves a dull one behind
her {part} feels fat and stiff, swelling into its own skin
the hurt in her {part} is a pressure more than a pain, until she moves it
her {part} feels tender from every side at once
a sour, nagging ache in her {part} that she cannot shake out
her {part} stings when it stretches and aches when it bunches
the ache in her {part} has spread a little further each time she checks on it
her {part} feels slow, as if it were wading through something thick
a hot point in her {part} with a cold, numb ring round it
her {part} jars on every step, a small hard knock each time
she can feel the swelling in her {part} pushing at her with each pulse
her {part} cramps once, as a warning, and lets go
the pain in her {part} is steady now, something she can count on being there

== sensation | level: very painful
her {part} pounds like a fist on a door that will not open
a deep, hot ache in her {part} that flares sharp whenever she forgets about it
her {part} feels packed with heat, the skin straining to hold it all
the pain in her {part} has a centre she could point to and edges that keep spreading
her {part} trembles under even small loads, and the trembling hurts
a twisting ache wrings her {part} and does not let go when she breathes
her {part} sends a bright stab up through her at every jolt
the throb in her {part} is so steady she starts breathing in time with it
her {part} feels scraped inside, every movement rubbing on the sore place
something in her {part} clenches when she reaches, a fist closing round the hurt
the pain in her {part} keeps calling her eyes down to it, again and again
her {part} burns in a long stripe and aches in a wide pool round it
she feels each heartbeat as a hard push outward from inside her {part}
her {part} stiffens between moves, and every restart is a fresh wrench
a sick, heavy ache in her {part} that settles in her stomach too
her {part} is too hot, too tight and too loud to ignore
the hurt in her {part} sharpens to a point whenever she breathes in deep
her {part} feels held in a vice that someone keeps tightening a turn at a time

== sensation | level: excruciating
her {part} blazes so fiercely that her eyes stream from it
the pain in her {part} swallows sound; for a moment she hears nothing at all
her {part} feels clamped in burning jaws that will not open
every twitch of her {part} sets off a burst of agony that rings through all of her
her {part} is a wall of pain she keeps walking into
the hurt in her {part} pulses so hard it shakes the breath in her
her {part} throbs with a heat that seems to pour out into the rest of her
the pain in her {part} is too big to brace against; it goes straight through every brace
her {part} feels hammered flat and still being hammered
she cannot find one quiet place anywhere in her {part}
her {part} jolts with sharp, sickening shocks, each one emptying her lungs
the agony in her {part} comes up into her throat and sits there, choking her
her {part} is so tender that the stir of air from her own movement hurts it
her thoughts keep breaking on the pain in her {part} like waves on a rock
her {part} feels pressed in a hot fist and twisted
the pain in her {part} makes her stomach heave and her sight swim

== sensation | level: devastated
her {part} has gone past answering; it only lies there and hurts
the pain in her {part} has a dull, endless roar to it, like water over a weir
her {part} feels hollowed out and filled again with ache
she has stopped trying to use her {part}; even the thought of it hurts
her {part} is heavy, hot and shaking, and all of it is pain
her {part} sends up a thin cry inside her every time her heart beats
the faintest pressure on her {part} turns her whole body into a flinch
her {part} feels like something heavy strapped to her that hurts as if it were her own
there is no edge to the pain in her {part} any more; it simply is her {part}
her {part} quivers on its own, and every quiver is a fresh hurt
she has forgotten what her {part} felt like before; she knows only this
her {part} burns and freezes by turns, and she cannot bear either
her {part} lies still because it can do nothing else, and the stillness aches
the hurt in her {part} has outlasted her flinching; now she only shakes
her {part} seems to drag the rest of her down toward it, an anchor of pain
her {part} hurts so steadily that a moment of less pain startles her

== sensation | zone: head | level: very painful, excruciating, devastated
the ground swims each time she moves her eyes
a heavy pressure fills her skull, as if it were stuffed with wet sand
light hurts; she squints, and the squinting hurts too
her ears hum with a high thin note that will not stop
her thoughts come slow and in the wrong order
a hot, bruised tightness clamps her face from ear to ear

== sensation | zone: head | level: sore, hurting
a pulse taps at her temple, light and quick
her eyes water a little and her sight stays clear
a dull pressure above her eyes, like a storm coming on
her face feels hot on the side that took it

== sensation | zone: neck | level: sore
her throat tickles and she swallows twice to quiet it
a pull down one side of her neck when she looks round

== sensation | zone: neck | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
every swallow catches halfway, as if on a burr

she tastes copper at the back of her throat and swallows it

# PAIN_SHOW: how she visibly shows the pain of a hit in one part, sized to its level ({part} as in the main file)
== pain_show | level: minor
a short, unimpressed breath through her teeth
she keeps her eyes on her opponent and not on {part}
a flicker across her face, gone before it can settle

a flat look, and nothing else given away

a tiny wince she wipes off her face at once
she lets {part} take it and does not bother to guard it afterwards
a slight tilt of her head, as if she had heard something odd
nothing in her movement changes, and that is a message of its own
the faintest hitch at the corner of her mouth

# TELLS: what an opponent can SEE of her
== tell | state: fresh | posture: standing
her weight sits light and centred, ready to go any direction
she watches with interest rather than worry
now and then she shifts her stance purely to make her opponent shift hers
she holds herself loose, nothing clenched, nothing hidden
her feathers lie smooth and tight against her | has: feathers
her flame burns tall and steady | has: flame

== tell | state: winded
her breathing is audible from across the ground now
she keeps a little more distance than she did
she tilts her head back slightly to get more air
she has started blinking more, quick and dry
her coils lie in looser loops than before | has: coils

== tell | state: tired
her movements have lost their snap; she arrives rather than strikes
she rocks her weight back onto her heels whenever she can | has: legs
she keeps her mouth open now even when she is not moving
her stance has widened, more for balance than for readiness
a pause before every effort, short, but there

== tell | state: spent
her eyes are dull and slow to follow anything
she leans toward her opponent as if the ground were tilted that way
every movement starts with a visible gathering of will
her head lifts only when something comes at it
her crest lies flat and has stopped moving at all | has: crest

== tell | state: winded, tired

her mane hangs lower, the points no longer bristling | has: mane
her coils shift restlessly, never quite settling | has: coils
