# ===============================================================================================================
# MOVED, BY THE ENGINE'S BLOW KIND: how this kind of blow rocks her where she is (rock: her feet stay put) or
# carries her off her line (stagger: a step or more the way the blow went).
# ===============================================================================================================

# blunt
== moved | kind: blunt | how: rock | where: standing
the dull weight of it rolls her head on her neck and she pulls it level again
her body takes the thud like a sack takes a kick, and her knees soften and stiffen
she rocks at the hips with it, chest pushed back, then comes forward again
her shoulders absorb the bump and settle lower than before

== moved | kind: blunt | how: rock | where: down
the thump presses her flat for a moment and she rolls half onto her side
the blow jolts her against the ground and dust puffs up from under her
her whole body shifts a little way on the ground under the force, then lies still

== moved | kind: blunt | how: rock | where: held
the thud jolts through her and into the grip, and the grip only clamps down harder
her head knocks back as far as the hold allows and stops sharp
the blow rocks her in the hold like a bell in its frame

== moved | kind: blunt | how: rock | where: holding
the thud shakes her grip and she clenches down, teeth or claws, to keep it
she rocks with the blow but keeps her hold, the strain showing in her shoulders
her grip shivers loose for a breath and she sets it again, harder

== moved | kind: blunt | how: stagger
the heavy blow shoves her back a step and she plants a foot behind to stop
she is pushed backward in two short, scuffing steps, guard dropped
the force carries her sideways a pace, and her balance comes back late

# claw
== moved | kind: claw | how: rock | where: standing
she twists the raked place away from the claws and turns back to face it
the drag of the claws pulls her shoulder round with them before they let go
she jerks her head back from the swipe and her chin tucks in

== moved | kind: claw | how: rock | where: down
the swipe drags her a little across the ground before the claws come free
she rolls a little from the rake and curls round the stinging place
she jerks on the ground as the claws catch, her body pulled half round

== moved | kind: claw | how: rock | where: held
she twists in the grip as the claws drag, and the grip hauls her straight again
the rake jerks her against the hold and she has nowhere to pull away to
the claws catch and pull, and the hold keeps her from giving with them

== moved | kind: claw | how: rock | where: holding
the claws rake her and she flinches, but her hold stays clamped
she ducks her head from the swipe without letting go
the rake twists her shoulder and her grip slides, then bites in again

== moved | kind: claw | how: stagger
she reels away from the rake, a step and then a half-step, clutching at the stinging place
the claws drag her off her line and she stumbles sideways after them
she jerks back so hard from the swipe that she trips over her own rear foot

# blade
== moved | kind: blade | how: rock | where: standing
she flinches from the edge, her body pulling into itself, then straightens
the cut turns her shoulder away and she brings it slowly back round
she sucks in a sharp breath and her body jerks back from the edge

== moved | kind: blade | how: rock | where: down
the edge catches her and she curls away from it, rolling onto her side
she twists on the ground from the slash, her limbs drawing in
the cut makes her body jerk tight where she lies

== moved | kind: blade | how: rock | where: held
the edge bites and she wrenches in the hold, and the hold takes the wrench
she jerks back from the cut and the grip catches her short
her whole body tightens in the hold as the edge passes over her

== moved | kind: blade | how: rock | where: holding
the edge rakes her and her grip loosens for a heartbeat before she clamps again
she hunches over her hold to shield herself from the cut, and keeps it
the slash jolts her, but whatever she holds stays held

== moved | kind: blade | how: stagger
she jerks back from the edge so fast that she staggers two steps
the cut drives her sideways, her feet scrambling to keep up
she lurches back from the slash and plants herself, low and wary

# bite
== moved | kind: bite | how: rock | where: standing
the jaws clamp and shake, and her whole body is jerked side to side with them
she wrenches back from the bite and it lets her go only when it wants to
the bite tugs her toward the biter and she leans back against the pull

== moved | kind: bite | how: rock | where: down
the jaws catch her and drag her a little way across the ground
she is shaken where she lies, the bite worrying at her
the bite pulls her half round on the ground before it lets go

== moved | kind: bite | how: rock | where: held
the bite jerks her in the hold and she can only twist, not pull away
the jaws worry her while she is held and her body jolts with each shake
she wrenches against the bite, and the hold yanks her straight again

== moved | kind: bite | how: rock | where: holding
the bite shakes her, but she tightens her own grip in answer
she is tugged sideways by the teeth, and she drags her hold along with her
she flinches round the bite with her grip still locked on

== moved | kind: bite | how: stagger
she tears herself loose from the bite and stumbles back from it
the jaws drag her forward a step before they let go, and she nearly goes down
the shake of the bite throws her off balance and she lurches sideways

# blast
== moved | kind: blast | how: rock | where: standing
the blast leans on her and she bows her head into it until it passes
her body is pressed back at the shoulders and holds there, straining, until the force runs out
she bends under the blast like grass under a gale and straightens after

== moved | kind: blast | how: rock | where: down
the blast pushes her along the ground a little way and she claws to stop
the force flattens her against the ground and pins her there till it passes
she is rolled half over by the blast and lies blinking at the dust it raised

== moved | kind: blast | how: rock | where: held
the blast pounds her in the hold, and she has nowhere to bend away to
the force jolts her and the one holding her together, and the hold barely shifts
she is pressed against whatever holds her as the blast breaks over her

== moved | kind: blast | how: rock | where: holding
the blast pushes against her and she hunches over her hold, keeping it
the force tries to pry her loose and her grip clenches against it
she braces her whole body round her hold as the blast hammers her

== moved | kind: blast | how: stagger
the blast drives her backward in a rush of scuffing steps
she is shoved across the ground by the force, digging in with every step
the blast spins her half round and she catches herself two steps on

# shock
== moved | kind: shock | how: rock | where: standing
the current jerks her upright and stiff, then lets her sag again
her body jolts in place, every limb snapping straight and then going slack
she twitches all over from the shock and rocks on locked joints

== moved | kind: shock | how: rock | where: down
the current makes her body jump on the ground, limbs jerking out
she spasms where she lies, the ground giving her nowhere to put the shock
her back arches off the ground for a heartbeat and drops

== moved | kind: shock | how: rock | where: held
the shock runs through her and into the hold, and both of them jerk
her body goes rigid in the grip and then shudders loose
the current locks her stiff in the hold, unable even to twist

== moved | kind: shock | how: rock | where: holding
the current locks her fingers or jaws tighter on her hold for a moment
the shock jolts her grip, and it clenches rather than loosens
her muscles seize round what she holds, and she cannot let go even if she wanted

== moved | kind: shock | how: stagger
the shock throws her limbs out and she reels back on stiff legs
her muscles jerk her a step to the side and she struggles to regain control
the current pitches her forward a pace before her body unlocks

# fire
== moved | kind: fire | how: rock | where: standing
she turns her face and shoulder from the heat and turns them back slowly
she hunches from the flame, drawing the burnt place in close
she flinches from the heat and her body twists half away before she squares up

== moved | kind: fire | how: rock | where: down
she rolls a little to smother the heat against the ground
the flame makes her writhe where she lies, twisting the burning place away
she curls tight on the ground away from the heat

== moved | kind: fire | how: rock | where: held
she writhes in the hold from the heat and can't get the burning part away
the flame washes over her while she is held, and she can only twist in place
she jerks against the grip, the singed place straining away from the heat

== moved | kind: fire | how: rock | where: holding
the heat makes her flinch, but she keeps her hold through it, eyes squeezed shut
she turns her face from the flame and her grip stays on
the fire licks over her and she tightens her hold against the urge to let go

== moved | kind: fire | how: stagger
she reels back from the heat, stumbling to get out of it
the flame drives her a step back and then another, face turned aside
she lurches away from the blaze, shaking the heat off the singed place

# cold
== moved | kind: cold | how: rock | where: standing
the cold stiffens her and she rocks in place, slow to bring herself back
her body clenches against the frost and she hunches small round it
she shudders hard from the chill and her shoulders draw up tight

== moved | kind: cold | how: rock | where: down
she curls on the ground against the cold, limbs drawn in
the frost makes her shiver where she lies, the shudders shaking her whole length
she jerks and stiffens on the cold ground

== moved | kind: cold | how: rock | where: held
the cold sets her stiff in the hold, and she can barely twitch
she shudders in the grip, her muscles locking against the frost
she strains against the hold with frozen limbs and gets nowhere

== moved | kind: cold | how: rock | where: holding
the chill stiffens her grip and she cannot tell if it is tighter or looser
she keeps her hold though her muscles are going slow and heavy with cold
the frost numbs her and her grip slips, then locks again

== moved | kind: cold | how: stagger
she staggers on stiffening legs, her balance thrown off by the cold
the frost makes her feet slow and clumsy and she stumbles a step
she lurches back from the cold, her movements jerky and slow

# mind
== moved | kind: mind | how: rock | where: standing
her head jerks as if slapped, but no one touched her
her head circles slowly on her neck, her eyes fixed on nothing
she rocks as though something unseen shoved her, then holds still, blinking

== moved | kind: mind | how: rock | where: down
her body jerks where she lies, her head turning to an unseen blow
she twists on the ground, clutching at her head with nothing there to fight
the force presses her flat and her eyes roll

== moved | kind: mind | how: rock | where: held
she goes slack in the hold for a heartbeat, her eyes empty, then jerks back
her head lolls in the grip and she fights to bring it back up
the unseen force wrenches at her and the hold is the only thing keeping her upright

== moved | kind: mind | how: rock | where: holding
her grip goes loose and dreamy for a moment and she catches it back
her hold stays on, but her head swims and her eyes lose focus
she clutches her hold the way someone clutches a railing when the ground spins

== moved | kind: mind | how: stagger
she staggers as if the ground has tipped under her, feet going the wrong way
she lurches a step, then two, her sense of direction gone
the unseen blow sends her reeling, one hand or paw clutching at her head

# ===============================================================================================================
# CRUSHED, more: pressed between the charger and something hard, for more bodies and sheaths
# ===============================================================================================================
== crushed
the hard surface at her back is cold and rough, and the weight grinds her into it
her breath comes in short, shallow sips between the shoves
her face is pressed sideways, one eye squeezed shut against the wall or bark
she feels every ridge of the surface behind her pressed into her skin
she pushes once with her whole body and the weight simply leans back in
her horn scrapes against the wall behind her as her head is forced back | has: horn
her tail is caught behind her, crushed between her and the hard thing | has: tail
her fins are folded flat and bent against the surface behind her | has: fins
her shell grinds against the surface behind her with a hollow scraping | has: shell
her spikes dig into whatever is behind her and lock her there, unable to slide | has: spikes
her mane is mashed flat and twisted between her neck and the surface | has: mane
her feathers are crushed and bent the wrong way against the hard surface | has: feathers
her flame is squashed and gutters against the surface behind her | has: flame
her antennae are bent back against the wall and quiver there | has: antennae
her crest is pressed flat and crooked against the surface | has: crest
her talons scratch uselessly at the ground beneath her | has: talons
her beak opens wide with nothing coming out | has: beak

== crushed | kind: fire
the fire of the charge breathes over her face and she cannot turn from it
her back is pressed into the surface while the flames on her front sizzle her skin

== crushed | kind: water
the water squeezes out from between them, pouring off her in sheets
she gulps for air and gets water, pressed too tight to cough it up

== crushed | kind: body
the bulk of the other body leans into her, warm and heavy and unmovable
the other's shoulder grinds into her chest and she can feel the bones of it

== crushed | state: fresh, winded
she braces her back against the surface and pushes with every muscle she has
she snarls through clenched teeth and shoves, gaining nothing but holding her own

== crushed | state: tired, spent
she presses back so weakly that it only seems to invite more weight
her breath comes and goes in thin, rattling wisps, and she cannot fill her chest

# ===============================================================================================================
# BODY TYPES: selling hits and hurt on specific bodies. Every entry is conditioned on `has:` so it only shows for a
# body that has that feature. Sized by size: (the blow), level: (the part's hurt) and state: (what is left of her).
# ===============================================================================================================

# WINGS: what a blow does to wings, how hurt pulls at them, how they show tiredness and pain
== impact_mark | has: wings
the blow catches the edge of a folded wing and it flicks half open before she clamps it back | size: glancing
her near wing jerks out from her side on the impact and settles back unevenly, not quite flat | size: solid
both wings jolt open with the force of it, rigid for a heartbeat, then fold in slow and crooked | size: heavy
the hit slams a wing flat against her side and the whole length of it shudders out to the tip | size: heavy
her wings fling wide on their own, as if to catch a wind that is not there, and hang trembling before they fold | size: tremendous
the gust of it bellies her wings out like sails and leaves them disordered along her back | kind: wind, beam

== sensation | has: wings
the hurt in her {part} pulls at the roots of her wings whenever she shifts them | level: sore, hurting
every small adjustment of her wings to keep her balance tugs on her {part} and sets it throbbing | level: hurting, very painful
the ache in her {part} has spread into her wing muscles until even folding them feels like lifting a weight | level: very painful, excruciating
her wings feel far away and heavy, as if the hurt in her {part} had drained the strength out of them | level: excruciating, devastated

== tell | has: wings
her wings sit tight and neat along her back, ready to snap open at a moment's notice | state: fresh
her wings ride a little open now to cool her, the tips lifting with each breath | state: winded
the tips of her wings have dropped low and she keeps hitching them back up into place | state: tired
her wings droop loose at her sides and the tips trail, too heavy to fold away | state: spent
the wing on the hurt side is held out from her body, stiff, as if it does not trust touching her | zone: arm | level: very painful, excruciating, devastated

== pain_show | has: wings
she draws her wings in a little tighter, then makes herself loosen them again | level: minor, sore | state: fresh, winded
a wing half-opens and curves over her {part}, sheltering it, and stays there while she breathes | level: hurting, very painful
her wings tremble along their whole length, a constant shiver she cannot settle | level: very painful, excruciating | state: tired, spent
she breathes in short pulls and her wings rise and fall with each one, the tips shaking | level: excruciating, devastated | state: spent

== reaction | has: wings
her wings give a single irritated rustle and fold flat again | size: glancing
her wings jolt open to the wrist and she snaps them shut on a hiss of breath | size: solid, heavy
the hit barely registers; her wings never even leave her sides | size: solid | state: fresh
her wings flare out wide and stay there, shaking, while she drags in air | size: tremendous
her wings sag open with the blow and she cannot seem to fold them back | size: heavy, tremendous | state: tired, spent

== moved | has: wings
the blow turns her and her wings open a little on their own to steady her where she stands | how: rock | where: standing
she is driven back with her wings beating wildly, clawing at the air for a balance the ground won't give | how: stagger
her wings slap once against the ground as the blow rocks her where she lies | how: rock | where: down

== landing | has: wings
she comes down with her wings thrown wide, and they smack the ground a heartbeat before the rest of her

== getup | has: wings
she plants the bend of each wing on the ground and pushes, levering herself up off it

== dodge | has: wings
one hard beat of her wings hops her clear, and the blow passes through the space she left

== self_look | has: wings | zone: arm
she fans the wing out at her side and looks along it, testing each joint by moving it

== held_in | has: wings
her wings clench tight against her body, and that is all the sound she allows herself | state: fresh, winded
her wings shake against her sides with the effort of keeping quiet | state: tired, spent

she shakes out a paw as if flicking off water | size: glancing

# --- situational story blocks, second pass: more variety, more selling, more struggle ---

# off_target (the attacker): the blow lands, but not where she meant it
== off_target
she lines it up on the sore place and the other turns just enough; it lands on the hard part beside it
the target slides away under her blow like a fish under a hand, and she gets the edge of it instead
she aims low and the other hunches, and the blow thumps into a shoulder that was not there a moment ago
her strike finds its mark a little high, a little wide, and she feels the difference all the way up her limb
she wanted the soft middle and gets the muscle next to it, firm and ready for her
the other's chin comes down and soaks up the blow meant for the throat below it
a half turn from the other, and her best blow spends itself on the meaty side instead of the hurt one
she sees her aim spoiled a heartbeat before contact and drives through anyway, landing on whatever is there
the blow goes in true and arrives late, and the place it was meant for has already moved on
she catches more of the guard than the body, and only the tail end of it gets through
her timing is right and her line is wrong; the hit glances onto the next part over
the other flinches the hurt part away and she buries the blow in the healthy one beside it

# off_target_take (the one hit): she moves where it lands, not whether
== off_target_take
she drops her chin and the blow meant for her throat thuds into her jaw instead
a hunch of the shoulders and the hit lands high on the muscle, not on the sore place under it
she turns the hurt side away and gives the blow her other flank to chew on
she cannot get out of it, so she chooses what takes it, and chooses the part that is still sound
the strike was aimed at the place that hurts most and lands a hand's width off, on the part that hurts least
she curls just enough to spoil its aim, and pays for it on a part she had been keeping safe
she lifts a limb across herself and the blow finds that instead of the soft place behind it
a jerk of the head, and the hit meant for her eye skids across her brow
she sucks her middle in and the blow catches the hard edge of her ribs rather than the hollow below
she takes it, but on her own terms: the blow lands where she has braced, not where it was sent
she twists from the waist and it lands broadside, spread out and blunter than it was meant to be

# collapse_thought (the one watching the other fall)
== collapse_thought
*Don't move. Don't move.* she does not know which of them she is telling
*Is she breathing? She's breathing. Good. Stay there.*
*That's not a feint. Her eyes aren't even open.*
*Watch the limbs. If one of them moves, go.*
*It's over. Isn't it? Is it over?*
*I can't go again. Please don't get up.*
*Look at her. Look what it took.*
*Wait. Count her breaths. Then believe it.*
*Too easy. Nothing about her has been easy.*
*She's done. She has to be done.*
she holds her breath with the other's, as if the next one decides everything
she keeps her weight forward, ready to go in, and hopes she will not have to
a strange tightness in her throat as she watches the other go down, nothing she has a word for
her own body wants to fold in sympathy, and she will not let it

# last_stand (one final everything)
== last_stand
she stops guarding the hurt places; there is no point protecting what she is about to spend
her breath goes in deep, all the way down past the ache, and comes out as movement
she lets the pain have her voice and keeps everything else for the blow
there is a kind of calm in it: nothing left to decide, only the one thing to do
she finds she is snarling, or smiling, as she goes in, and cannot tell which
the trembling in her limbs stops for the length of the effort and starts again the instant it is done
she hurls herself forward with no plan for what happens if it misses
she goes in low and ugly, all weight and no grace, the way a spent body really fights
her eyes are wet and wide and fixed on one place, and she does not blink once on the way there
it is the biggest thing she has thrown all fight, and she throws it with nothing behind it but will
she draws herself up from the slump all at once, and the other sees the change before she moves
every hurt part screams at her to stop and she answers by going faster
she gathers the last of herself into her chest, holds it a heartbeat, and lets it all go at once
a gasp, a lunge, a cry torn ragged at the edges: everything she has, aimed at one place
she comes in so hard that her own body shudders with it, as much hit as hitter
there is nothing careful left in her; she spends breath, guard and balance all in the one go
the effort is so total that her eyes squeeze shut at the moment it leaves her
she spends the last stubborn spark in her the way a fire flares on its last log: all at once, bright
the shout goes up out of her and cracks at the top, and she is already committed
she throws herself at it as if the other were a wall she means to go through
afterwards there will be the shaking and the empty lungs; for now there is only this
she pushes off from her exhaustion like a swimmer pushing off a wall, and it carries her

# frenzy (a flurry of blows, one after another)
== frenzy
she hits and hits, and the sound of it becomes a fast, flat beat in her ears
there is no breath between the blows, only the next one already on its way
her eyes narrow to slits and stay that way while the strikes keep falling
she stops seeing the other's face and sees only the spot, opening and closing under her blows
each blow jars back up her own body, and she uses the jar to throw the next one
her mouth is open, panting, and a sound comes out on every strike that she does not hear
the strikes come faster than her thinking can follow, and she lets them
she can hear the other's breath being knocked out in pieces, one piece per blow
her limbs ache, burn, go past burning into something hot and loose, and she keeps going
she leans into it, crowding close, giving the other no room to fold away from the blows
the rhythm speeds up as she goes, not slows, as if the hitting itself were feeding her
somewhere in the middle she forgets what she is angry at and only remembers being angry
she hits the same hurt place so often it stops being a target and becomes a habit
she spits a breath out with each strike, hard and short, like someone chopping wood
the blows lose their shape and keep their weight, crude, fast, relentless
she hears herself snarling and does not recognise the sound
her strikes start to land on top of one another, each burying the last
she does not stop when the other sags; she stops only when her own limbs do
it goes on until her body chooses to end it, not her
her vision is a tunnel and at the end of the tunnel is the place she keeps hitting
the effort pours sweat and heat off her, and still the blows come
she forgets to guard, forgets to breathe, forgets everything but the next blow and the next

# anticipate (the hurt one sees it coming at her sore place)
== anticipate
her eyes squeeze shut a heartbeat before it arrives, and she hates herself for it
she sucks in a sharp breath and holds it, every muscle braced round the place she knows it is going
a thin whimper slips out of her as she sees where the blow is headed
she hunches over the sore place and her whole body goes tight as a fist
she sees it coming for the hurt and her breath catches in a little hitch, high in her throat
the sore part twitches under her skin before anything has touched it
she tucks her head and pulls the hurt part in, the way a cold animal tucks itself up
she cannot stop her eyes going to the blow and then to the hurt, back and forth, the fear plain
her mouth tightens, her eyes narrow, and she braces the way you brace for cold water
the old ache wakes up and starts throbbing as if it heard the blow coming
she flinches so hard she nearly spoils her own footing, and it hits her anyway
she curls her body round the sore place, making the rest of her a shield for it
a small helpless sound gets out of her, half breath, half word, before the impact
she tightens over the hurt so hard her own muscles start to tremble with it
the instant she sees the angle, she knows exactly how much it will hurt, and her face shows it
everything she has left goes into pulling the hurt part out of the line, and it is not enough
she shrinks smaller, as if being smaller would help, her eyes fixed on the blow
she braces so early that she is already shaking by the time it lands
her breath stops, her eyes close, and she simply waits for it
she twists her sore side away and leaves her good side hanging out, and the blow follows the twist anyway
her ears flatten tight to her skull as she watches it come for the hurt | has: ears
her feathers clamp down hard over the sore place, as if that could stop it | has: feathers
she draws a wing tight across the hurt place, trembling, a thin shield | has: wings
the coils nearest the hurt draw in and tighten before she has decided anything | has: coils
she flicks her tail in under herself, guarding, the movement small and frightened | has: tail
her hood flares wide in alarm and then folds tight as the blow comes in | has: hood
she curls a forearm in across the hurt part and grits her teeth behind it | has: arms
her spikes bristle up all over her as the blow comes, a body trying to look sharper than it feels | has: spikes
her mane rises and flattens again, and she shrinks behind it | has: mane
she ducks her head behind her horn as if it could catch the whole blow | has: horn

# anticipate_seen (the attacker sees her protect it)
== anticipate_seen
she watches the shoulders hunch round one spot and has her target before she has decided to strike
the other's breath catches as she shapes the blow, and the catch tells her she is right
she sees the eyes squeeze shut early and puts the blow exactly where the fear is
the hurt part is tucked in so carefully that the care itself marks it, plain as a painted line
she sees the trembling start before she has even touched the place
she changes the angle at the last moment to go round the guard, straight onto the sore part
the flinch is so big it opens everything else, and she takes her pick
she sees the other's whole body lean away from one side, and goes to that side
she watches the guard hover over the one place, never leaving it, and smiles inside
she sees a whimper shape the other's mouth before the blow lands, and does not soften it
the hurt is guarded so hard the rest of the body is forgotten, and she uses both
she reads the flinch like a word spoken aloud: here, it says, here
*Holding it like that? Then that's where it goes.*
*She's already hurting. Just finish what's started.*
*Every time I move she curls round that spot.*
*Scared. She's scared of it. I can see it in her eyes.*
*She's trying not to show me. Too late.*
she marks the way the other breathes high and shallow to spare the sore place, and aims under the breath
she feels a small cold satisfaction as the hurt part twitches away from her
she sees the eyes flick to her limb and away, measuring, dreading, and she uses that dread
she lets the blow go slow at first, to watch the other cringe, and then lets it fall
the guarded side is too guarded, and she knows exactly why

# juggle (knocked up into the air; caught in the air by a second blow)
== juggle | moment: up
the blow comes from below and she feels herself rise, the ground sliding away under her
her breath leaves her at the top of the arc, and she has no idea where it went
everything she was braced against is suddenly gone, and her body has nothing to hold on to
she hangs for a heartbeat with her limbs spread wide, eyes wide, mouth open on a gasp
the air rushes past her face and the ground is far below, wrong side up
she rises with her body curled round the place she was struck, too hurt to uncurl
she is light as a leaf for a moment, and the lightness is the most frightening thing yet
her stomach lurches and the whole place swings round her in a slow wheel
she sees her opponent below her, small and waiting, and understands what the waiting means
the blow throws her up and her own weight turns her over, helpless as a dropped stone
she reaches out for anything to hold and closes on empty air
she tries to twist in the air and her body only flails, slow and useless

== juggle | moment: caught
the blow meets her at the top of her rise and folds her in half in the air
she has no weight to give with, no ground to take it, so it takes all of her
it lands while she is still helpless and she feels every bit of it, nothing spared
the second strike drives the breath out of her that the first had only shaken loose
caught with nothing under her, she simply goes where the blow sends her, limp as a thrown rag
she is hit in the air and turned right over, and the ground rushes at her from a new direction
the strike catches her open and spread and she cannot even curl round it
she cries out in mid-air, a sound cut short by the second impact
she is batted down out of the air and the landing is the second half of the blow
she sees it coming and can do nothing about it but close her eyes
the force reverses her rise in an instant and she drops like something thrown
she takes the strike flat across her body, unguarded, and the air leaves her in one gasp

# sputter (water forced into her)
== sputter
she coughs until her eyes squeeze shut and tears run with the water
she bends over and the water pours out of her nose and mouth, and she gasps after it
every gasp pulls in more spray, and every cough throws it back out
she chokes, gags, and her whole body heaves to get the water out of her chest
her breath comes in short, wet snatches between the coughs
she shakes her head violently, spraying water, trying to clear her nose
the cough is so deep it hurts her ribs and leaves her trembling
she hacks up water and then can only stand there, panting and dripping
there is water in her ears, her eyes, her throat, and she cannot get it out fast enough
she wheezes, and the wheeze is wet, and she coughs again to clear it
her eyes are red and streaming and she can hardly see the other through them
she blows the water out of her nose in a hard snort and coughs again straight after
she cannot stop coughing; it comes in fits, each worse than the last
a long, gurgling cough doubles her over, and she spits and gasps when it ends
she breathes in carefully, afraid of the next cough, and the next cough comes anyway
her throat is raw from coughing and still it will not stop
she tastes the water in the back of her throat and gags on it
she tries to hold her breath against the coughing and it bursts out of her anyway
the water has gone down the wrong way and her body fights her to get it back up
her chest heaves and shudders, and between heaves she manages one thin breath
she squeezes her eyes shut and coughs until her sides ache
the coughing leaves her limp and blinking, water dripping from her chin
she sneezes, coughs, sneezes again, and her eyes water with each one
she shakes the water from her feathers and coughs, hunched and miserable | has: feathers
water streams off her fins and she gasps like something pulled out of the water | has: fins
the water runs out of her mane in streams as she coughs and shakes | has: mane

# feint (fooled by a fake; or seeing through it)
== feint | moment: bit
she jerks her guard up to meet nothing, and the real blow walks in under it
a flick of the other's eyes sends her weight the wrong way, and the strike is waiting there
she blocks the empty air with everything she has, and has nothing left for what comes
she sees the limb come up and braces for it, and it never lands; something else does
she ducks the fake and ducks straight into the real one
she flinches from a twitch, and the twitch was the whole point
she turns her shoulder to take a blow that changes its mind halfway
she reads the other's lean and believes it, and the lean was a lie
she gets her guard exactly where it was invited to go
a feint at her hurt side, and she curls round it, and the open side pays
her body answers the bluff before her mind can stop it
the fake comes so close to her face that she shuts her eyes, and that is all the other needs
she steps into the threat that is not there and leaves herself wide for the one that is

== feint | moment: read
she watches the other's hips, not the limb, and the hips have not moved
the eyes give it away: they look at one place and the shoulders go for another
she lets the fake go past her face without a twitch and waits
she has felt that shape once already this fight and her body refuses to answer it again
she sees the weight is still back on the heels and knows the strike cannot come from there
she holds her ground, and the feint dies in the air between them
she gives the bluff a small, false flinch of her own and meets the real blow head on
the move is too pretty to be real, and she trusts the plain thing behind it
she does not move her guard; she only moves her eyes, and her eyes find the real one
she lets the other waste the trick on her stillness
she breathes out slow and stays put while the fake twitches and fades
the feint asks her a question and she declines to answer it
she reads the setup and is already turned to meet the real strike when it comes

# crash (her charge misses and she hits something hard)
== crash
her momentum carries her past the target and into the hard thing beyond it with a jolt that rattles her skull
she hits so hard the breath leaves her in a grunt and her eyes water
she rebounds off it and sprawls, limbs everywhere, the world ringing
she meets it with the side of her head and for a moment everything goes bright and thin and ringing
the shock of it runs down her spine and into every limb
she clings to the thing she hit for a moment, dazed, before she can push away
she bounces off and has to throw out a limb to stay upright
she hits it chest first and folds over it, gasping
the whole thing shudders with her weight, and so does she
she slides down it slowly, shaking her head, the miss still in her eyes
the impact jars her teeth together so hard she tastes it
she comes off it with a fresh scrape along her side and a temper to match
her own speed comes back at her all at once, and she has nowhere to put it
she hits, gasps, and hangs there a heartbeat, every muscle gone stiff with the shock
she pulls back from it slow and careful, testing the shoulder that took it
the noise of the crash bounces round the place long after she has stopped moving
she shakes off the daze in quick hard jerks, ashamed to have been seen like that
for a heartbeat she does not know if she has hit or been hit
she smacks into it flank first and the air whooshes out of her
she scrabbles at it, dazed, to get off it and turn | has: legs
she crumples against it and has to push herself off with both arms | has: arms
her wings snap open on instinct as she hits, too late to slow anything | has: wings
her coils pile up against it behind her, her own length crashing into itself | has: coils
her horn bites into the thing she hit and she has to wrench her head free | has: horn
her shell takes it with a hollow knock, and she is rattled inside it | has: shell
her feathers burst loose at the impact and drift round her | has: feathers

# escape_after (just free of a pin)
== escape_after
she lies there for one breath after the weight leaves, unable to believe it is gone
she drags in air so deep it makes her cough, and then drags in more
the first thing she does free is curl round the place that was pressed hardest
she gets clear and her whole body trembles, the held-in strain finally let out
she works her jaw, rolls her neck, and every movement finds a new ache from the hold
the ground where she lay is warm and pressed flat, and she does not want to look at it
she is out, and the out is the only thing she can think about for a moment
she shivers, though she is not cold; it is the hold leaving her body
she keeps blinking, trying to bring the place back into proper focus after the weight
she feels lighter than she should, almost dizzy, as though the weight had become part of her
she sucks air through her teeth as the feeling rushes back into the squashed places, hot and prickling
the relief is so big it comes out of her as a sound, half sob, half growl
she gets her breath back in big shuddering pulls and does not care how it looks
her limbs feel wrong for a moment, as if they belong to someone who was held much longer
she tests one limb, then another, as though counting them after the weight
she puts the length of the place between them and only then lets herself breathe out
the pressed places tingle and burn as the feeling comes back into them
she glares at the other, breathing hard, with the memory of the weight still on her face
she does not go straight back in; she gives herself three breaths, and takes them all
her muscles twitch in odd places as the cramp of the hold lets go
she brushes the dirt off her side where she was ground into it, and winces at the bruises underneath
she wants to sag and does not let herself; not with the other still standing there
every heartbeat she is free she can feel in her chest, hard and fast and grateful
she keeps one eye on the other's limbs, the ones that held her, and gives them room
the hold leaves an ache along her back that she will feel with every move after
her eyes are watering and she blinks it away, angry at herself for it
she shakes out her ruffled feathers, every one of them crushed out of place | has: feathers
she flexes her wings once, slowly, checking they still open | has: wings

# devastating (why the blow was so severe)
== devastating | reason: position
she had just shifted her weight onto the one limb that could not take it, and the blow came at that instant
the blow found her stretched out mid-reach, long and thin and open, with nothing tucked in to guard
she was twisted round looking the other way, and her body had no idea it was coming

# clash (two attacks meet head on)
== clash
the two forces lock in the space between them, humming, and neither will give a hair
her attack shudders against the other's like two rams with horns locked, and she leans in harder
the shock comes back into her own chest so hard it knocks a grunt out of her
for a heartbeat the light between them is so bright she has to squint through it
the meeting point throws off sparks, steam and grit in a fan that stings her face
she feels the other's strength through the meeting, pushing, pushing, and pushes back
the air pops in her ears as the two meet
the backlash runs up her own body and rattles her teeth
both attacks bend and twist against each other like two ropes pulling different ways
she grits her teeth and holds, eyes watering in the glare
the ground between them shakes and a crack of sound rolls out across the place
the push of it ruffles everything on her, from head to back
when the two forces break apart she is breathing hard, as if she had wrestled
the heat or the cold or the glare of the meeting washes over both of them at once
she gets a heartbeat's look at the other's face through the light, straining just as hard
the forces grind against each other, and she can feel her own strength draining into it
the meeting tears a hollow in the ground beneath it
she sets herself and shoves her will into the attack, and it holds, barely
the two attacks cancel each other in a burst that blows dust back over them both
for one long breath it is pure strength against strength, nothing clever about it
she feels the other give a little, then come back, then give again
the backwash slaps into her and she has to close her eyes against it
the clash leaves both of them blinking at a ragged scar on the ground between them
her limbs shake with the strain of holding her attack against the other's
a high ringing hangs in the air after the meeting and does not fade quickly
she flinches at the noise of it, a crack loud enough to feel
spray, ash, light and grit all burst out together, and both of them are caught in it
she pours more into it and feels the other do the same, and the middle swells, bright and loud
the two forces meet and spill sideways, scouring the ground in a long ragged line
her feet dig in as the meeting pushes her back by a hair | has: legs

# weather (rain, sun, hail coming in)
== weather | weather: rain
the first heavy drops smack the ground and then it is all coming down at once
rain runs off her head and into her eyes and she blinks it away, again and again
the ground churns to mud and the footing slides with every shift

the cold of the soaking settles into her and she shivers between moves

== weather | weather: hail

# opening (the start of the fight, before anything happens)
== opening
each of them takes the measure of the other, top to bottom, without hurrying
one of them shifts her weight, and the other's eyes follow it
the air between them feels heavy, as if the place itself were holding its breath
one of them draws a long breath and lets it out slowly, and the other hears it
a small breeze moves through and stirs both of them, and neither notices
the place is quiet except for the sound of two creatures breathing
one of them looks away for half a heartbeat, and the other notices
they stand far enough apart to talk, close enough to be ready
the light falls between them, splitting the space in two
one of them flexes something, a limb, a jaw, a muscle, and lets it settle
each of them seems to be listening for the other to make the first sound
one of them lowers her head a fraction, a small and meaningful thing
the ground between them is smooth and unmarked, and will not stay that way
they hold each other's gaze until it becomes a contest in itself
one of them settles low and still, and the other paces, restless
a bird calls somewhere, or water drips, and neither of them looks toward it
one of them is bigger, and both of them know it
one of them sizes up the other's reach, then looks at her own
the waiting goes on long enough that it starts to hurt
each of them chooses where to stand, and the choosing says something
the place smells of earth and damp and something else, something sharper
they circle slowly, each keeping the same distance, as if tied together
one of them blinks first, and pretends it does not matter
the stillness before the first move is so tight it almost hums
each of them breathes quietly, saving everything for what comes
one of them tests the ground with her weight, pressing, checking
the other's eyes are steady and give nothing away
both of them are still for a long moment, and then one of them is not
neither has yet decided to begin, and both are ready to
the light catches in one pair of eyes and makes them gleam

# knockout (how she goes out) - per moment, and general
== knockout | moment: instant
the blow lands and her eyes go blank in the same heartbeat, as if a lamp had been blown out
her body drops straight down, nothing reaching out, nothing catching
she is out before the sound of the blow has faded
the strike turns her head and the rest of her simply follows, slack

she hits the ground already gone, not even flinching at the landing
the blow lands, her eyes close, and she sags down in the same motion
her body goes slack and pours down to the ground in one smooth fall
the strike finds her and every muscle she has quits together

== knockout | moment: crumple

she leans, leans further, and then slides down to the ground
her body crumples in on itself and lies still, curled

she drops to the ground in a slow heap and does not try to rise

she sinks lower and lower, until there is no lower to go
she tries once to stay up, and the try ends with her lying down
her strength drains away a little at a time, and she goes down with it

== knockout | moment: fade
her eyelids droop, lift once, droop again, and stay down
her breathing slows and her body sags, the fight seeping out of her
the edges of the world go soft and grey, then closer, then over her
she sways gently, eyes losing focus, and then lists down to the ground
the sounds of the place drift far away, and she drifts after them
she blinks slowly, and the next blink does not end

her eyes drift closed as if she were falling asleep standing

she pushes up again and again, each time weaker
she thrashes, wild and desperate, trying to break the hold

# constriction (squeezed in coils)
== constriction
the coils tighten a little more every time she breathes out
she feels her ribs pressed in from every side at once
the squeeze is everywhere, steady and slow and patient
she can feel her own heartbeat against the coils
she pushes against the loops and they press back harder
the coils shift and settle, finding new places to press
her breath comes short and shallow, all she can manage
the scales rasp against her as the coils tighten

== constriction | fade: fighting
she squirms against the coils, looking for any slack
she strains with all her strength, and the coils hold firm
she bites at the nearest loop and the squeeze tightens in answer
she gulps air while she still can and fights on
she twists in the coils, refusing to go still
she pushes outward, her face tight with effort

== constriction | fade: weakening
her struggles come slower and weaker, with longer rests between them
she gasps for every breath and gets less each time
her limbs feel heavy and slow, as if the squeeze had filled them with sand
the fight is going out of her, bit by bit
she pushes against the loops, and the push is so feeble the coils hardly notice
her eyes start to droop and she forces them open

== constriction | fade: fading
she can barely breathe at all, only small sips past the weight on her ribs
her eyes are half closed and unfocused, drifting over nothing
her limbs hang limp in the coils, trembling now and then
the world is going quiet and far away
her struggles have shrunk to small twitches she hardly knows she is making
she can hardly feel the squeeze any more

== constriction | fade: nearly out
her head lolls and her eyes slide shut
her breath is barely a whisper, and each one ends in a tiny whimper
she hangs limp in the coils, only her heartbeat still going
she makes no move at all against the squeeze
the coils hold her and she does not fight them any more
a shallow breath, a long pause, then another shallow breath, each one smaller in the coils

# crushed (pinned between a charging body and something hard)
== crushed
she is pressed flat between the two and the breath goes out of her in a long wheeze
the hard thing behind her bites into her back with every push
she is squeezed so tight her ribs ache and her chest cannot rise
she can feel every bump and edge of the surface behind her
the push goes on and on and she can only take it
her head is pressed sideways against the surface, her cheek scraping
she gasps for air and gets only a little
she squirms but there is nowhere to go
her limbs are trapped against her and cannot move
she groans long and low as the pressure builds and builds
the weight in front of her leans in harder
she is held between the two, pressed and helpless
she grits her teeth and her eyes squeeze shut against the pressure

== crushed | kind: fire
the heat of the charge sears against her front while her back is pressed to the surface
the flames lick at her and she cannot pull away
her fur singes where she is pressed against the burning body
she gasps and the hot air burns her throat

== crushed | kind: water
the water of the charge floods over her face and she chokes on it
she is pressed under the water and against the surface, and cannot breathe
the water pours over her and she sputters and gasps
she is soaked and squeezed, and cannot tell which is worse

== crushed | kind: body
the bulk of the other leans into her, all muscle and weight
she can feel the other's heartbeat through the press
the other's body is hot and heavy against her
she is pressed into the surface by sheer weight

== crushed | state: fresh, winded
she shoves back against the press with all her strength
she grits her teeth and refuses to be flattened
she squirms and fights, looking for any gap
she glares at the one doing it, eyes blazing
she takes a breath and holds it, bracing
she growls through clenched teeth, the sound squeezed thin by the press

== crushed | state: tired, spent
she goes limp and lets the weight have her
a weak whimper escapes her each time the weight leans in again
her eyes close and she can only endure
her limbs hang slack and she cannot push back
she gasps, thin and weak, and the press lets almost none of it in
she sags against the surface and shakes, small helpless tremors all through her

# guard (block, reflect, catch, turn)
== guard | moment: block
the attack splashes against the barrier and runs off it like rain off glass
the shield shivers and rings, and inside it she stays perfectly still
the blow stops short as though it had hit a wall of air
the barrier glows bright where the attack meets it, then fades
she watches the attack crumble against her guard without a flicker
the force spreads across the shield and drains away harmlessly
her opponent's best effort ends in a flat, dull thud against nothing
the barrier holds, and she lets out a slow breath behind it
the strike glances off the shield in a shower of light
the shield takes it, and she does not even blink
the blow hangs against the barrier for a heartbeat before it dies
the shield shimmers once, settles, and is gone, its work done

== guard | moment: reflect
the strike bounces back off her and slams into her opponent instead
she takes the force and turns it round, sending it home
the blow rebounds with all its strength into the one who threw it
her opponent staggers as her own blow comes back at her
the attack flashes back along its path, and her opponent cannot dodge it
she lets it land and gives it right back, every bit of it
the force reverses and her opponent takes the full weight of her own move
the strike turns in the air and comes home to its owner
she catches the blow's force and throws it back twice as fast
her opponent's attack rebounds into her face, and she gasps at it
she does nothing but stand there, and the blow goes back the way it came
her opponent is hit by her own attack, and the surprise on her face is plain

== guard | moment: catch
she gets her guard up just in time and the blow slams into it
she catches it on her shoulder, and the shock runs down her side
she blocks it hard and grunts with the effort
the guard takes the hit, and the force numbs her limb all the way to the shoulder
she braces and takes it on her guard, teeth gritted
the blow jars her guard and her limb throbs from it
she catches it and holds, though it hurts
her guard shudders under the blow but holds
she blocks it with a hard thump, and shakes out the sting
she takes it on the guard and feels the jolt of it all the way up into her shoulder
she catches the blow on her horn and pushes back against it | has: horn
she blocks it with a wing, and the feathers shudder | has: wings
she blocks with her arm and winces as it lands | has: arms
she takes it on her coils and they ripple with the force | has: coils
she catches it on her shell, and it rings with a hollow knock | has: shell
she blocks it with her tail, and the tail throbs | has: tail

== guard | moment: turn
she deflects it with a quick twist, and it slides off
she turns it aside, and it misses her completely
she redirects the blow and it goes past her harmlessly
she guides it away with a touch, and it spends itself on empty air
she angles her guard, and the blow skids off it
she meets it at a slant and it glances away
she parries it neatly and lets the force run off past her shoulder
she turns the blow, and her opponent stumbles past
she sends it off course with a flick, and it misses wide
she lets it slide along her guard and off into nothing
she brushes it aside like an insect, almost lazily, and that stings the other worse
she turns it with her horn, and it glances off | has: horn
she flicks it aside with her wing | has: wings
she sweeps it away with her tail | has: tail
she turns it off her coils, and it slides away | has: coils

# status_seen (the attacker seeing the status as an opening)
== status_seen | status: flinched
she sees the flinch and goes in while the other is still frozen
the other's eyes are screwed shut, and she uses every heartbeat of it
*She froze. Now.*
she sees the head turned away and goes for the side it has left open
the flinch lasts a breath too long, and she spends that breath well

== status_seen | status: dazed
she sees the glazed eyes and the slow blink, and closes the distance
the other is looking a little to one side of her, and she goes in on the other side
*She can't see me properly. Go.*
she watches the other shake her head, slow and lost, and moves before it clears
she sees the other's eyes swim and strikes before they settle

== status_seen | status: off_balance
she sees the weight on the wrong foot and drives in on that side
the other is still finding her balance, and she does not let her find it
*She's tipping. Push.*
she sees the lean and leans into it with a blow
she catches the other mid-wobble and goes for the open side

== status_seen | status: doubled_over
she sees the other folded over and goes for the bowed head and back
*She can't straighten. Hit her now.*
the guard is gone, both limbs clutching the hurt, and she takes the gap
she sees the other bent double, breathing at the ground, and strikes from above
she sees the other hunched and helpless and does not hesitate

== status_seen | status: breathless
she hears the other gasping for air and moves in while she cannot
*She's got nothing. No breath, no fight.*
she sees the mouth open on nothing and closes in
she watches the chest heave and strikes before the breath comes back
she knows a body with no breath cannot block, and goes straight in

== status_seen | status: sputtering
she sees the other choking on water and closes while she is coughing
*She can't even see. Go.*
she watches the eyes stream and the cough bend the other over, and strikes
she waits for the next cough and goes in on it
she sees the other spitting water, blind and helpless for a heartbeat, and takes it

== status_seen | status: stiff
she sees how slow the other is to turn and comes in from that side
the other moves like a creature carved of wood, and she makes her move twice
*She can't bend. Go round her.*
she times her strike to the other's stiffness and lands before the guard can come

== status_seen | status: cramped
she sees the cramp lock the limb and goes for that side
the other is fighting her own muscles, and she uses it
*Her body's against her. Good.*
she sees the limb pulled in tight and strikes where it should be guarding

== status_seen | status: bound
she feels the held limb weaken and tightens her grip
*That limb is done. Work on the rest.*
she sees the other try to use the bound limb and fail, and moves on that side
she presses the advantage while the limb is useless

== status_seen | status: paralyzed
she sees the twitching limbs and the locked jaw and goes straight in
*She can't move. Now.*
the sparks still crawling on the other mean she has a heartbeat, and she takes it
she sees the other seize up and closes the distance at once

== status_seen | status: confused
she sees the other turn to face the wrong way and goes in behind her
*She doesn't know where I am.*
she watches the other sway, lost, and picks her moment
she sees the confusion and slips in where the other is not looking

== status_seen | status: constricted
she feels the other weaken in the coils and tightens them a little more
*She's fading. Hold on.*
she feels the struggles slow and squeezes gently, patiently
she feels the breath go out of the other and takes up the slack

== status_seen | status: staggered
she sees the other knocked out of her stance and follows her in
*She's off her feet. Follow her.*
she sees the stumble and goes after it before the other can plant
she follows the stagger in, keeping her off balance

== status_seen | status: opening
she sees the guard come down and drives into the space
the opening is there for half a breath, and she fills it
*There. Now.*
she slips her blow into the gap before it closes
she does not think; she just goes where the opening is

# status_tell (how the status shows on her body) - 8 or more for each status the program uses
== status_tell | status: flinched
her eyes clamp shut and her head jerks aside, and for a heartbeat she cannot make it come back
she freezes mid-movement, every muscle locked, waiting for a blow that is not coming
her whole body twitches away from the hit and stays curled for a breath too long
she hunches, shoulders up round her ears, and only slowly lets them down | has: ears
her breath stops and her limbs lock, and she has to force them loose again
she cringes from it, and the cringe will not let go of her
her face screws up tight and she cannot open her eyes for a moment
she goes rigid, quivering, the way a startled animal goes still

== status_tell | status: dazed
her eyes drift and lose their focus, then slowly find it again
she sways gently on the spot, blinking, her head tilted
the place swims round her and she cannot make it stand still
she shakes her head slowly, eyes half closed, and the fog does not lift
her gaze wanders past her opponent and has to be hauled back
she blinks hard, again and again, as if trying to clear water from her eyes
her mouth hangs a little open and her eyes are far away
everything comes to her a beat late, sounds and shapes both

== status_tell | status: off_balance
her weight lurches the wrong way and she flails to catch it
she tips sideways and has to throw her weight back the other way
her balance is gone, and she wobbles with her limbs flung out to catch it
she overreaches and her body follows, too far
she rocks on the spot, trying to find her center
she leans too far and has to scramble to stay up
her footing slips and she stumbles, catching herself just in time | has: legs
she totters on the spot, every part of her working to stay upright

== status_tell | status: doubled_over
she bends double round the hurt, gasping, head hanging
she cannot straighten; her body folds in on itself and stays folded
her limbs curl in to clutch the hurt and her guard is gone
she hunches over, breathing hard at the ground, eyes squeezed shut
she rocks a little, bent over, a low groan coming out of her
she clutches her middle and cannot raise her head, breath coming in groans
she curls tight round the hurt and stays there, trembling
she bends so far her head nearly touches the ground

== status_tell | status: breathless
she gasps and gasps, and the air will not come
her mouth opens and closes, trying to drag in a breath
her chest heaves, but nothing seems to go in
she wheezes, a thin desperate sound, and her eyes go wide
she stands there gulping at air like a fish on land
her ribs jerk with empty pulls, and her eyes water
she cannot breathe, and the panic of it shows in her face
she hunches, mouth open, waiting for her breath to come back

== status_tell | status: stiff
her limbs move slow and wooden, as if they had forgotten how
she tries to turn and her body answers late, stiff in every joint
she flexes the limb that was held and it barely bends
her movements are jerky and clumsy, nothing flowing
she shakes out her limbs and the stiffness clings anyway
every move she makes is a beat behind where it should be
the numbness tingles in her limbs, slow to clear
she winces as she moves, every joint grinding

== status_tell | status: bound
the held limb goes numb and heavy, the feeling draining out of it
she pulls at the trapped limb and it will not come
the grip squeezes tighter and she gasps, the trapped limb throbbing in it
the limb tingles, then burns, then goes cold
she strains against the hold and her limb trembles with it
she cannot feel the limb properly, only the grip on it
the bound limb hangs heavy and useless, prickling, the fingers of feeling gone out of it
she twists against the grip and gets nowhere, the limb going slack

== status_tell | status: sputtering
she coughs and chokes, bent over, water pouring from her nose and mouth
she gasps between coughs, eyes streaming, every breath wet and thin
she spits and coughs and spits again, trying to breathe
she shakes her head hard, coughing, water flying
the coughing bends her over and will not stop
she wheezes and gags, eyes squeezed shut, water running off her chin
her breath gurgles and she coughs to clear it
she chokes on the water, eyes running, unable to see

== status_tell | status: cramped
her muscles knot and lock, and she gasps at the pain
the cramp seizes her limb and pulls it in tight
she tries to stretch it out and the cramp only bites harder
she hisses through her teeth as the muscle locks
she stands rigid, the cramp holding her in its grip
she shakes the limb hard, and it will not loosen
the cramp makes her limp and wince with every move
her body curls round the cramping muscle, trembling

== status_tell | status: paralyzed
sparks crackle over her and her body seizes up
she tries to move and her limbs only jerk
her muscles lock and twitch, and she cannot make them stop
she stands frozen, quivering, sparks dancing on her
her jaw clenches and will not open, a buzzing hum in every muscle
she jerks once, twice, and then stays locked
every nerve in her buzzes, and nothing obeys
she twitches all over, her body not her own

== status_tell | status: confused
she turns the wrong way and stops, puzzled
her eyes swim and she sways, unsure where she is
she lunges at nothing and catches herself, blinking
she shakes her head and looks round, lost
she cannot tell which way her opponent is
she turns her head one way, then the other, swaying on the spot as she tries to get her bearings
her eyes keep sliding off things, never quite landing
she blinks, slow and stupid, as if the world had gone strange

== status_tell | status: constricted
the coils tighten and her breath comes out in a squeak
she cannot move inside the loops, only her head is free
she gasps for air and the coils take it away
she strains against the coils and they press harder
her face goes tight and her eyes squeeze shut
she can feel her heartbeat in her ears as the coils squeeze
her limbs go heavy and slow inside the loops
every breath is shorter than the last, and she can feel the next one shrinking

== status_tell | status: staggered
she reels back, her feet scrambling to keep up with her body
the blow knocks her off her line and she stumbles to catch herself
she lurches sideways, two quick unplanned steps, and plants her feet wide
she staggers, arms out, and only just keeps her balance
her feet tangle and she nearly goes down, catching herself at the last
she stumbles back, shaking her head, trying to get her feet under her
the force carries her a step and then another, and she fights to stop
she staggers off her stance and has to find it again, quickly

# self_look (she looks herself over): by zone, and in general
== self_look
she looks down at the {part} and sees it trembling, though she is holding it still
she glances at the {part} and sees the swelling, already rising
she sees a thin scratch on the {part}, beaded with blood
she looks at the {part} and finds it held at a strange angle
she watches the {part} twitch, and cannot stop it
she lifts the {part} a little and it shakes the whole way
she sees the fur on the {part} matted and flattened
she looks at the {part} and sees it drawn up close to her body
she looks at the bruise darkening on the {part} and winces
she sees the {part} has gone a little stiff, a little slow
she glances at the {part} and quickly looks away
she sees dirt ground into the {part} from the blow
she tries to move the {part} and watches it lag behind

== self_look | state: tired, spent
she looks at herself and sees how badly she is shaking
she glances down and sees she is leaning to one side
she looks at her own body and sees every bruise
she sees her breath coming in short shaky pulls
she looks down at herself and the whole world tilts with the movement

== self_look | zone: head
she squeezes her eyes shut, opens them, and the world is still swimming
she can feel the swelling on her head throbbing with every heartbeat
she blinks and blinks and cannot clear the blur
she feels her face tighten round the hurt
her head throbs, and she can feel the beat of it behind her eyes
she tries to focus and her eyes keep sliding off

== self_look | zone: neck
she turns her head and the pain in her neck stops her short
she can feel her neck stiffening, the muscles locked
she tries to lift her head and it will not go all the way
her neck throbs every time she swallows, and she tries not to swallow
she holds her head very still, afraid to turn it

== self_look | zone: back
she cannot see her back, but she can feel the hurt there spreading
she feels her back tighten and lock round the hurt
she tries to straighten and the pain in her back stops her
she can feel every breath pulling at the hurt on her back
her back throbs with a hot, dull ache she cannot reach

== self_look | zone: chest
she looks down and sees her chest heaving, too fast
she sees her ribs move with each breath, and each breath hurts
she sees the bruise on her chest darkening
she looks at her chest and sees it trembling with each heartbeat

== self_look | zone: belly
she looks down and sees her belly drawn in tight
she sees the bruise on her belly spreading
she looks at her belly and sees it flinch with each breath
she sees her middle curled in on itself, guarding

== self_look | zone: arm
she looks at her limb and sees it shaking
she tries to lift her limb and watches it sag
she sees her limb held tight against her body
she looks at the hurt limb and sees it swelling

== self_look | zone: leg
she looks at her leg and sees it trembling under her weight

she watches her leg shake as she puts weight on it
she sees the bruise on her leg, dark and swollen

== self_look | zone: tail
she looks back and sees her tail hanging limp | has: tail

she looks at her tail and sees it held stiff and low | has: tail

# raw (a reaction too big to hold in)
== raw | raw: contained
she holds the sound in, and her {part} trembles with the effort
her eyes squeeze shut and her jaw clenches, and she makes no sound at all
she sucks a breath through her teeth and holds it, the {part} quivering
she goes very still, and only her shaking {part} gives her away
a small strangled sound escapes her, quickly swallowed
she grips the {part} and her face goes white, but she does not cry out
her breath shudders in and out, and she does not let it become a sound
she bites down on her lip until the urge to cry out passes
the {part} jerks, and she forces it still
she breathes through her nose, fast and hard, eyes watering
her whole body tenses round the {part}, silent
she lets out one hiss and then nothing more

== raw | raw: cry
she cries out, sharp and short, and clutches the {part}
a yelp bursts out of her and her {part} jerks back
she gasps and cries out, and her eyes water
she whimpers, a small high sound, then bites it off with her eyes squeezed shut
a strangled cry escapes her and she curls round the {part}
she cries out and then pants hard, shaking
she yelps and pulls the {part} in tight
the cry is out before she can stop it, high and broken
she gasps out a cry and her whole body flinches
she cries out, then clamps her mouth shut
a sharp sound breaks from her and she trembles after it
she cries out, and her {part} quivers on its own long after the cry is done

== raw | raw: scream
she screams, and the sound rips out of her
a scream tears from her throat and she curls round the {part}
she screams until she has no breath left
the scream bends her over, and she shakes after it
she screams, and her eyes fill with tears
a scream bursts out of her and her {part} jerks away
she screams and then gasps, and then screams again
the scream is long and ragged, and it hurts her throat
she screams, and her whole body goes rigid
she screams and clutches the {part}, rocking over it on the spot
she screams, high and raw, and it echoes off everything
the scream leaves her trembling and gasping, eyes wet, the {part} held close

== raw | raw: raw
she cannot stop the sound; it comes out of her in broken gasps and sobs
she shakes uncontrollably, her {part} clutched against her
she wails, a raw broken sound, and cannot stop
her eyes stream and she cannot catch her breath
she curls tight round the {part} and sobs
every breath is a moan, and she cannot make it stop
she shakes so hard she can barely stay upright
she whimpers and gasps in turns, her {part} trembling against her
the pain has taken her voice and made it into something raw
she sobs and shakes and clutches the {part}
she gasps for air between broken cries, and each gasp sets off the next cry
she trembles all over, and the sounds keep coming

# pin_strain (what holding the pin costs the one on top)
== pin_strain | held: early
she settles her weight and feels her own heart thudding hard against the body under her
the first heave from below jolts through her and she grits her teeth to absorb it
she has to spread herself wider than is comfortable, and her joints let her know it
her breath is still quick from the takedown, and she works to slow it
the hold is fresh and strong, but her muscles are already humming with it
the first try from below nearly unseats her, and she has to clamp down hard
her sore places wake up as she presses down, one after another | has: hurts
she winces as she braces, a bruise of her own pressed against the other | has: hurts
a quick sting from her own hurt makes her shift, and she has to shift back fast | has: hurts
she locks into place and feels the strain start to gather in her shoulders
she feels every twitch below her, and every one makes her tighten
her own pulse is loud in her ears as she holds on
the effort makes her breath hiss through her teeth
she presses down and feels her muscles bunch and tremble, just a little

== pin_strain | held: middle
her shoulders burn with holding, a deep, slow fire
she is panting now, as hard as the one under her
the tremble in her limbs has started and will not stop
she shifts her weight to rest one limb and feels the hold go loose, and shifts it back
her own hurts throb with every heave from below | has: hurts
her worst bruise has started to pound in time with her heart | has: hurts
she bites her lip against her own pain and keeps pressing | has: hurts
sweat runs into her eyes and she has to blink it away
the strain has moved into her neck and back, and settled there
she grunts with every try from below, her whole body jolting
her muscles ache from the long stillness, worse than any movement
she can feel her strength draining slowly into the hold
she squeezes her eyes shut for a moment, and opens them again fast

== pin_strain | held: long
she is trembling all over now, the shake running into the hold itself
her breath comes in ragged sobs, as spent as the one beneath her
her muscles scream for rest, and she keeps them locked anyway
she is holding on by stubbornness alone, eyes squeezed shut, limbs quaking
her own hurts have become a single great ache that never lets up | has: hurts
she can barely feel her own wounds any more, only the burning in her limbs | has: hurts
her own injured part gives way for a moment and she catches herself, gasping | has: hurts
her eyes are drifting half shut and she has to force them open
she sways on top of the other, and only just keeps the hold
her limbs are shaking so hard the other must feel it
she whimpers under her breath with the effort, and does not let go
her body is one long knot of strain, and she cannot tell where it hurts most
she is so tired she could lie down on top of the other and sleep, and she does not
her grip has gone numb, and she holds on by feel alone
every breath she takes shakes, and still she presses down

# pin_hold (the one on top, keeping the other down)
== pin_hold
she keeps her weight centred and low, giving nothing away
she watches the other's face for the next try
she presses down a little harder each time the other moves
she shifts her grip, quick and careful, and holds again
she keeps her breathing slow and steady, hiding her own strain
she feels the next heave building and is ready for it
she leans all her weight into the hold and keeps it there
she follows each wriggle with her weight, never letting it gain ground
she keeps her face still and calm, though her limbs are burning
she holds the other's limbs pinned flat and does not let them move
she presses the other's shoulders down and keeps them there
she listens to the other's breathing and times her pressure to it
she braces against each heave and lets it spend itself
she keeps her head low and out of reach
she does not look away, not once, though her own eyes are watering
she lets the other struggle and simply holds
she adjusts her weight a hair at a time, always one step ahead
she feels the other tiring and presses her advantage
she leans in close and holds still, a heavy, patient weight
she keeps one limb free, ready to follow any move
she holds so still she seems carved there
she grips harder whenever the other gasps for breath
she feels the other's heartbeat through the hold, fast and frightened
she keeps the hold firm without crushing, saving herself
she lets the other's struggles wash over her like waves
she keeps her weight exactly where it does the most good
she shifts to block every gap the other tries for
she plants herself and becomes immovable, a weight the other cannot shift
she keeps the other flat with steady, even pressure
she breathes through her own strain and does not ease off
she presses her chest down and keeps it there
she never lets the hold settle into a rhythm the other could use
she presses down through her legs and hips | has: legs
she pins the other's limbs with her arms, elbows locked | has: arms
she wraps her coils tight and lays her weight along them | has: coils
she spreads her wings to steady herself and keeps her body pressed down | has: wings
she grips with her talons and leans in | has: talons
she keeps her own hurt side clear of the struggling limbs | has: hurts
she ignores her own aches and keeps her weight where it belongs | has: hurts
she holds the other's gaze, unblinking | facing: up
she lowers her face close, and the other has to look at her | facing: up
she keeps her face out of reach of the other's teeth | facing: up
she presses down and watches the other's eyes go hard with frustration | facing: up
she holds firm, though her breathing is hard now | state: tired, spent
she keeps the hold, though it costs her more each moment | state: tired, spent
she holds easily, still fresh, the effort hardly showing | state: fresh, winded
she keeps her weight on the other without a sign of strain | state: fresh
she keeps the hold with her eyes half closed, too tired to do anything but endure | state: spent
